veḷiyīṭu
tamiḻmaṇ patippakam
2, ciṅkārāvēlar teru
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi -600 017
to.pē: 2433 9030

moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vitti ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. ati muḷikkac ceytavar paritimāl kaliñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟimaliyām maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkiḷi nili nāṭṭiya maliyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavi eḻutiyavi aṉittum meymmiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭit tam āyvu matukiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuriyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uriyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavi alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkki añciṟit tumpiyāka avaṟṟi yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉi akara niral tokiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kāliyil, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum tamiḻ maṇṇiyum tamiḻ iṉattiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uriyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉi vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkarutti yāṉ urikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷi amittār.
tēvanēyar paṭippukaḷ aṉittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷit tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷiyum kaṭṭurikaḷiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉivar kikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya niliyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpati nilip paṭuttum vakiyil avarkkup paṭiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumiyarum toṇṭarumāvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!	iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


patippuri
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻi mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatit tam paṉmoḻip pulamiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ aṉittiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatit tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷi yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta viraccuraṅkam. ittokuppukaḷi veḷiyiṭu vatil perumip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlatti vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

cul (tuḷittaṟ karuttuvēr)
cul : cūl - cūl. cūlutal = 1. tōṇṭutal.
nuṅku cūṉṟiṭṭaṉṉa kaṇṇīrmi (nālaṭi. 44). 2. aṟuttal (yāḻp.).
aṟuttalum oruvakit tuḷittalē. kattiyāl nīṭṭup pōkkākap paṉmuṟi muṉṉum piṉṉum iḻuttut tuḷittal aṟuttalum, ceṅkuttāka orē aṟiyil tuḷittut tuṇit tal allatu ciṟitu tuḷittal veṭṭutalum, ākum.
cul - (cuṉ) - cuṉi = 1. uṭṭuḷiyiṉiṉṟu nīr varuvatu pōṉṟa nīrūṟṟu. 2. nīrūṟṟuḷḷa malik kuṇṭu.
vāṉka ṇaṟṟavaṉ maliyē vāṉattu
mīṉka ṇaṟṟataṉ cuṉiyē  (puṟam. 109)
3. nīrūṟṟuḷḷa poyki (piṅ.).
cuṉi - te. coṉa, ka. toṉa. cuṉ - cūṉ = iru vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷiṉ iṭiccantu.
cuṉi - (coṉi) - cōṉi = 1. vāṉattiṉiṉṟu nīrūṟṟuc corivatu pōṉṟa viṭāp perum peyal. mēkañ cōṉi paṭa (kamparā. ayōtti. kukap. 20). 2. (cōṉiyākap poḻiyum) kār mukil. cōṉivār kuḻaliṉār (kamparā. pāla. nīrviḷi. 14). 3. maliyaṭivāra viṭāmaḻic cāral.
te. cōṉa, ka. cōṉe.
cōṉimāri - cōṉāmāri - cōṉāvāri = viṭāp peru maḻi. cōṉāmāriyāyc corikiṟatē eṉpatu ulakavaḻakku. cōṉi māriyiṟ corintaṉaṉ (kamparā. yutta. piramāt. 59).
cōṉi - cōṉam = mukil. cōṉantaru kuḻalār (patiṉo. āḷu. mum. 12). te. cōṉa, ka. cōṉe.
cōṉi mēkam = perumaḻi mukil. corintatu cōṉi mēkam (pārata. campava. 79).
cōṉimēkam - cōṉāmēkam. cōṉāmēkam poḻivatu pōl (pārata. patiṉēḻām. 135).
cul - (cur) - cura. curattal = 1. uṭṭuḷiyiṉiṉṟu ūṟutal pōṟ pāl curattal. 2. pāl curattal pōl maḻi poḻital.
māmaḻi pōṟṟutum māmaḻi pōṟṟutum
nāmanīr vēli yulakiṟ kavaṉaḷipōl
mēṉiṉṟu tāṉcuratta lāṉ  (cilap. 1:9)
3. maḻi poḻital pōl iṟivaṉ aruḷpoḻital. āḻiyā ṉavaṉi nōkki yaruḷcurantu (kamparā. yutta vipīṭaṇa. 142).
4. mikak koṭuttal. neṭuntēr kaḷiṟoṭu curakkum (akam. 249). 5. (mikak koṭuttalāl) niṟital. viruppañ curanta cilanti muṭicūṭṭum perumāṉ (tiruvāṉi. ulā 60). 6. uṭampil nōy nīr peruki vīṅkutal.
cura - ma. curattu, ka. ōcar.
cura - curappu = 1. ūṟuki. curappuṟu ciṟip puṉal (aric. pu. vivāka. 107). 2. ūṟṟu. 3. kaṟakkumāṟu āviṉ maṭiyiṟ pālūṟuki, oru curappu ākiviṭṭatu (u.va.). 4. keṭṭa nīrāl ēṟpaṭṭa vīkkam. kāliṟ curappu uṇṭāyirukkiṟatu (u.va.).
curappu - curappi = naṉṟāka allatu nirampap pāl curakkum ā(vu).
o.nō : kaṟavi = kaṟappu, kaṟakkum āvu. curi = curappu. curakkum āvu.
curappi - va. curapi (Surabhi). vaṭamoḻiyil curapi eṉṉuñ coṟku mūlamilli. cu - rap Su-rabh = Pleasantly affecting eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akara mutali uṉṉippākak kūṟum.
cura - curam = uṭṭuḷi.
cura - curaṅku - curaṅkam = 1. nilavaṟi. 2. kīḻaṟukkum aṟi. 3. tiruṭar kaṉṉamiṭac cuvaril tuḷikkum tuḷi. 4. pāṟikaḷi uṭikka veṭimaruntu vikkum kuḻi.
curaṅkam - curaṅkā. (va.).
cur - curi. curittal = tuḷiyiṭutal.
curi = 1. tuḷi. (tivā.). 2. ēṭṭuttuḷi. ma. curi. 3. ēṭṭil tuḷiyiṭuṅ karuvi.
curiyūci = ēṭṭil tuḷiyiṭuṅ karuvi.
iccol kuttaṟ poruṭ kaṭṭuriyilum kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. kuttalum tuḷittalum, kalatti nīrmēl vittalum ataṟkuḷ muḻukkutalum pōṉṟa, neruṅkiya allatu aṭuttu nikaḻum viṉikaḷ eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. ataṉāl, orē col iraṇṭiyuṅ kuṟikkum. āyiṉum, karuvi vēṟupāṭṭāl 
viṉi vēṟupaṭum. kiyiṉāl atil allatu vēṟuṟuppiṟ kuttuvatu tuḷittaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭatu. ātalāl, tuḷikkuṅ kuttal viṉic coṟkaḷellām ikkaṭṭurikkē yuriyavi yeṉap pirittuṇarka. kuṭiccaṟ karuttik koṇṭa cūli nōyp peyarum, aṇivakit tuḷittalik kuṟittalāl ik kaṭṭurikkē yuriyatākum.
cur - curu - curuṅku = cāykaṭi (Gutter) (piṅ.).
curuṅku - va. curuṅkā.
curuṅku - curuṅki = 1. nuḻivāyil. (piṅ.). 2. māḷikiyiṉ cāḷaram. māṭamēṟ curuṅkiyiliruntu ..... mānakaraṇi pārttiṭum (cīkāḷat pu. nakkīra. 30). 3. puticālakam. peruṅkuḷa maruṅkiṟ curuṅkic ciṟuvaḻi (maṇi. 12:79). 4. kōṭṭiyil maṟivāṉa kīḻnila vaḻi. (cūṭā). pōka vamitta poynnilac curuṅkiyuḷ (peruṅ. 2:17:94).
curuṅki - va. curuṅkā.
o. nō : Gk. Surigx, Surigg = kuḻāy. L., E. Sgrinx = nāṇaṟkuḻal. L. Siringa, E. Syringe = uṟiñci (uṟiñcu kuḻāy).
curu - curuvam = 1. akappi vaki (aka. ni.). 2. neyt tuṭuppu. cīric curukkuc curuvamelām (uttararā. aṉumap. 41).
curuvam - va. ruva.
curu - curuvi = neyt tuṭuppu. antaṇāḷarkkuc curuviyum (tol. po. 629, uri).
o.nō : muḷ - muḻu - muḻuku. muḻu - muḻi = akappi. muḻukutalum tuḷittal vakiyē.
cura - curi = 1. kuḻinta iṭam. pāttirat takaṉ curip peyta vāruyir maruntu (maṇi. 11:117). 2. uṭṭuḷi. (piṅ.). 3. mūṅkiṟ kuḻāy. akaṉcū lañcurip peyta valciyar (akam. 113). 4. tirikkuḻāy. coricuri kavaru ney (patiṟṟup 47). 5. tirukāṇiyic celuttuñ ciṟu kuḻāy. 6. mūṭṭuvāy. curiyampu mūḻka. (kalit. 6). 7. pūṇ. ceṟicuri veḷvēl (akam. 216). 8. tōṇṭum kūrāṉa pārivaki. uḷivāyc curiyiṉ miḷira maṇṭi (perumpāṇ. 92). 9. neṟṟāṉapiṉ uṭṭuḷiyuḷḷa kāyvaki curivittup pōluntam pal (nālaṭi. 315). ma. cura, ka. core. 10. nuṇṇiya tuḷiyiṉiṉṟu curakki. kaṭuñcuri nallāṉ (kuṟun. 132). ka. core. 11. māṉmari maṟi āṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pāṉmaṭi. varuṭi māṉmaṟi curipoḻi tīmpāl (kuṟun. 187). 12. curakkum ā. (tivā.). curimali yamirtat tīmpāl (cūḷā. tūtu. 90).
cuḷ - cuḷi - (cuki) - cukir. cukirtal = 1. kiḻittal. palliṉāṟ cukirnta nāriṉ (cīvaka. 438). 2. mayir, nār mutaliyavaṟṟi vakirtal. cukir = uṭṭuḷi. (cūṭā.).
cur - (cūr) - cūṟu = eruvāy (Anus). cūṟu - cūṟṟu - cūttu = 1. eruvāy (malavāyil). 2. puṭṭam. cūttilaṭittāṉām, pallup 
pōccām (paḻamoḻi).
ma. cūttu. va. cuttu (c), cūttu (c), cuttī (c). vaṭamoḻiyil itaṟku mūlamilli, tamiḻil mūlam teḷivākavuḷḷatu. cūṟal (cūlutal) = tōṇṭutal. cūṟu = tuḷi, eruvāyil. iṅṅaṉamiruntum, 
cūttu eṉṉum teṉ coṟkuc cuttī eṉṉum vaṭacolli mūlameṉṟu talikīḻākac ceṉṉip pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutali (Lexicon) kāṭṭiyiruppatu ettuṇik kuṟumpuc ceytiyām!
cūttaḻaki = kuraṅkuc cūttu māmpaḻam.
cūttāṅ karaṭu = eruvāyiṉ taṭippu.
cūttāṭṭi = valiyāṉ eṉṉum oruvakik kuruvi.
cūttāmpaṭṭi = puṭṭam.
cūttericcal = eruvāy kāntutal.
iṭakkar viṉic coṟkaḷum vaḻakkukkaḷum iṅku viṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaḻakkukkaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum vaṭamoḻiyil illi.

cū viḷiyāṭṭu
(1)  pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : pāṇṭi nāṭṭil itu iṉṟu avuṭṭu (out) eṉṉum āṅkilappeyarāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ōṭit toṭum oruvaṉ, uṭkārntirukkum oruvaṉi avuṭṭu eṉṟu colli eḻuppum viḷiyāṭṭu, ac colliyē peyarākak koṇṭatu. itu mutalāvatu ucu viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām.
āṭuvār toki : iti āṭa aivar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : ūrp poṭṭalilum akaṉṟa teruvilum iti āṭalām.
āṭu muṟi : mūvar ivvirukaca iṭiyiṭṭu, orē tici nōkki variciyāka nilattil uṭkārntu koḷvar. oruvaṉ avaric cuṟṟiyum avarukkiṭiyilum oṭuvāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ avaṉit toṭa vēṇṭum. toṭukiṟavaṉ variciyic cuṟṟi yaṉṟi iṭiyiṟ cellakkūṭātu. āyiṉ, iṭiyil varicik kōṭṭit tāṇṭātu eṭṭit toṭalām.
ōṭukiṟavaṉukku mika vacatiyiruttalil avaṉit toṭuvatu 
aritu. toṭa muyalkiṟavaṉ nīṇṭa nēram āṭiyōṭip pārttu viṭṭu, piṉpu ōṭukiṟavaṉukku aṇmiyākat taṉakku muṉṉā liruppavaṉi avuṭṭu eṉṟu colli eḻuppiviṭṭu ava ṉiṭattil tāṉ uṭkārntu koḷvāṉ. uṭkārntiruppavar orē tici nōkki uṭkārntiruppatālum, muṉṉōkki yaṉṟip piṉṉōkki ōṭit toṭutal kūṭāmiyālum, eḻuppukiṟavaṉ eṉṟum eḻuppappaṭukiṟavaṉukkup piṉṉāka niṉṟē eḻuppuvaṉ. eḻuppap paṭṭavaṉ uṭaṉē ōṭip pōy ōṭukiṟavaṉit toṭṭuviṭiṉ, toṭṭavaṉ ōṭukiṟavaṉākavum oṭiṉavaṉ toṭukiṟavaṉākavum māṟal vēṇṭum. ōṭukiṟavaṉ tāṉ eppōtu virumpiṉum, uṭkārntirukkum oruvaṉi eḻuppi viṭṭu, tāṉ avaṉiṭattil uṭkārntu koḷḷalām.
ōṭukiṟavaṉ tāṉ kūṭiyamaṭṭum toṭappaṭātavāṟu toṭukiṟava ṉukkum uṭkārntirukkiṟavaṉukkum nēr muṉṉum aṇmi yilum nillātu mikat taḷḷiyē niṟpaṉ.
iṅṅaṉam vēṇṭumaḷavu toṭarntāṭappeṟum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : itu vēṭṭi viṉiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya tākat teri kiṉṟatu. toṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭikkāraṉum, ōṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭi vilaṅkum, eḻuppappaṭukiṟavaṉ vēṭṭi nāyum pōṉṟavarāvar.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : ōṭum oruvaṉip piṭippatum, oruvaṉukkup piṭikoṭāmal ōṭit tappuvatumākiya viṉippayiṟci, iv vaṭṭāṟ peṟappaṭum.
(2) cōḻanāṭṭu muṟi
āṭṭiṉ peyar : cū viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṉum taṉit tamiḻppeyar vaḻaṅkuvatu cōḻa nāṭṭiltāṉ. cū eṉṟu colli oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉi eḻuppum viḷiyāṭṭu accollāṟ peyar peṟṟatu.
āṭuvār toki : aṟuvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar iti āṭuvar.
āṭu muṟi : āṭuvār camattokiyavāṉa iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu, kaṭcikkoruvaṉāka iruvar niṟka, ēṉiya rellām variciyāy iṭiyiṭṭu nilattil uṭkārntu koḷvar. uṭkārntiruppavaruḷ, ovvoru kaṭciyārum oruvaṉ viṭṭoruvaṉāyiruppar. oru kaṭciyār kiḻakku nōkkiṉ maṟṟoru kaṭciyār mēṟku nōkkiyum, oru kaṭciyār teṟku nōkkiṉ maṟṟoru kaṭciyār vaṭakku nōkkiyum, iruppar.
niṟpavaruḷ, oruvaṉ ōṭa iṉṉoruvaṉ toṭal vēṇṭum. toṭukiṟavaṉ taṉṉāl toṭamuṭiyāteṉṟu kaṇṭāl, taṉ kaṭciyāruḷ oruvaṉic cū eṉṟu colli eḻuppuvāṉ. piṟa ceytikaḷellām pāṇṭi nāṭṭu muṟiyē.
iru kaṭciyākap pirintukoṇṭu etiretirt ticiyi nōkki yiruppatum, cū eṉṟu colluvatumē cōḻanāṭṭu vēṟupāṭām.
cūḷ vakikaḷ
cūḷ teyvañ cāṉṟākak kūṟum uṟutimoḻi; āṇi aracāṇiyiṉ pērāl kūṟum uṟutimoḻi; neṭumoḻi: pōr muṉiyil pakivar muṉ oruvaṉ taṉṉi mikuttuk kūṟum kūṟṟu; vañciṉam nāṉ eṉ pakivaṉukku iṉṉatu ceyyē ṉākil iṉṉanili yaṭiyak kaṭavēṉ eṉṟu kūṟum capatam; pūṭki oru kāriyam muṭiyum vari vēṟoṉṟi vilakki vikkum uṟutippāṭu; mēṟkōḷ ōr oḻukkatti allatu nalviṉiyi mēṟkoḷḷum kaṭippiṭi; poruttaṉi oruvaṉ taṉakkuk kaṭavuḷ ceyyum oru kuṟippiṭṭa naṉmikkup patilākat tāṉ oṉṟik kāṇikkiyākak koṭuppatākac ceytu koḷḷum vāyc col oppantam; nērttik kaṭaṉ kimāṟu karutiyum karutāmalum kaṭavuḷukku allatu teyvat tiṟku oru poruḷi otukki vittal. kaṅku allatu kaṅkaṇam oruvaṉ taṉ pakivaṉiṭattil paḻikkup paḻivāṅka vēṇṭu meṉṟu ataṟkaṭiyāḷamākak kaṭṭik koḷḷum kāppu; oṭṭu oruvaṉ tāṉ virumpātatoṉṟit taṉ etiri ceyyiṉ, avaṉ aḻintu viṭuvāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum āṇi.  (col. 59).
ceṅkuntar
ceṅkuntar, pāṇar eṉpavar tamiḻnāṭṭut tamiḻarē. āyiṉum, toḻil māṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
ceṅkuntam piṭittuc cōḻarukkup paṭimaṟavarāyiruntavar ceṅkuntar. pāṇ allatu icit toḻilik koṇṭirun tavar pāṇar. ivviru vakuppārum iṉṟu muṟiyē necaviyum, tiyaliyum mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
maliyāḷa nāṭṭup pāṇar kūṭiyum tāḻaṅkuṭiyum muṭivatit toḻilākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.  (col. 27,28).
ceṅkōl
nērmiyāṉa āṭcimuṟi nērāṉa kōlpōl iruttalāl ceṅkōl eṉappaṭṭatu. kuṭikaḷ ākkaḷ pōlavum, aracaṉ āyaṉ pōlavum iruttalāl aracaṉ kōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. kō = ā (pacu) kōvaṉ = āyaṉ.  (ti.ma. 310).
ceṭṭi
ceṭṭi - crēṣṭiṉ
eḻutal = uyartal. eṇṇutal = mēṉmēl aḷaviṭutal.
eṭṭutal = uyartal, uyarntu toṭutal, toṭumaḷavu neruṅkutal.
eṭṭi nōkkutal = aṇṇāntu pārttal  (peruṅ. naravāṇa. 8:82).
eṭṭam = uyaram, tolivu.
eṭuttal = uyarttutal, tūkkutal.
eṭṭu - eṭṭi = 1. uyarntavaṉ, mēlōṉ. 2. paṇṭi yaracar 
vaṇika mēlōṉukku aḷitta paṭṭam.
eṭṭi kumara ṉiruntōṉ ṟaṉṉi  (maṇi. 4:58).
3. vaṇikaṉ (tivā.).
eṭṭippū = eṭṭippaṭṭam peṟṟavaṉukku aracar koṭukkum poṟpū. eṭṭip pūp peṟṟu (maṇi. 22:113).
eṭṭippuravu = eṭṭip paṭṭam peṟṟavaṉukku aracaṉ koṭutta. nilam  (naṉ. 158, mayili. uri).
eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. o. nō : iḷi - ciḷi, uti - cuti, ēṇ - cēṇ. ceṭṭimi = vaṇikam, ceṭṭu.
ceṭṭi - ceṭṭu = ceṭṭittaṉam, cikkaṉam.
ceṭṭi - cēṭṭi - cēṭṭu = vaṭanāṭṭu vaṇikaṉ.
vaṭamoḻiyār kāṭṭum mūvēṟu mūlam varumāṟu :
(1) crīmat (tirumāṉ) eṉpataṉ uccattaram (sup. deg.).
(2) pracaya (pukaḻpaṭat takkavaṉ) eṉpataṉ uccattaram.
(3) crī (tiru) eṉpataṉ uccattaram.
teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭacollākkum vaḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟu mutaleḻuttiṉ piṉ rakaram iṭiccerukal.
e-ṭu: tamiḻam - tramiḷa, kamukam - kramuka, tiṭam - truṭa.
naṭṭam - nrutta, paṭi - prati, metu - mrutu. viṭi - vruṣa.
immuṟiyil ceṭṭi eṉpati (vaṭamoḻiyil ekaram iṉmiyāl) crēṭṭi eṉat tirittu, ataṟkēṟpap poruntap poyttal eṉṉum utti paṟṟi vevvēṟu mūlaṅ kāṭṭu vārāyiṉar.
ceṭṭi eṉpatu, tamiḻil vaṇikaṉi maṭṭuṅ kuṟikkum eṉṟum, crēṣṭiṉ eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟantōṉ evaṉiyuṅ kuṟikkum eṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟika.  (va.va. 164-165).
ceṭi
ceṭi - jaṭi (gh, t|) = ceṭi.
ceḷ - ceḻi - ceṭi. ceṭittal = aṭartal. ceṭi = aṭartti.
ceṭikoḷ vāṉpoḻil cūḻ  (tiruvāca. 29:5).
ceṭi = ilikiḷi yaṭarnta ciṟu nilittiṇi vaki.
ma. ceṭi, te. ceṭṭu, ka. kiṭa (g). te. ceṭṭu - i. jāṭ (d|)= maram.  (va.va. 163).
centamiḻnāṭu 
centamiḻ nilatti vikiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkumām eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvariyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliyōr urittaṉar.
centamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta pakuti iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭēyā yiruttaliṉ, ivvuri poruntātu.
centamiḻ nāṭāvatu : viyiyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum, marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum, karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum, maruvūriṉ mēṟkum eṉpa. ivvāṟu urittaṟku ōr ilakkaṇaṅ kāṇāmiyāṉum, viyi yāṟṟiṉ teṟkākiya koṟkiyum, karuvūriṉ mēṟkākiya koṭuṅkōḷū rum, marutayāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkākiya kāñciyum tamiḻtiri nilamātal vēṇṭu mātalāṉum, aḵtu uriyaṉṟu eṉpār urikkumāṟu:
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ
eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭi
eṉṟamiyāṉum, itaṉuḷ tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakameṉa vicēṭit tamiyāṉum, kiḻakkum mēṟkum elli kūṟātu teṟkelli kūṟiyavataṉāṟ kumariyiṉ teṟkākiya nāṭukaḷiyoḻittu, vēṅkaṭa maliyiṉ teṟkum, kumariyiṉ vaṭakkum, kuṇakaṭaliṉ mēṟkum, kuṭakaṭaliṉ kiḻakkumākiya nilam centamiḻ nilameṉ ṟurippa eṉṟurittār teyvac ciliyār.
iv vuriyē ciṟantatākum. centamiḻ nilam,
cantaṉap potiyac centamiḻ muṉiyuñ
cavuntara pāṇṭiya ṉeṉuntamiḻ nāṭaṉuñ
caṅkap pulavarum taḻittiṉi tirukkum
maṅkalap pāṇṭi vaḷanā ṭeṉpa
eṉpatu piṟkālattiṟ kēṟṟatākum.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi  (tol. ecca. 4)
eṉpatāl, centamiḻnāṭ ṭelli tāṇṭiya paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷ koṭuntamiḻnāṭu eṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ vitappāy vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ ticiccoṟkaḷ eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
centamiḻ varampīṭu
ulakam muḻuvatum nōkkiṉ. moḻiyamippu. moḻi vaḷarppu. moḻikkāppu ākiya mūvaki moḻiviṉiyilum taliciṟanta var kumarinilat tamiḻarē eṉpatu. teḷivākiṉṟatu.
vāyccōmpalālō, taṭpa veppanili paṟṟiya nila vēṟu pāṭṭālō, iraṇṭiṉālumō, moḻi potumakkaḷ vāyil tirivatiyum. attiripiṟku elliyillāmiyiyum. kaṇṭa tamiḻaṟiñar. iyaṉilit tamiḻukkuc centamiḻ eṉṟum. tirinilit tamiḻukkuk koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum peyariṭṭu. centamiḻiyē ulakuḷḷa aḷavum niliyāṉa aḷavi moḻiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
naṭimoḻi (dialect) yuṇmi paṇṭit tamiḻarukkut teriyā teṉṟum, vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl (descriptive linguistics) tōṉṟiya piṉpē naṭimoḻiyārāycci ulakeṅkum paraviya teṉṟum, mēliyarum avaric cārntavarkaḷum karutik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻar tam moḻiyip piṟamoḻi kaḷuṭaṉ maṭṭumaṉṟi, tam conta moḻik kuṭumpattic cērnta piṟamoḻikaḷuṭaṉkūṭa, oppunōkka orukālum muyaṉṟa 
tilli. moḻik kuṭumpam eṉa oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṉum karutti, avarkaḷ eṉṟēṉum uṭkoṇṭatilli, eṉṟu kālṭu velārum eḻutiviṭṭār. āyiṉ, avar kālattil tol kāppiyap payiṟciyiṉmiyāl, avar kūṟṟu maṉṉikkat takkatē.
tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyāyiruttal pōṉṟu, moḻiyārāyc ciyum mutaṉmutal am moḻiyilēyē tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉpatu ki.mu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭileḻunta tolkāppiyam eṉṉum cārpiṟ cārpunūlāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tolkāppiyam vaṭacol lum āriya ilakkiyamum āriyar marapum tavira maṟṟellā vaṟṟiyum kumari nāṭṭu nilimi taḻuviyē kūṟuvatāl, ak kālattil (aḵtāvatu āriyar varumuṉ) centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum pākupāṭu tavira, iṟṟit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ kūṭiyatu pōṉṟa oru moḻikkuṭumpam tōṉṟavilli eṉpati yaṟital vēṇṭum.
mēlum, āriyar vantapiṉ tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyum moḻi yārāycciyum taṭukkappaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, kālṭuvelār kuṟikūṟal valiyaṟṟatākiṉṟatu.

centamiḻ iyalpu.
1. tūymi allatu piṟamoḻic colliṉmi.
2. tiruttam allatu eḻuttum collum olippilum 
eḻuttilum uriya vaṭiviliruttal.
3. ilakkaṇam allatu coṟṟoṭaramippil vaḻuviṉmi.
im mūviyalpum illatu koṭuntamiḻ.
teṟkē mūḻkippōṉa paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭiṉ teṉkōṭiyil irunta kumarimalit toṭariṉ teṉ koṭumuṭi, paṉimali yaḷavu toli viliruntataṉālum, iṟantupaṭṭa mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkiṉa vātalālum, akkālattut teṉ col vaḷam, kuṟinta pakkam iṉṟuḷḷatupōl irumaṭaṅkiṉa tāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṇika vāyilāka vanta veḷi nāṭṭup poruḷkaṭkellām, uṭaṉuṭaṉ tamiḻp peyarkaḷ puṉiyap paṭṭaṉa.

centamiḻ nilam
centamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya nilam perumpālum pāṇṭināṭākavē yiruntatu. tamiḻ tōṉṟi vaḷarnta nilamum mukkaḻakamum irunta nāṭum pāṇṭi nāṭēyeṉṟum, avaṟṟip purantavar pāṇṭiyarē yeṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum.
viyi yāṟṟiṉ vaṭakkum marutayāṟṟiṉ teṟkum karuvūriṉ kiḻakkum maruvūriṉ mēṟkum eṉpatu. piṟkālattup poruḷil puṉinturiyām.
centamiḻc colvaki
centamiḻc coṟkaḷ iyaṟcol (Primitive). tiricol (Derivative) eṉa iru vakippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. e-ṭu: veḷ eṉpatu iyaṟcol: veḷḷam, veḷḷi, veḷḷi, veḷi, veḷil, veḷiṟu, veḷu eṉpaṉa tiricol.

koṭuntamiḻ nilamum ticiccollum
koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷil taṉicciṟappāka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ (ikkālattu mēṉārikkam eṉṉum teluṅkac collum camāḷi eṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum pōlvaṉa). ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭup pala ticiyiṉiṉṟum vantamipaṟṟit ticic col (provincialism) eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticitcoṟ kiḷavi  (tol. ec. 4)
tolkāppiyar kālattup paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilam eviyeṉpatu tiṭṭamākat teriyavilli.
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭam kuṭamkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
paṉṟi yaruvā ataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
cītam malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
ciṅkaḷam cōṉakam cāvakam cīṉam tuḷukkuṭakam
koṅkaṇam kaṉṉaṭam kollam teluṅkam kaliṅkamvaṅkam
kaṅkam makatam kaṭāram kavuṭam kaṭuṅkucalam
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuli tāmiviyē.
eṉṉum piṟkālattup pāṭalkaḷum, avaṟṟit taḻuviya
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamiḻoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticicco leṉpa  (naṉ. 273)
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvum, koḷḷattakkaṉa valla. avaṟṟāl aṟiyak kiṭappatellām, oru kālattil ilaṅkiyuṭpaṭa intiyā muḻutum tamiḻē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatē.
centamiḻ varampīṭṭiṉ naṉmi

1. moḻikeṭāmi
kumarināṭṭu moḻiyākiya tamiḻ oṉṟē, iṉṟu palvēṟu vakiyil tirintu patiṉeṇ moḻiyākap pirintuḷḷatu.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ	  maliyāḷam	teluṅku	kaṉṉaṭam	tuḷu
yāṉ, nāṉ		ñāṉ	nēṉu	nāṉu	yāṉu
oṉṟu		oṉṉu	oṇṭu, okaṭṭi	ontu	oñci
vā		vā	rā	pā	pā	
maruntu		maruṉṉu	mantu		martu		martu
iṅṅaṉam coṟkaḷellām tiriyātiruntiruppiṉ, tiraviṭa moḻi kaḷellām iṉṟum tamiḻākavē vaḻaṅkum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru tiraviṭamoḻi terittāḷvatum, tamiḻiṉ tiraviṭat tāymiyik kāṭṭum.
e-ṭu:
maliyāḷam 	teluṅku 	kaṉṉaṭam
vīṭu 	illu(il) 	maṉe(maṉi)
tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ pēccu vaḻakkil tirintiruppiṉum. ilakkiya vaḻakkilēṉum tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyiruntiruppiṉ iṉṟuḷḷa vēṟupāṭum māṟupāṭum nērntirā. iṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pēccu vaḻakku iṭattiṟ kēṟpa vevvēṟu vakiyil tirintirup piṉum, ilakkiya vaḻakkiṟ centamiḻik kaṭippiṭippatāl, nāṭumuḻutum tamiḻnāṭeṉṟē vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.

2. nilaṅkuṉṟāmi
centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻākat tirintu piṉṉarp palvēṟu moḻikaḷākap pirintu pōṉataṉāl, oru kālattiṟ paṉimali vari paraviyirunta tamiḻnilam paṭippaṭiyākac curuṅki , iṉṟu cēranāṭuñ cērātu vaṭacōḻamākiya toṇṭināṭum muṇṭa mākiyuḷḷatu. kaṭikkaḻakak kālattil vēṅkaṭak kōṭṭam vari orumoḻi nāṭāka irunta nilam, iṉṟu tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu, kuṭakam, tuṭavam, kōttam, paṭakam, maliyāḷam, koṅkaṇi eṉṉum orupāṉmoḻi nāṭākap pirintuḷḷatu.
3. iṉavoṟṟumi
moḻi yoṟṟumiyālēyē iṉavoṟṟumi nilittu niṟkum. moḻi vēṟupāṭṭāl iṉa vēṟupāṭum ataṉāl oṟṟumiyiṉmiyum, iṉṟu intiyetirppup pōrāṭṭattil viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. tiraviṭamoḻiyār camaṟkirutattip pōṟṟuvatum tamiḻip puṟakkaṇippatum moḻi vēṟupāṭṭiṉ viḷivē.

4. vērpporuḷ maṟiyāmi
ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē, eṉṟu collak kūṭiya moḻi, ivvulakil tamiḻ oṉṟē. atu centamiḻ varampil niṟpatāl, atu tōṉṟi aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaḷāyiṉum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭiccoṟkaḷum iṇippuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭum, iṉṉum ataṉ perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ kāṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. piṟamoḻika ḷellām tirimoḻikaḷātalāl, avaṟṟiṉ coṟkaṭku vērpporuḷum mūlamum teriyātu. vaṭamoḻi yāḷar vaṭacoṟkaḷi iṭukuṟi, karaṇiyakkuṟi eṉa iruvaki yāyp pirittatōṭu, mēliyarum ellā moḻikaḷum iṭukuṟic coṟṟokutikaḷē eṉṟu muṭivuceytu. avvaṭip paṭiyil vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūliyum tōṟṟi vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar.
col iyaṉiliyil mūlapporuḷ kāṭṭuvatiyum tirinili yiṟ kāṭṭāmiyiyum, kīḻvarum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṟ kāṇka.
iyaṉili 		tirinili
iṭikaḻi 		ṭēkaḻi - ṭēḻi - rēḻi (kocci)
				ṭēkaḻi - tēkalī(va)

5. varalāṟṟut teḷivu
coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ kāṭṭiṉāltāṉ moḻivaralāṟṟi aṟiyamuṭiyum. tamiḻ oṉṟē iyaṉmoḻiyātaliṉ. moḻi varalāṟṟut tiṟavukōl atilēyē āḻap putintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.

8. aḻakuṭimi
kalvikkaḻaku kacaṭaṟa moḻital ōtima (aṉṉa) naṭiyiṉum mātar naṭiyiṉum uyarnta aḻakuḷḷatu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ naṭiyākiya centamiḻ naṭi.
mēliyar, intirōppiya moḻik kuṭumpattiṟku mūlamāṉa tamiḻi aṭippaṭiyākak koḷḷātu āriyattiyē koṇṭārāyntu. kōṭṭic cuvaril muṭṭiya kuruṭarpōl iṭarppaṭṭu. tāymoḻiyum kiḷimoḻiyum eṉṉum muṟimiyiṉṟi. taṉiyāḷ naṭimoḻi (personal dialect), kuḻu naṭimoḻi (group dialect), vakuppu naṭimoḻi (communal or class dialect), toḻil naṭimoḻi (professional dialect), iṭa naṭimoḻi (local dialect), vaṭṭāra naṭimoḻi (regional dialect) eṉa ovvoru perumoḻiyiyum pala naṭimoḻi kaḷākap pakuttu, ārāyntu nūlāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
cilar ulaka vaḻakkiṟkum (colloquial dialect) kocci vaḻakkiṟkum  (slang usage) vēṟupāṭu teriyātu. pēccu moḻiyiyum kavaṉittal vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli varukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻil uyarntōr pēccu vaḻakkē ulaka vaḻakkām.
vaḻakkeṉap paṭuva tuyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci avarkaṭ ṭāka lāṉa  (tol. marapiyal. 93)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. ellā vakiyilum. mēṉmakkaḷ naṭiyiyē kīḻmakkaḷ piṉpaṟṟal vēṇṭum. kīḻmakkaḷ pēcci oppuk koḷḷavēṇṭumeṉiṉ. kīḻmakkaḷ oḻukkattiyum oppukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷiṉ, āṭciyum kāvalum vaḻakkut tīrppum kuṟṟat taṇṭaṉiyum vēṇṭiyatēyilli.
karuttaṟivipput tāṉē moḻiyiṉ payaṉ! ennaṭiyiṟ pēciṉā leṉṉa? eṉpar cilar. atu, paciyip pōkkuvatutāṉē uṇa viṉ payaṉ! eṅṅaṉam (paltulakkātum kuḷiyātum aṭukalattiṟ kuḷ kiyiṭṭum) uṇṭāleṉṉa? eṉṟu viṉivuvatu pōṉ ṟirukkiṉṟatu. āṟaṟivu paṭitta nākarika māntaṉ evviṉi ceyyiṉum, tiruntiya muṟiyilēyē ceytal vēṇṭum.
moḻikkuc cemmi varampiṭātu vāypōṉa pōkkellām pēccut tiriyaviṭiṉ, atu caṇṭik kutiripōl ōriṭattu nillātu kāṭumēṭāy iḻuttuc cellum; iṟutiyiṟ kuḻikkuḷḷum taḷḷum putupputu naṭi tōṉṟip paḻanaṭi vaḻakku vīḻum. paṇṭi ilakkiyam payaṉaṟṟup pōm. āyiram āṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa āṅkila ilakkiyam, iṉṟu ārāycci yāḷarkkē payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻilakkiyamō, iṟṟi yilakkiyampōl ellārkkum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, centamiḻ marapi ettuṇip pāṭupaṭṭum poruṭ celaviṭṭum pōṟṟik kāttal vēṇṭum.
nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ
irutiṇi aimpāl iyalneṟi vaḻāamit
tirivil colloṭu taḻāal vēṇṭum  (tol. marapiyal 90)
marapunili tiriyiṉ piṟitu piṟitākum  (tol. marapiyal 92)
āriyarāl mūvāyiram āṇṭum. ayalaracukaḷāl munnūṟāṇṭum. viyāpurikaḷāl aimpāṉāṇṭum citikkappaṭiṉum, iṉṟum tamiḻi aḻiyātu kāttatu ataṉ cemmi varampē tamiḻ ellā vakiyilum oppuyarvaṟṟa taṉimoḻi. ataṉ uyirnāṭit taṉmi tūymi.  - tāmaric celvar va. cuppiyā piḷḷi pavaḷa viḻā malar 1973.
centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum toṭarkaḷiluḷḷa cemmi koṭumi eṉṉum aṭikaḷ, muṟiyē nērmi, kōṇal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum, ilakkaṇa nērmiyuḷḷa tamiḻ centamiḻ; atu kōṇiya tamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ.
cemmi, koṭumi eṉṉum paṇpukaḷi maṭṭum eṭuttuk koḷḷiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ etu muntiyatu etu pintiyatu eṉac collaviyalātu. ēṉeṉiṉ, iyaṟkiyil nērāṉa poruḷkaḷ ceyaṟkiyil kōṇalākkappaṭuvaṉavum, iyaṟkiyil kōṇalāṉa poruḷkaḷ ceyaṟkiyil nērākkappaṭuvaṉavum ettuṇiyō kaṇkūṭākak kāṇkiṉṟōm.
āyiṉ, centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉuḷ etu iyalpu etu tiripeṉiṉ, centamiḻē iyalpām. koṭuttamiḻē tiripām. itu maṟukkoṇāta vuṇmiyāyiṉum, ārāycci yillātārkku mayaṅkaṟ kiṭaṉām. centamiḻ, koṭuntamiḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iyalkaḷi allatu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi aṟivā rāyiṉ, evarum mayaṅkār.
centamiḻiṉ tiripē koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum uṇmiyi uṇartaṟku. mutalāvatu avviraṇṭum vaḻaṅkum ticiyi nōkkutal vēṇṭum. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapāluḷḷa nāṭukaḷē paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷuḷ, moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟum tamiḻtiri nilam eṉṟum cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum, nāvalan tēyattiṉ teṉkōṭiyilēyē tamiḻ vaḻaṅkavum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkum koṭuntamiḻt tiripāṉa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷē vaḻaṅkavum, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum teṟku nōkkiyē tamiḻ tiruttic cellavum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
koṭuntamiḻiṉ paṇpāṭē centamiḻāyiṉ, centamiḻ nilam muntuniliyil koṭuntamiḻ nilamāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟkoru cāṉṟu millāmiyōṭu, paṇṭorukāl cen tamiḻ nilamāyirunta pakutikaḷ iṉṟu koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷākat tirintuḷḷamiyuṅ kāṇkiṉṟōm. vaḻaṅkum pōtu centamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkāta putuc coṟkaḷ koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkuvatu viyappaṉṟu. piyāṉi (maliyāḷam), alluṇṭu (teluṅku), kokku(tuḷu) eṉpaṉa ittakiya.
(piyāṉi = oruvaki naccup pāmpu. alluṇṭu = marumakaṉ. kokku = māmaram).
tamiḻiluḷḷa oruporuṭ pala coṟkaḷil cila, tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṉum, koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷil vaḻakkaṟā tuḷḷaṉa. aḵtāvatu, tamiḻnāṭṭu ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ cila koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭu ulakavaḻakkil uḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:		tamiḻ		teluṅku
		vetir		veturu
		cāla		tcālā
		paṇi		paṉi
		neyttōr	netturu
iṅṅaṉam olittiripu. coṟṟiripu. poruṭṭiripu. ticiccol, ilakkiyac colvaḻakku ākiya aivaki iyalpē centamiḻik koṭuntamiḻākkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟoṭu vaṭamoḻik kalappum cēriṉ. koṭuntamiḻ tiraviṭamākat tirikiṉṟatu. cēranāṭṭuc centamiḻ koṭun tamiḻākip piṉpu maliyāḷamāyt tirintuḷḷami kāṇka.
itukāṟum kūṟiyatu moḻittiṟamē. ilakkaṇa vilakkiyattiṟam nōkkiṉ, tamiḻukkuḷḷa taṉippaṭṭa toṉmutu ilakkaṇa vilakkiyam koṭuntamiḻukkilli. avaṟṟukkuḷḷaṉa vellām piṟkālattaṉavum āriyattiṟ kuriyaṉavumē. itaṉāl, moḻi niliyāl maṭṭumaṉṟi ilakkiya niliyālum muṟpaṭṭa cen tamiḻ. ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭatum ataṉ tiripāṉatumāṉa koṭun tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruttal muṭiyāteṉpatu tēṟṟam.
centamiḻiṉ tiripē koṭuntamiḻ; tiripiṉṟēl koṭun tamiḻilli. ākavē tamiḻt tiripē tiraviṭam; tiripiṉṟit tiraviṭamilli. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻika ḷellām tiruntiya vaṭivil pēcavum eḻutavum paṭiṉ, tamiḻē yaṉṟi avvat tiraviṭa moḻikaḷākāmi kāṇka.
cilar, uṇṭi (uruṇṭi), koṭu (koṇṭu) eṉat tamiḻil iruvaki vaḻakkilum aruki vaḻaṅkum aintāṟu coṟkaḷik koṇṭu. tamiḻum tiraviṭampōl tiripuṭiyatē eṉa nāṭṭa virumpukiṉṟaṉar. evvitikkum vilakkuṇṭātaliṉ, vilakkik koṇṭu vitiyi maṟukka muṭiyātu. oru poruḷiṉ perum pāṉmi yiyalpiyē ataṉ uṇmiyiyalpākak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ perumpālum iyalpuṭiyateṉṟum, tiraviṭam perumpālum tiripuṭiyateṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum. itaṉālēyē centamiḻiyum koṭun tamiḻiyum muṟiyē tamiḻum tiraviṭamum eṉa iṭikkālattu ilakkaṇikaḷ cilar vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. itaṉip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻukkiṉamāṉa moḻi kaḷiyellām tiraviṭameṉap pirittuk kūṟuvatē cālap porutta muṭittām. tiraviṭam eṉṉum tokuti tamiḻi uḷappaṭuttāmiyiṉ, avviraṇṭiyum oruṅkē kuṟikkat tamiḻam eṉṉum colliyē āḷavēṇṭum.
nūṉ, nūm, nuṅkaḷ; yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ; nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ; nīṉ, nīm, nīṅkaḷ; tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ; avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avi; eṉa moḻik kaṭippaṭiyāṉa mūviṭap peyar kaḷum, tamiḻil iyalpākavum oḻuṅkākavum irukkavum; teluṅkil, nēṉu, mēmu; maṉamu, nīvu, mīru; tāṉu, tāmu; vāṭu, ati(ā.me), vāru, ati, avi eṉat tirintum, oḻuṅkaṟṟum iṉamiḻantum iruttal kāṇka. moḻikkaṭippaṭiyāṉavum perumpālum tiriyātaṉavum ōracippaṭṭaṉavum ulaka moḻikaṭkellām toṭarpu kāṭṭuvaṉavum aṭikkaṭi collappaṭuvaṉavum nilittu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavumāṉa mūviṭap peyarkaḷē ittuṇit tiripaṭintiruppiṉ, vēṟu coṟ kaḷip paṟṟic colla vēṇṭuvatilli. im mūviṭap peyar kaḷi oru cōṟṟup patamākavē koḷka. iṅṅaṉam iyalpi yum tiripiyum muṟiyē tam ciṟappiyalpākak koṇṭa tamiḻi yum tiraviṭattiyum eṅṅaṉam oṉṟāka iṇikkavoṇṇum?
tamiḻ iyalpākavē cemmiyuṭimiyiṉ, tamiḻ eṉiṉum centamiḻ eṉiṉum oṉṟē. tamiḻiṉ tiripākiya koṭun tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟavē centamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. iyalpāṉa pāl taṇṇīrp pāliṉiṉṟum pirittuk kūṟat taṉippāl eṉappaṭṭāṟpōla.
tamiḻ oṉṟē mikunta ilakkaṇa varampuṭiyatu.
kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇu ṟatterin tāyntavip pacuntamiḻ ēṉi
maṇṇi ṭiccila ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇi ṭippaṭak kiṭantatā eṇṇavum paṭumō?
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar tarukkik kūṟiyatit tamiḻaraṉivarum. ciṟappākat tamiḻccivar, kavaṉittuk kāṇpārāka!
ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa eḻuttu, col, yāppu,  aṇi ākiya nāṉkumē, tamiḻil mikunta varampum virivum koṇ ṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉi, piṟamoḻikaṭ killāta poruḷilakkaṇattip paṟṟiyō collavē vēṇṭuvatilli. piṟamoḻikaḷi  makkaḷ eṅṅaṉamellām pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉarō, aṅṅaṉa mellām avaṟṟiṉ iyalpum ilakkaṇamum ami kiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, tamiḻ eṅṅaṉam pēcappaṭiṉum, mēṭiyēṟip pēcum pōtum ēṭeṭut teḻutum pōtum ilakkaṇa varampuṭaṉēyē pēcavum eḻutavumpaṭa vēṇṭum. ituvē teṉṟamiḻukku ilakkaṇikaḷ iṭṭa eṉṟumuḷa varampu. iv varampuṭiyatē tamiḻ allatu centamiḻ.
kakara voliyiṉ nuṇukkamākiya āytamoḻinta (uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya) muppāṉ olikaḷē. tamiḻukku allatu centamiḻukku uriyavām. koṭun tamiḻ moḻikaḷākiya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ, tamiḻukku ēlātaṉavum urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olippaṉavumāṉa ceyaṟki vallolikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivi āriyak kalappuṟṟa piṟkālattu vallolikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivi āriyak kalappuṟṟa piṟkālattu viḷivalla; ēṉeṉiṉ, tūya tiraviṭac coṟkaḷilum ivvolikaḷ colluṟuppāka amintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ 		teluṅku
Fo-Gudi 		(kuṭiki)
vēṅkaṭā  	veṅkkaṭṭa
entu (eṉṉatu) 	enttu
tamiḻ coṉmutal varāta eḻuttukkaḷ, koṭuntamiḻil ilakkaṇap pōlit tiripu kāraṇamākac coṉ mutal varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ  		teluṅku
ilatu 		lētu
poḻutu 	prottu
urappiyum eṭuttum kaṉittum olikkum āriya allatu tiraviṭavolikaḷellām. tamiḻiluḷḷa (ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa) eṉṉum valliṉattiṉ tiripukaḷē. ataṉālēyē, aivarukka mutalil tamiḻeḻuttukaḷē allatu tamiḻoli yeḻuttukaḷē variyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
orē tamiḻ valliṉa veḻuttu, tiraviṭattil vevvēṟiṭattil vevvēṟoliyāyt tirivatuṇṭu.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ  		teluṅku
cey	 	ccēyu
			jēcu (toliṉē jēsiṉa)
cāla	 	tccālā
āriyattilum iṅṅaṉamē.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻ  		teluṅku
poṟu 		A.S.beru, L.Fero, gero, Gk. Phero, Skt. bhri.
vitir 		Skt. vithura, Vidhura.
iṉi, āriya moḻikaḷil ayaṟcoṟkaḷaṉṟit taṉ coṟkaḷ kūṭa orēyeḻuttoliyi eṭuttum eṭukkātum āḷvatu muṇṭu. 
e-ṭu:
urappāvoli	eṭuppoli
purse		bursar, disburse.
ivveṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷāl, valliṉa veḻuttolikaṭ kellām tamiḻ vallolikaḷē mūlam eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aivarukkat tiṭiyiṉ mūṉṟum avvam mutalum eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatum iṅku nōkkattakkatu.
eṭukkā voliyiṉiṉṟu eṭuppoli tōṉṟumēyaṉṟi, eṭuppoliyiṉiṉṟu eṭukkā voli tōṉṟātu. kuḻanti vāyil eṭukkā voliyē muntit tōṉṟum, muntiyal māntaṉ kuḻanti pōṉṟavaṉ. muṉṉit tamiḻaṉ muntiyal māntaṉ pāṟpaṭṭavaṉ. ākiyāl, eṭukkā voliyē iyaṟkiyām. teṉkōṭiyil vāḻum anākarika makkaḷ moḻiyil eṭup polikaḷ illi. muṉṉāriyamākiya mēli moḻikaḷil avi tōṉṟip piṉṉāriyamākiya camaṟkirutattil muṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻc coṟkaḷ tirintē koṭuntamiḻ tōṉṟum. attirivu : (1) coṟṟiripu (2) poruṭṭiripu eṉa iru vakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, coṟṟiripu palvēṟu vakiya. avaṟṟuḷ cilavē īṇṭuk kāṭṭappaṭum.
tamir	teluṅku 	tiripuvaki
ūr 	ūru 	miki
kaṇ 	kaṉṉu	tiritalum mikiyum
nīr	mīru	pōliyum mikiyum
avaṉ	vāṭu	ilakkaṇap pōliyum tiritalum
māṟṟam	māṭṭa	tiritalum keṭutalum
eḻupatu	ṭeppati	ilakkaṇap pōliyum pōliyum tiritalum
curuṭṭu 	cuṭṭu	tokuttal
veṇṇey	veṉṉa	citivu
poruṭṭiripu. (1) āṭci vēṟupaṭal, (2) ciṟappu nīṅkal eṉa iruvakittu.
atē eṉṉum colli ām eṉṉum poruḷilum, mati eṉṉum collip pōtum eṉṉum poruḷilum, mali yāḷattil vaḻaṅkal āṭci vēṟupaṭal. viṭicollutalik kuṟikkum ceppu eṉṉum collic collutal eṉṉum poruḷilum, viral maṭakkiya kiyāl allatu kuccāl aṭit talik kuṟikkum koṭṭu eṉṉam colli aṭittal eṉṉum poruḷilum, teluṅkil vaḻaṅkal ciṟappu nīṅkalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkātu piṟa tiraviṭa nāṭukaḷil maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ ticic coṟkaḷākum. tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ kuḷḷēyē oru poruṭku ōriṭattil oru collum maṟṟōri ṭattil maṟṟoru collum collum tamiḻ iyalpu taṉip paṭṭatu. itaṉālēyē, taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ eṉappeṟṟatu. ākiyāl pirākirutattiṉiṉṟu camaṟkirutam ākkap peṟṟāṟ pōl. koṭuntamiḻākiya tiraviṭattiṉiṉṟu centamiḻākiya tamiḻ ākkappeṟṟatu eṉpatu oru ciṟitum poruntātu.
tamiḻ mikat tiruntiyatum paṇpaṭṭatumāṉa moḻiyākum. ilakkaṇa varampilā moḻi eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar iḻittuk kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻiyākiya camaṟkirutamum oṉṟu eṉpati maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu. tillicciṟ ṟampalat tumeṉ cintiyuḷḷum uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭaliṉ āyntoṇ ṭīntamiḻ eṉṟa māṇikkavācakarē viyantum nayantum kūṟuvarāyiṉ, tamiḻ ettuṇi viḻumiya moḻiyākum.
eṇṇukku meṭṭāt toṉmitoṭṭut tamiḻ vaḻaṅki variṉum, iṉṟum ataṉ perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tōṉṟum iyalpu niliyiluḷḷaṉa; koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷō perumpālum vēṟupaṭṭum vēraṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.
āka, ākātu, āyiṟṟēl, ām(ākum) eṉṟa coṟkaḷ. teluṅ kil muṟiyē kā, kātu, ayittē, auṉu eṉat tirintuḷḷaṉa. rāy (aṟi), ley(eḻu), lō(uḷ), lu(kaḷ), rōlu(ural), rā(vār) mutaliya teluṅkuc coṟkaḷil eṅṅaṉam vērkāṇa muṭiyum? āka, uḷ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tirintu, kā, lō eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tōṉṟumā? allatu, kā, lō eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tirintu āka, uḷ eṉṟa coṟkaḷ tōṉṟumā? iyalpiliruntu tiripu tōṉṟiyatā? tiripiliruntu iyalpu tōṉṟiyatā? ivaṟṟi yellām āyntu pārātu kallūrikaḷilum palkalikkaḻakaṅ kaḷilum talimit tamiḻp pulami naṭāttum perum pulavaruṅ kūṭap pirākirutattiliruntu camaṟkirutam tōṉṟiyavāṟē. koṭuntamiḻiliruntu centamiḻ tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟa kuruṭṭuk koḷki yuṭiyarāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṉimēlāyiṉum, avar āyntu uṇmi kāṇpārāka!
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ palavāyiṉum, avaṟṟuḷ, muntit tirintatum talimiyāṉatum tarukkuṟṟatum tamiḻip paḻippatumāṉa teluṅkē iṅkup perumpālum eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭa teṉka.  - centamiḻc celvi ceppiṭempar 1948



cempiyaṉ
cempiyaṉ - ciyya (b)
āriyar varumuṉ orukālattil intiyā muḻutum pāṇṭiyar āṭciyiliruntatu.
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmaliyaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi.
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ (cilap. 11:19-22) kūṟutal kāṇka.
akkālattiṟ pāṇṭiyak kuṭiyiṉar cilar vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu vāḻn tiruntaṉar. avarē pāṇṭavariṉ muṉṉōrāṉa vaṭanāṭṭut tiṅkaḷ marapiṉar. ataṉpiṉ, cōḻak kuṭiyiṉar cilarum vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟu āṇṭaṉar. avarē irāmaṉiṉ muṉṉōrāṉa vaṭanāṭṭuk katiravaṉ marapiṉar. itaṉālēyē, maṉu, māntātā, mucukuntaṉ, cempi mutaliyōr, cōḻarukkum vaṭanāṭṭuk katiravak kulattiṉarukkum potu muṉṉōrākac collap paṭuvārāyiṉar. cempi vaḻivantavaṉ cempiyaṉ. cempi eṉṉum peyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ cipi eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
cempiyaṉ = cōḻaṉ. cempiyar marukaṉ (puṟam. 228:9).
cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉappeṟṟamiyālum, cempiyaṉ tamiḻap pērariyaṉ, cempiyaṉ tamiḻavēḷ eṉpaṉa cōḻarāṟ koṭukkap peṟṟu vanta paṭṭaṅkaḷāyiruntamiyālum, cempi eṉṉum peyar tūya tamiḻc collākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. maṉu, māntātā mutaliyōriṉ tamiḻp peyar maṟiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa.
taliyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉap peṟṟirunta miyāl, cōḻarkuṭit toṉmutu paḻimiyum cempiyiṉ mutu paḻimiyum uṇarappeṟum.
āriyar varumuṉ vaṭanāṭṭil tamiḻar kuṭiyēṟiyiruntatu pōṉṟē, tamiḻa aracarum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntaṉar eṉa aṟika.
akattiyar tuvarāpatippōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭiyaṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmariyum patiṉeṇkuṭi vēḷiruḷ ḷiṭṭāriyum aruvāḷariyum koṇṭu pōntu, kāṭukeṭuttu nāṭākkip potiyiṉ kaṇ iruntār eṉṟa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatiyum nōkkuka.  (va.va. 165-166).



cempukkālam (Copper Age)
(tērarā. ki.mu. 30,000 - 15,000)
poṉkiṭippu varavarak kuṉṟiyatālum, aṇikalaṉkaṭkum uṇkalaṅkaṭkum kuṭikalaṅkaṭkumē poṉṉip peritum payaṉpaṭuttiyatālum, nāḷaṭiviṟ pala karuvikaḷiyum kalaṅkaḷiyum ceytaṟkēṟṟa cempik kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar.
kuykkoḷ koḻuntuvi neyyuṭi yaṭicil
maticēr nāṇmīṉ pōla naviṉṟa
ciṟupoṉ naṉkalañ cuṟṟa irīik
kēṭiṉ ṟāka pāṭunar kaṭumpeṉa
aritupeṟu polaṅkalam eḷitiṉiṉ vīci
naṭṭōr naṭṭa nallicik kumaṇaṉ  (puṟam. 160)
eṉṉum piṟkālac ceyyuḷ muṟkāla nilimiyiyum uṇarttum.
poruni yeṉṉum nelli māvaṭṭa āṟṟaṅ kariyiṟ cempu kiṭittatāl, avvāṟu tāmparac ceppu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tāmira paraṇi eṉṉum irupiṟappic collāl  (hybrid) piṟkālattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. nelli yarukiluḷḷa oru nakar ceppaṟi (cempu + aṟi) eṉap peyarpeṟṟuḷḷatu. civaperu māṉiṉ aivaki ampalaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa nelliyam palam, ceppōṭu vēyap peṟṟatāl ceppampalam (tāmiracapi) eṉappaṭṭatu. potiyamali cempiṟ poruppu eṉappaṭṭatu.
teṉkāl viṭukkuñ cempiṟ poruppu  (kallā. 51:11).
cempiṟ poruppu = cemputtātu uḷḷa mali. itic ceppu variyeṉṟu kuṟṟālat talapurāṇaṅ kūṟum.
cenniṟamāyiruntataṉāl, cempu eṉṟum cempoṉ eṉṟum tāmparam eṉṟum, putitāyk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa māḻi allatu poṉṉam peyar peṟṟatu.
cem - cempu - ceppu.
tum - tumpu - tumparam = civappāṉa attippaḻam, appaḻam paḻukkum maram.
tumparam - va. utumpara.
tumpu - tuppu = 1. civappu. 2. pavaḻam. tuppuṟaḻ toṇṭic cevvāy (cīvaka. 550). 3. arakku. (piṅ.).
tum - tumar - tuvar = 1. civappu tuvaritaḻc cevvāy (cilap. 6:26). 2. pavaḻam. (tivā). 3. tuvaram payaṟu allatu ceṭi. tuvarṅkōṭu (tol. eḻut. 363, uri) 4. kāvi. tuvaruṟukiṉṟa vāṭi yuṭalpōrttu (tēvā 608:10). 5. tuvarppu. tuvarmaruvap puḷippēṟṟi (tilava. tila). 6. nāval allatu pūvanti, kaṭu, nelli tāṉṟi, āl, aracu, atti, itti, muttakkācu allatu karuṅkāli, māntaḷir eṉṟa pattut tuvarppu maruntup poruḷ. viriyoṭu tuvaruñ cērtti (cīvaka. 623) 7. pākku. vāca maṇattuvar vāykkoḷ vōrum (paripā. 12:22).
kācukkaṭṭiyum cāyappākkum pōṉṟa ceñcarakkut tuvarppā yiruppatāl, tuvar eṉṉuñ col tuvarppu poruḷ koṇṭatu.
tuvar (civappu) - te. tokaru. 1. tokar.
tuvar (tuvarppu) - va. tuvara.
tuvarttal = 1. civattal. tuvartta cevvāy 
(kamparā. nīrviḷi. 13). 2. tuvarppātal.
tuvarppu = 1. aṟucuvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. (piṅ.).
2. pattu (tuvarppup poruṭ ṭoki) (tilava. tila) 
tuvarāṭi = kāviyāṭi. antuva rāṭip potuvaṉoṭu (kalit. 102:35).
tuvarkkaṭṭi = kācukkaṭṭi.
tuvarkkaṇṭal = centāḻi. (tilava. tila. 135).
tuvarkkāy = pākku. tuvarkkāyoṭu cukkuttiṉṉum (tēvā. 660:10).
tuvarppū = vāṭic civanta pū. taṉṟali taṅkiya tuvarppū vēṟṟi (patiṉo. tirukkaṇ. maṟam. nak. 61).
tuvarvaliyuṟutti = tuvarppu maruntu (Astringent tonic).
tuvar - tuvaram = tuvarppu. (piṅ.). tuvaram - va. tuvara.
tuvar - tuvari = 1. ilavam pū. tuvarik kaṉivāy nilamaṅki (tiv. periya ti. 8:8:9).
2. kāviniṟam. tuvari yāṭiyar maṭṭiyar (tiv. periya ti. 2:1:6).
tuvarittal = cenniṟamūṭṭutal. tuvaritta vuṭiyavarkkum (tiv. periya ti. 5:6:8).
tuvar - tuvari = 1. tuvaram payaṟu. 2. tuvarañceṭi. 3. kāṭṭatti, 4. karuntuvari, 5. cempurukki vārttuk kaṭṭiya ceppuk kōṭṭi matil.
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici 
yuvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu  (puṟam. 201).
6. kaṇṇaṉ āṇṭa tuvāraki. va. tvārakā.
tuvarañcampā = oru nelvaki.
tuvarikkōmāṉ = iṭikkaḻakap pulavarākac collappaṭum oruvar. (iṟi. 1, uri).
tuvarippati - va. tvāravatī.
tuvar - tukar - tukir = 1. pavaḻam. poṉṉun tukiru muttum (puṟam. 218).
2. pavaḻakkoṭi. centukir paṭarun tirikkaṭal (kallā. muruka. vaṇa). tukir - te.ka.tu. tokaru.
tukirttāḷi = pavaḻamalliki (mali).
tukir - tukil = 1. cen nallāṭi. paṭṭun tukilu muṭuttu (nālaṭi. 264). 2. centuṇikkoṭi (piṅ.). tukil - tukiliki = centuṇik koṭi.
puricimēṟ puṉinta vāṇilā neṭun tukiliki (kanta pu. tirunakara. 20).
tumparam - tamparam - tāmparam = cempu. (patārtta. 1170). tāmparam - va. tāmra.
tamparam - tampar = veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil (māṟaṉalaṅ. 470, utā.).
tamparam = tampalam = 1. veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil. tilli nallār potut tampalaṅ koṇarntō (tirukkō. 396). te. tamma. 2. veṟṟili pākku. tiyāl tampalan tiṉṟiyō (kalit. 65). 3. tampalap pūcci.
tampalam - va. tāmpūla.
tampalap pūcci = tampalam pōṟ civanta mūtāy.
tampalam - tampal = veṟṟili tiṉṟu civanta eccil.
veḷḷilit tampal  (kamparā. varikkā. 49).
tampalam - tampali = civanta ilantip paḻam, atu paḻukkum muṭceṭi.
tumar - tamar - tamari - tāmari - cem muḷari.
tāmari eṉṉuñ col iṉṟu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷiḻantu, potupporuḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉāl, cemmuḷariyik kuṟikkac centāmari eṉṟu mikipaṭak kūṟalākac colla vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. itu, ariñāṇ koṭi, kuḷirnta taṇṇīr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻu vaḻakkē.
tāmari - va. tāmarasa.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, vaṭacollākak karutappaṭum tāmparam, tāmari eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷum teṉcollē yeṉṟum, iṅṅaṉamē nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil iṉammaṟintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum tamiḻ uṇmiyil tiraviṭattāyum āriya mūlamum ākumeṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
poṟkālattiṟ poṉṉāl uṇkalam kuṭikalam nīrkkalam mutaliya palvakik kalaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavēṉum, avi perumakkaḷālēyē payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṉmutalākap potumakkaḷ puḻaṅkiya māḻik kalaṅkaḷ, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaviyē. ataṉāl, cila kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭiviyuṭiya kalaṅkaḷ evvek karuviyāṟ ceyyappaṭiṉum, iṉṟum cempu, ceppu, ceppukkuṭam eṉac cempuk kālap peyarkaḷālēyē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
niliyāka vittup pōṟṟa vēṇṭiya āvaṇaṅkaḷum muṟikaḷum, eṅkum eḷitāka eṭuttuc cellak kūṭiya vakiyil, ceppēṭukaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuc ceppup paṭṭiyam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
cempil vārkkappaṭṭa teyvap paṭimikaḷ cempuk kuṭṭi yeṉṟum, cepput tirumēṉi yeṉṟam collap paṭṭaṉa.
puḷiyiṭṭa cempiyum pōṟṟukilēṉuyar poṉṉeṉavē 	(paṭṭiṉattār, potu. 61).
mutaṉmutal marattiṟ kuṭiyappaṭṭa allatu ceyyappaṭṭa marakkāl eṉṉum mukattalaḷavik karuvi, cempilum vārkkap paṭṭuc ceppukkāl eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. ceppukkāl tirucciṟṟampalamuṭiyāṉ eṉpatu, cōḻar kālattil vaḻaṅkiya nellaḷakkuṅ karuvi vaki.
uḷi, katti, aram, vāḷ, kaṟaṇṭi, kalappik koḻu mutaliya palavakik karuvikaḷum, poṟkālattiṟkup piṉ cempilēyē ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
valiya pakivarālum eḷitāyt takarkka muṭiyāvāṟu, cempi yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiya iñci yeṉṉuṅ kōṭṭi matilvaki, erumiyūr (micūr) nāṭṭut tuvari nakariliruntami mēṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. irāvaṇaṉ kōṭṭi attakiya matiluṭimiyāṟ ceppuk kōṭṭi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cempiṭṭuc ceyta iñcit tirunakarc celvam tēṟi  (kamparā. kumpa. 160)
neytal nila māntar, kaṭṭumaram, timil, paṭaku mutaliya kalaṅkaḷic celutti āḻkaṭalil mīṉ piṭittatoṭu, calaṅkukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu muttuk kuḷittum pavaḻat tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟu pavaḻaṅ koṇarntum tam vāḻkkiyi vaḷampaṭuttiṉar. kiḷiñcil muttumālikaḷic celvarum, valampuri iṭampuri muttumāli kaḷi aracarum, vāṅkiyaṇintaṉar. caṅku vaḷiyalum pavaḻamāliyum peṇṭirāl virumpiyaṇiyap paṭṭaṉa. cippi nīṟṟuc cuṇṇam ciṟanta veṇcutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
oruvaṉ oruttiyoṭu maṟivākak kūṭiyapiṉ avaḷoṭu kūṭavilliyeṉṟu maṟutturittalum, avaḷik kiviṭutalum, oruvaṉ oruttiyoṭu valintu kūṭutalum, ākiya poyyum oḻukkak kēṭum palariṭit tōṉṟiyapiṉ, peṟṟōriṉ icivu peṟṟē oru peṇṇik koḷḷumāṟum, irupālarum veḷippaṭiyākak kaṟpuṭaṉ oḻukumāṟum, viḻāvoṭu kūṭiya karaṇam eṉṉum tirumaṇac caṭaṅkip periyōr ēṟpaṭutti vittaṉar.
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉḷar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa.  (tol. 1091).
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē  (. 1088)
tirumaṇak karaṇam perumpālum piṉvarumāṟu naṭantatu.
uḻuntutalip peyta koḻuṅkaḷi mitavi
peruñcōṟ ṟamali niṟpa nirikāl
taṇperum pantart tarumaṇal ñemiri
maṉiviḷak kuṟuttu māli toṭarik
5   kaṉiyiruḷ akaṉṟa kaviṉpeṟu kālik
kōḷkāl nīṅkiya koṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷ
kēṭil viḻuppukaḻ nāḷtali vanteṉa
uccik kuṭattar puttakal maṇṭiyar
potucey kampali mutucem peṇṭir
10  muṉṉavum piṉṉavum muṟimuṟi tarattarap
putalvaṟ payanta titaliyav vayiṟṟu
vāliḻi makaḷir nālvar kūṭik
kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala vutavip
peṟṟōṟ peṭkum piṇiyi yākeṉa 
15   nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari
palliruṅ katuppiṉ nelloṭu tayaṅka
vatuvi naṉmaṇaṅ kaḻinta piṉṟik
kalleṉ cummiyar ñerēreṉap pukutantu
pēriṟ kiḻatti yākeṉat tamartara
20  ōriṟ kūṭiya vuṭaṉpuṇar kaṅkul  (akam. 86).
itaṉ poruḷ :
1-4. uḻuttam paruppoṭu cērttuc camitta koḻumiyāṉa kuḻinta poṅkalōṭu periya cōṟṟut tiraḷiyi uṇṭal iṭiyaṟātu nikaḻa, variciyāṉa kālkaḷiyuṭiya kuḷirnta periya pantaṟkīḻk koṇṭuvantu koṭṭiya maṇalip parappi, vīṭṭil viḷakkēṟṟi, mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu,
5-10 tīya kōḷkaḷiṉ toṭarpu nīṅkiya vaḷinta veṇṇi lāvik kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟanta pukaḻiyuṭiya cakaṭam eṉṉum nāḷ aṭiya, mikunta iruḷ nīṅkiya aḻaku poruntiya viṭiyaṟ kāliyil, uccantaliyiṟ kuṭattiyum kiyiṟ putiya akaṉṟa montiyiyum uṭiya, maṇañ ceytu vikkum āravāra muḷḷa mutiya maṅkala makaḷir muṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum piṉṉē taruvaṉavaṟṟiyum muṟippaṭi eṭutteṭuttuk koṭukka,
11-16. makaṉip peṟṟa tēmaluḷḷa aḻakiya vayiṟṟiyum tūya aṇikaḷiyum uṭiya makaḷir nālvar kūṭiniṉṟu, kaṟpiṉiṉṟum tavaṟātu palanaṟpēṟukaḷip peṟṟu, uṉ kaṇavaṉ virumpip pēṇum viruppattiṟ kiṭamākuka eṉṟu vāḻtti, nīroṭu cērttup peyta kuḷirnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya pūkkaḷ, aṭarnta kariya kūntalil nelloṭu viḷaṅka,
17. nalla maṇaviḻā muṭinta piṉpu,
18 - 20. cuṟṟattār āravāra ōciyuṭaṉ virintu vantu, periya maṉikkiḻatti yāvāy eṉṟu collic cērttu vikka, ōr aṟiyil uṭaṉ kūṭiya puṇarccikkuriya iravil.
mippaṟap puḻukkiṉ neykkaṉi veṇcōṟu 
variyā vaṇmiyoṭu puriyōrp pēṇip
puḷḷuppuṇarn tiṉiya vākat teḷḷoḷi
aṅkaṇ iruvicumpu viḷaṅkat tiṅkaṭ
5   cakaṭam maṇṭiya tukaḷtīr kūṭṭattuk
kaṭinakar puṉintu kaṭavuṭ pēṇip
paṭumaṇa muḻavoṭu parūuppaṇi yimiḻa
vatuvi maṇṇiya makaḷir vituppuṟṟup
pūkkaṇum imiyār nōkkupu maṟiya
10  meṉpū vākip puṉpuṟak kavaṭṭili
paḻaṅkaṉṟu kaṟitta payampamal aṟukit
taḻaṅkukural vāṉiṉ talippeyaṟ kīṉṟa
maṉṉumaṇi yaṉṉa māyitaḻp pāvit
taṇṇaṟu mukiyoṭu veṇṇūl cūṭṭit
15  tūvuṭip polintu mēvarat tuvaṉṟi
maḻipaṭ ṭaṉṉa maṇaṉmali pantar
iḻiyaṇi ciṟappiṟ peyarviyarp pāṟṟit
tamarnamak kītta talināḷ iraviṉ  (akam. 136)
itaṉ poruḷ :
1-9. neñcē, kuṟṟamaṟap paruppuṭaṉ kalantākkiya neymikunta veṇcōṟṟi, nīkkāta īkit taṉmiyuṭaṉ uyarnta cuṟṟattār mutaliyōri uṇpittu, puṭkuṟi iṉitākakkūṭa, teḷḷiya oḷiyiyuṭiya aḻakiya iṭamakaṉṟa periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamaṟa viḷaṅka, tiṅkaḷic cakaṭam kūṭiya kuṟṟa maṟṟa naṉṉāḷil, maṇa maṉiyic cuvaṭittuk kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭu, maṇamēḷattuṭaṉ periya muracam muḻaṅka, talivikku maṇa nīrāṭṭiya makaḷir, tam kūriya kaṇkaḷāl imiyātu nōkki maṟiya,
10-18. melliya pūviyuṭiya vākiyiṉ aḻakaṟṟa piṉ puṟattik koṇṭa kavartta iliyi, mutiya kaṉṟu, kaṟitta paḷḷattiṟ paṭarnta aṟukiṉ, iṭi muḻaṅkiya vāṉattu mutaṉmaḻikku arumpiya kaḻuviya nīlamaṇi pōlum kariya itaḻiyuṭiya pāviyotta kiḻaṅkiṭattuḷḷa kuḷirnta maṇamuḷḷa arum puṭaṉ, cērttuk kaṭṭiya veḷḷiya nūlic cūṭṭi, tūya puttāṭiyāṟ poliyac ceytu, viruppattuṭaṉ kūṭi, maḻi yōci pōṉṟa maṇavōci mikunta pantalil aṇikaḷi mikutiyāy aṇintiruntataṉāl uṇṭāṉa viyarviyi viciṟi yāl āṟṟi, avaḷ cuṟṟattār avaḷi namakkut tanta mutalnāḷ iravil
ivi piṟkālattaṉavāyiṉum, pirāmaṇaṉum camaṟkiruta mantiramuṅ kalavāta paṇṭit tūya tamiḻat tirumaṇa viḻāviṉ iyalpit teḷivākak kāṭṭuvaṉavākum.
marutatti yaṭuttu nāṭṭiṟ kaṇittāka vuḷḷa mulli nilavāṇar, āṭumāṭerumi yeṉṉum munniriyiyum, ciṟappāka āniri yi, vaḷarppati mutaṉmit toḻilākavum, vāṉāvārip payir viḷivippatit tuṇittoḻilākavuṅ koṇṭu, piṟarkkut tīṅku ceyyātum ēṟutaḻuvi maṇakkum maṟaviyali mēṟkoṇ ṭum vāḻntuvara; kuṟiñciyi yaṭuttuk kāṭṭuc cārpākavum nāṭṭiṟkut tolivilum uḷḷa mulli nilavāṇarō, tam vāḻnilam āṇṭutoṟum vēṉilil vaṟṟivaṟaṇṭu vem pāliyāka māṟiyatāl, āṟalittaliyuñ cūṟikōṭali yumē tam kulattoḻilākak koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. koṭu vaṟaṭci koḷḷikkē tūṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻakam kuṟiñci mulli marutam neytal pāli eṉa aintiṇip pākupāṭuṟṟatu. tiṇi aintāyiṉum, nili yāṉa nilam nāṉkēyātalāl, ñālam nāṉilam eṉṉappaṭṭatu. pālinilattalivar viṭali, kō, vēḷ, mīḷi, kāḷi eṉap palpeyar peṟṟaṉar.
marutanilattu ūrkaḷilēyē nel viḷintataṉāl, pāli nilantavira maṟṟa munnila māntarum tattam nilattuc ciṟappup paṇṭaṅkaḷi aṅkuk koṇarntu nelliṟku māṟiṉar:
kāṉuṟi vāḻkkik katanāy vēṭṭuvaṉ
māṉṟaci corinta vaṭṭiyum āymakaḷ
tayirkoṭu vanta tacumpu niṟiya
ēriṉ vāḻnar pēri lariviyar
kōkkaṇṭu maṉṉar kurikaṭal pukkilar kōkaṉakap 
pūkkaṇṭu koṭṭiyum pūvā toḻintila
eṉṉum taṉippāṭalāl, maṉṉaṉiṉum periyavaṉkō eṉpati aṟika. kō eṉpatu kōvaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu. kōkkaḷi (ākkaḷi)k kākkum āyaṉ pōla makkaḷik kāppavaṉ aracaṉ eṉpatu karuttu. ivvoppumi paṟṟiyē, āyaṉ kōl pōl aracaṉum orukōlik kiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ. āṭci nērmiyā yirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatik kāṭṭavē, nērāṉa kōl koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kōl āṭciyik kuṟikkuñ ciṉṉa mātalāl, muṟiyāṉa āṭci ceṅkōl eṉṟum, muṟitavaṟiya āṭci koṭuṅkōl eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa. koṭumi vaḷivu. cemmi nērmi. kōṉ eṉṉum teṉ collē turukkiyiṟ kāṉ eṉṟu tirintatākak kālṭuvelār kūṟuvar.
kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kō = aracaṉ.
kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ iṉṟum āyaṉiyuṅ kuṟittal kāṇka. kōṉ eṉṉuñ col ār eṉṉum uyarvup paṉmi yīṟu peṟṟuk kōṉār eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.
pāli nilantavira maṟṟa nāṉku nilaṅkaḷuḷ, oṉṟum palavuṅ kikkoṇṭavar kōkkaḷāvar. amiccar, paṭittalivar, pūcakar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉṉum aimperuṅkuḻuvār avarukku āṭcit tuṇiyāyiruntaṉar.
aintiṇikaḷun tōṉṟiyapiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ kuṟiñciyil vēṭṭi yāṭuṅ kuṟavarum, mulliyil munniri vaḷarkkum iṭiyarum, marutattil uḻutoḻilic ciṟappākac ceyyum uḻavarum, pāliyil vaḻippaṟittuk koḷḷiyaṭikkum maṟavarum, neyta lil mīṉpiṭikkum paṭavarum, ākap perumpālum ovvoru vakuppārē vāḻntiruppar.
ovvoru tiṇinilattilum uḷḷa ovvoru makkaṭ kuṭiyiruppilum, aṭakkiyāḷavum vaḻakkut tīrkkavum oru talivaṉ tōṉṟiyiruppaṉ. avaṉ iṟṟi nāṭṭāṇmik kāraṉpōl piṟar ceyyun toḻiliyē ceytiruppaṉ. teyva vaṇakkam allatu accam iyaṟkip paṇpātalāl, tali vaṉukkup piṉ oru pūcakaṉ allatu tēvarāḷaṉun tōṉṟi yiruppaṉ. avarkkumuṉ, marutanilap pērūrkaḷil paṇṭamāṟṟiyar allatu vaṇikar tōṉṟiyiruppar. āṇṭu tōṟum pala kūlaṅkaḷum putitākavum ērāḷamākavum viḷintatāl, muntiṉa āṇṭil mīntu pōṉa kūlaṅkaḷi vāṅkavum, avaṟṟiṟ kīṭāka uḻavarkku vēṇṭiya piṟanilattup poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu vantu taravum, uḻavariṉiṉṟē oru cilar vaṇikarākap pirintirut tal vēṇṭum. yāṇṭukaḻi veṇṇel eṉṟu aiṅkuṟu nūṟṟuppāṭal kūṟuvati nōkkuka. oṉṟiraṇṭām vāṇikam eṉpatum vaṇika muyaṟciyi ūkkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. vaṇikarukkup piṉ palvēṟu kittoḻilāḷar paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟa lāyiṉar. nākarikamum araciyalum vaṇikamum vaḷara vaḷara, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa paṇikaḷum aluvalkaḷum putitu putitāka ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aṟivu vaḷarcci yaṭintataṉāl, pūcakar kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyat toḻilum mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. uḻavu, vaṇikam, kāval, kalvi eṉṉum nāṟperun toḻil paṟṟi, marutanila makkaḷ uḻavar, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉa nāl vakuppārāy amintaṉar. patiṉeṇ kittoḻilāḷarum uḻavarkkup pakkat tuṇivarākak karutappaṭṭataṉāl, avaruḷ aṭakkappaṭṭaṉar.
paṇṇiyam = paṇṇappaṭṭa poruḷ, viṟpaṉip paṇṭam. (maturik. 405).
paṇṇiyam - paṇṇiyaṉ - paṇṇikaṉ - paṇikaṉ - vaṇikaṉ = pala paṇṭaṅ kaḷi viṟpavaṉ. vaṇikaṉ - vāṇikaṉ.
pārppāṉ = nūlkaḷip pārppavaṉ. itu aṉ cāriyi allatu aṉaṉ iṟu peṟṟup pārppaṉaṉ eṉṟum niṟkum. mukkāla viṉimuṟṟum iṅṅaṉam īṟu koḷḷum.
e-ṭu:	pārttaṉaṉ 		- 	iṟanta kālam.
		pārkkiṉṟaṉaṉ	- 	nikaḻ kālam
		pārppaṉaṉ		- 	etir kālam
paṭippaṉar (cilap. 10:134), virivaṉaṉ (puṟam. 150) eṉṉum etirkāla viṉi muṟṟukkaḷi nōkkuka. pārppaṉaṉ eṉpatu pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉum colliṉ tiripaṉṟu. kalvit toḻil ceyyum āciriyarum camaya kuravarum pōṉṟa tamiḻariyellām pārppā reṉṟē colla vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇari iccollāṟ kuṟikka vēṇṭiṉ, āriyap pārppār (allatu pārppaṉar) eṉṟu aṭi koṭuttē kūṟavēṇṭum,
moḻivaḷarcciyil, eṇvēṟṟumikaḷum mukkālamum palvaki ecca muṟṟukkaḷum, muḻuniṟivāṉa coṟṟoṭaramippum, cempukkālattil tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. eḻuttu vaki yil oliyeḻuttut (Phonetic characters) tōṉṟiyirukkum. āyiṉ, ekara vokarak kuṟiluyi rolikaḷum ḷakara ḻakara ṟakara ṉakara meyyolikaḷum perumpālun tōṉṟiyirā. ik kālattil tāṉ, pirākirutam eṉṉum vaṭatirāviṭa moḻi yāḷariṉ muṉṉōr vaṭanāvalam (intiyā) ceṉṟiruppar. pāvakiyil āciriyamum kaliyum tōṉṟi yirukkum. makkaḷiṉ eṇṇuntiṟaṉ patiṉāyiram vari ceṉṟirukkum.
matattuṟiyil, maṟumiyuṇarvum viṇṇulakak koḷkiyum tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
ceyyuḷ
paṇṭip pulavar urinaṭi, ceyyuḷ eṉṉum iruvaki naṭiyuḷḷum ceyyuḷ ciṟantateṉṟu kaṇṭē, urikaḷum akaramutali pōṉṟa uriccoṟṟokutikaḷum uṭpaṭa, ellāp paṉuvalkaḷiyum ceyyuḷil iyaṟṟiṉar. itiyaṟiyātu, paṇṭip pulavarkku urinaṭiyil eḻutat teriyāteṉṟum, urinaṭi nūlillātatu paṇṭiyilakkiyattiṟku oruperuṅ kuṟiyē yeṉṟum, kūṟuvār porimāvi mecciṉāṉām pokkivāyaṉ. eṉṟa paḻamoḻikkē eṭuttuk kāṭṭāvār.
nālvakip pākkaṭkuḷ, veṇpāvum kalippāvum pōṉṟavi vēṟemmoḻiyiṉuṅ kāṇamuṭiyātu. iṟivaṉi vaḻuttu vataṟkum kātaṟceytiyi vaṇṇippataṟkum, ottāḻicik kalivaki pōṉṟa yāppu vēṟoṉṟumilli.
aṇi
poruḷi viḷakkuvataṟkum teriyāta poruḷit terivip pataṟkum uvami iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiyālum, perumpālum ellā aṇikaṭkum uvamiyē mūlamātalālum, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟu vāriṉ tiṟamikkut takkavāṟu iyalpākavē aṇi amiyu mātalālum, kālañ cellac cellap putituputitāka aṇikaḷ tōṉṟumātalālum, mutaṉūlāciriyar aṇivakiyil viḷakkak karutiyatu uvami yoṉṟē.
uvami ellāmoḻikaḷiluṅ kiyāḷappaṭumēṉum, uḷḷuṟi yeṉṉum uvami vaki tamiḻukkē ciṟappāka vuriyatām. oru talivaṉit talivi allatu tōḻi viḷikkum pōtu, avaṉ taṉmi pulappaṭumāṟu avaṉ nāṭṭuk karup poruḷiṉ ceyali eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu uḷḷuṟi yuvamamām.
pintaliya nāka paṇameṉṟu pūkattiṉ
aintaliyiṉ pāḷitaṉi yiyuṟṟu - manti
teḷiyā tirukkun tirunāṭā vuṉṉi
oḷiyātu kāṭṭuṉ ṉuru.  (naḷa. kalinīṅ. 70)
itu irutupaṉṉaṉiṉ tērōṭṭiyāka vantirunta naḷaṉi nōkkit tamayantiyiṉ tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
naḷaṉ kārkkōṭakaṉ eṉṉum pāmpiṉāṟ kaṭiyuṇṭu niṟam māṟiyirunta taṉāl, teḷivāka aṭiyāḷan teriyātu mayaṅki niṉṟa tōḻi, uṉ nāṭṭumanti, aimpirivāṉa kamukam pāḷiyip pārttu aintali nākamō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟut teḷiyātirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟatu, nāṉum uṉṉip pārttu naḷaṉō vēṟoruvaṉō eṉṟu mayaṅkit teḷiyātirukkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa niṟpatāl, uḷḷuṟi yuvamamām.
talikkaḻakak kālattilēyē, miṭaṟṟiciyum (vāyppāṭṭum) tōl tuḷi narampu uṟi (va. kañcam) eṉṉum nālvakik karuviyiciyum, vaḷarcci yaṭintiruntaṉa.
paṇ (7 ici), paṇṇiyal (6 ici), tiṟam (5 ici), tiṟattiṟam (4 ici) eṉap paṇkaḷi nālvakiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar.
narappukkaruvikaḷ muntiyāḻ (ātiyāḻ, peruṅkalam, āyira narampu), vilyāḻ (pala narampu) pēriyāḻ (21 narampu), cuṟaviyāḻ (makarayāḻ 19 narampu), cakōṭa yāḻ (14 narampu), muṇṭaka yāḻ (9 narampu), ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ (7 narampu), cīṟiyāḻ (4 narampu), curiyāḻ (1 narampu), eṉap palavakiya. āyiram eṉṟatu perun tokiyi. uṟi yeṉṟatu uṟiyiṉālēyē ceyyappaṭṭa tāḷak karuviyi.
narampuk karuvi ici vaḷarcciyiṉ uccaniliyik kāṭṭum. yāḻt taṇṭiṉ kaṭiyil yāḷittaliyuruvam poruttap paṭṭataṉāl, narappukkaruvi yāḻ eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. maṭaṅkal (ciṅka) uṭampum yāṉittutikki pōṉṟa nīṇṭa mūkkuṅ koṇṭa yāḷi eṉṉum vilaṅkiṉam, kumarināṭṭiṟkē yuriyatākum.
yāḻiciyiṉpattil āḻntu īṭupaṭṭa acuṇam eṉṉum vilaṅkum kumarināṭṭiṟ kuriyatē. atu paṟaviyō eṉṟu cilar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, ati acuṇamā eṉṟu cintāmaṇi kiḷantē kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉṉaḷik kural kēṭṭa vacuṇamā (cīvaka. 1602). naṟṟiṇi acuṇaṅ koḷpavar kipōl (304) eṉṟu iṉacciṟappup peyariyē kuṟittiruppiṉum, iciyaṟi vilaṅkākiya acuṇa māṉi eṉṟu, oru māṉvakiyākavē nārāyaṇacāmi aiyar uri varintirukkiṉṟār. māṉiṟicci ellārālum virumpap paṭuvatālum, māṉvēkamāy ōṭum vilaṅkiṉamātalālum, acuṇam oru ciṟanta māṉvakiyākavē iruntirukkalām.
yāḷikkum, valimiyum tōṟṟap polivum maṭṭumaṉṟi, iciyuṇarcciyum iruntirukkalām.
āyappāli, vaṭṭappāli, caturappāli, tirikōṇap pāli eṉṉum paṇtirippu muṟikaḷ, muṟiyē, muḻuviciyum ariyiciyum kāliciyum arikkāliciyum paṟṟiyavāyiṉ, tamiḻicikku ulakil iṉṟum iṇiyilliyeṉṟē collalām.
nāṭakam
iyaṟṟamiḻkkuriya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkattil nāṭaka vaḻakkamum kalantiruppataṉālum, talikkaḻakattu ilakkaṇam māpiṇṭa meṉṉum muttamiḻilakkaṇamākavē vaḻaṅkiyatālum, nāṭakamum akkālattu vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
kaṇakku
kumarināṭṭu makkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟ kālattiṟ pattu variyum, putukkaṟ kālattil nūṟu variyum, poṟkālattil āyiram variyum, cempukkālattiṟ pattāyiram variyum, uṟikkālattil nūṟāyiram variyum, irumpuk kālat toṭakkattiṟ pattu nūṟāyiram eṉṉum kōṭi variyum, eṇṇat terintiruttal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, neytal, kuvaḷi, āmpal, caṅkam, tāmari, veḷḷam, paratam mutaliya aṭukkiya kōṭikaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. paratam eṉpatu, 1 - iṉpiṉ 24 cuṉṉaṅ koṇṭatu.
toṭakkattil iru ki viralkaḷiyum eṇṇip patteṉṉum eṇṇip peruntokiyākak koṇṭataṉālēyē, mēṟ paṭṭa piṟkālattup peruntokikaḷi yellām muṟiyē pappattu maṭaṅku uyarntaṉavākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
mēlvāyilakkam pōṉṟē kīḻvāyilakkamum (fractions) neṭṭaḷavu kaṇṭaṉar. muntiri 1/320. kīḻ muntiri 1/320 - il 1/320 iruvāyilakkaṅkaṭkum catura vāypāṭukaḷum (Square tables) iruntaṉa. kīḻ vāykkuc ciṟukuḻi; mēl vāykkup peruṅkuḻi.
kōlāriya māntar kiviralkaḷōṭu kāl viralkaḷiyuñ cērttu eṇṇiyataṉāl, pēreṇkaḷi yellām pappattu maṭaṅkākak koḷḷātu ivvirupatu maṭaṅkākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āyiṉ, mēlināṭuṭpaṭa nākarika nāṭukaḷellām tamiḻ muṟiyiyē piṉpaṟṟi varuvatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
kaṇiyam
kumarināṭṭuk kaṇitanūl vallār kūrṅkaṇṇarāyirunta miyiṉ, 27 nāṭkaḷiyum katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷumallāta aiṅkōḷkaḷiyum paṉṉī rōrikaḷiyum kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar.
pakaliravāl nāḷiyum, vaḷarpiṟi tēypiṟiyāl mātatti yum, irutici iyaṉattāl (ayaṉattāl) āṇṭiyum, aṟintaṉar.
eḻukōḷkaḷāl kiḻami (vāram) eṉṉum eḻunāṭkāla aḷavum, paṉṉī rōrikaḷāṟ paṉṉiru mātappeyarum, ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
mātam eṉpatu teṉcollē. mati = tiṅkaḷ, mati - mātam - va. māsa. tiṅkaḷik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉuñ col vaṭamoḻiyil illi. x.neh.: moon-month.
eḻunāṭ kiḻami ulakameṅkum toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatu, tamiḻ nākarikap paravaliṉ viḷivākum. viviliyattiṉ mutaṟ pottaka mutalatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa paṭippu varalāṟṟil, iṟivaṉār aṟunāḷ paṭittu ēḻām nāḷ ōyntiruntār eṉṟatu, tamiḻar vakutta kiḻami muṟiyit taḻuviyē.
āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkivarum paṉṉī rōrip peyarkaḷum, tamiḻppeyarkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppē.
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi eṉṉum perumpoḻutu (āṇṭiṉ pakutikaḷ) āṟaṟkum etirāka; kāli, naṇpakal, ēṟpāṭu (cāyuṅkālam), māli, yāmam, vikaṟi eṉṉum ciṟupoḻutu (nāḷiṉ pakutikaḷ) āṟum, iyaṟkiyāka amintaṉa.
matam
talikkaḻakak kālattil, civa matattiṟkum tirumāl matattiṟkum potuvāṉatum, ūr pēr kuṇaṅkuṟiyaṟṟu eṅkum niṟin tirukkum oru param poruḷi eṅkum eṉṟum uḷḷattil toḻuvatum, āṉa kaṭavuḷ neṟi (citta matam) eṉṉum uyarnili matam, tamiḻat tuṟaviyarāl tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu.
itaṉ viḷattamum (vivaramum), camayam matam eṉṉum irucollum teṉcollē eṉpatu aṟika.
mutaṟ kaṭalkōḷ
akkālattiṟ puyalālum pāṟiyālum kalaccētam aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntatāl, peruṅkaṭalik kaṭappatum kaṭantāl mīḷvatum aritāka viruntaṉa. neṭiyōṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉ, kaṭal kaṭantu kīḻttici nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu ēmamāka mīṇṭāṉ. kaṭal taṉakku utaviyāka irunta teṉṟu karuti, taṉ naṉṟi yaṟivik kāṭṭum mukamākak kaṭal teyvattiṟku oru viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉāṉ. avaṉiyē munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ (puṟam. 9) eṉṟu neṭṭimiyār kuṟittār.
iṉṉoru pāṇṭiyaṉ, kalappaṭi yamittuk kīḻtticit tīvoṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu, tāṉ kaṭalik kaṭantu viṭṭamiyāl ati veṉṟu taṉakku aṭippaṭuttiyatākak karuti, ataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka, kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa pāṟi yoṉṟil taṉ aṭic cuvaṭukaḷip poṟittu, avaṟṟik kaṭal taṉ aliyāl eṉṟum alacik kaḻuvumāṟu ceytāṉ. ataṉāl, vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
kaṭalukku añciṉa neṭiyōṉ vēṟu; ataṟku miñciṉa vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ vēṟu.
iṉṉumoru pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭaṉmēṟ cellumpōtu, kaṭal kontaḷittatu. atik kaṭal talivaṉiṉ viṉiyākak karuti, atiyaṭakka oru vēli viṭṭeṟintāṉ. kontaḷippu iyaṟki yāka aṭaṅkiṟṟu. āyiṉ, atu vēleṟintataṉ viḷiveṉṟē appāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ uḻiyarum karutiṉar. potumakkaḷ ataṉāl eṉṉa nērumō eṉṟu añciyiruttal vēṇṭum. ciṟitu kālattiṉpiṉ, avvaccattiṟ kēṟpavē, pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyik kaṭal koṇṭatu. atu ki.mu. 5000 pōl nikaḻntatākum. ataṉiyē, 
aṭiyil taṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇartti 
vaṭivēl eṟinta vāṉpaki poṟātu
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
eṉṟu (cilap. 11:17-20) iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟār.
muṉṉoru kālattut taṉatu perumiyiṉa taḷavi aracarkkuk kālāṉ mitit tuṇartti, vēlā ṉeṟinta antap paḻampaki yiṉik kaṭal poṟātu, piṉṉoru kālattu avaṉatu teṉṟicik kaṇṇatākiya paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉē, palavākiya pakkamalikaḷiyuṭiya kumarik kōṭṭiyum koṇṭataṉāl, vaṭaticik kaṇṇatākiya kaṅki yāṟṟiṉiyum imayamaliyiṉiyum kik koṇṭu āṇṭu, mīṇṭum teṉṟiciyi yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻvāṉāka.
..... kaṭal eṟintu koṇṭa elliyaḷavum vaṭapāl taṉatākki, mīṇṭum teṉṟiciyi yāṇṭaveṉa oppākkalu moṉṟu. aṭiyāluṇartti eṟinta paki poṟātu koḷḷat tāṉuṅ koṇṭu āṇṭa teṉṉava ṉeṉka.
eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār ippakutikku varintuḷḷa uriyi nōkkuka.
aṭiyil taṉṉaḷa varacark kuṇarttiyatu vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ ceyti. īraracarum oru kuṭiyiṉar eṉṉuṅ karuttāl, iruvar ceyalum oruvar ceyalākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa mutaṟkaṭalkōḷ ituvē. āppirikkāvum āttirēliyāvum kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu aṟavē pirintu neṭuntolivu nīṅki viṭṭaṉa. āppirikkā pirintata ṉāl arapikkaṭal tōṉṟiṟṟu. atu vaṅkak kaṭaliṉum muntit tōṉṟiyataṉālēyē. vāraṇaṉ mēṟṟicit talivaṉākak koḷḷappaṭṭāṉ.
paḻampāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉ perum pakuti mūḻkavē, talik kaḻakamum oḻintatu.
kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntavuṭaṉ, oru peruṅkūṭṭattār vaṭatici nōkkic ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭattārum taṉippaṭṭavarum vaṭamēṟkum vaṭakiḻakkum palvēṟu nāṭukaṭkuc ceṉṟu, kaṭalkōṭ ceytiyip parappiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. piṟ kālattil, annāṭukaḷ ovvoṉṟilum kaṭalkōḷ allatu perumaḻi veḷḷam nikaḻntatākak kati eḻuntirukkiṉṟatu.
alōracu (Alorus) eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya aracaṉ kālattil, pilittiyariṉ (Philistines) tākōṉ (Dagon) teyvattip pōl ari māntaṉum ari mīṉumāṉa (atāvatu, arikku (iṭuppiṟku) mēl māntaṉ vaṭivamum ataṟkukkīḻ mīṉ vaṭivamuṅ koṇṭa), oyaṉṉecu (Oannes) eṉṉum ōr uyiri pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭal (Persian Gulf) vaḻiyāka vantu, pāpilōṉiyarukkuk kalvi muḻuvatiyum nākarikak kalikaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum kaṟpittatākap pāpilōṉiya varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
avvōyaṉṉecu, mēṟkūṟiya mutaṟ kaṭalkōṭkuppiṉ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟa oru tamiḻaṟiñaṉākavē yiruttal vēṇṭum. avaṉukku arimīṉ vaṭivaṅ kaṭṭik kūṟiyataṟku avaṉ kaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṉṟatum, pāṇṭiyaṉukku mīṉak koṭiyum mīṉa muttiriyum paṟṟi mīṉavaṉ eṉṉum peyarirun tamiyumē, karaṇiyamākum.
tamiḻak kalavaṇikar mēlkaṭaṟkari yōramākavē karācci vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu, pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭaluḷḷum ceṅkaṭaluḷḷum pukuntu, mēliyāci yāvoṭum ekiptu nāṭṭoṭum vāṇikañ ceytu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu. irāmaccantira tīṭcitariṉ tamiḻar tōṟṟamum paravalum eṉṉum āṅkila nūlip pārkka.
ceyvaki
muti paḻaṅ kolli; iti putukkolli; puṉcey ciṟitu paṇpaṭṭa nilam; naṉcey naṉṟāyp paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭa nilam; puṟavu mulli nilam; kolli mulli nilattup puṉcey; puṉam kuṟiñci nilattup puṉcey; vāṉāvāri (māṉāmāri) maḻi peytu viḷiyum nilam.  (col. 72).
ceyappāṭṭu viṉi vikutikaḷ
paṭu eṉpatu viṉiyāl tuṉpuṟutaliyum; uṇ eṉpatu viṉip payaṉukarcciyiyum, peṟu eṉpatu viṉiyāṟ peṟum pēṟṟiyum, ciṟappāka vuṇarttum.  (col. 58).
ceyaṟkariyavi
maṉamaṭakkalum avāvaṟuttalumē ceyaṟkariyavi.  (ti.ma. 51).
ceruppu vaki
mitiyaṭi - orēyaṭiyuḷḷatu.
toṭutōl - iṭiyaṉ ceruppuppōlak kutiṅkāṟkum puṟaṅkāṟkum vārpūṭṭiyatu.
cappāttu - akaṉṟa aṭiyuḷḷatu.
cōṭu - tiraṇṭa aṭiyuḷḷatu.
ceruppu - aḷavāṉa akalamum tiraṭciyumuṭiya aṭiyuḷḷatu.
kuṟaṭu - kaṭṭiyāṟ ceytatu.  (col. 44)
cevvanti
cevvanti - cēvatī.
cevvanti = cevvanti nērattiṟ pūkkum pū. cevvanti -cevanti.  (va.va. 166).
cevvi
cevviyāvatu oruvarik kaṇṭu uriyāṭutaṟku ēṟṟa iṉiya maṉanili. cevviyāṉa nili cevvi.  (ti.ma.101).
ceviyuṟu
ceviyuṟu - cru. (i.vē.) = kēḷ, cevikkoḷ.
cru eṉṉum citicolliṉiṉṟē cruti (kēḷvi, maṟi), crōtriya, cravaṇa, crāvaṇa mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkum. (va.va. 166).
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻakarāti
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyiṉ ciṟanta nōkkaṅkaḷ
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarāti atit tokuttavariṉ tavaṟṟiṉāl mikac cīr keṭṭiruppiṉum, ataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarāl vakukkappaṭṭatiṉāl, mika mēmpaṭṭavi yāyuḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa :
1.	iv vakarāti, camayam, maruttuvam, kaṇiyam mutaliya paltuṟi yilakkiyaṅkaḷil varum kaliyiyaṟ coṟkaṭkum arukiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaṭkum kavaṉamākap poruḷ viḷakkiyum, neruṭāṉa coṟkaḷi virivāka ārāyntum, marapu neṟippaṭṭa tamiḻp pulavariṉ karuttiṟku ottatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
2.	itu, tamiḻukkum piṟa moḻikaṭkum iṭippaṭṭa moḻiyiya luṟavi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi,mēliyaṟiñariṉ ciṟanta karutti niṟivēṟṟu vatāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
3.	itu ulaka vaḻakkilum ilakkiya vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷi āṅkilac coṟkaḷiyum marapiyuṅ koṇṭu teḷivāka viḷakki, tamiḻ makkaḷuṭ perumpālārkkum āṅkilamaṟintōrkkum payaṉpaṭumāṟu, tullipamum niṟivum ikkālattiṟ kēṟṟatumāṉa akarātiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum
in nōkkaṅkaḷ tammaḷavil taliciṟantaṉavāyiṉum, ivaṟṟi niṟivēṟṟum nilimi ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattil iruntatilli. ataṉālēyē piṉvarum kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷ nērntuḷḷaṉa.



	I. colvaḻukkaḷ
1. colliṉmi
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akarātiyiṉ mupperuṅkuṟikaḷuḷ, mutalatu palvakic colliṉmiyākum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu.

2. colliṉ maṟuvaṭiviṉmi
cila coṟkaḷiṉ maṟuvaṭivam ceṉṉi yakarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭa villi.

3. colliṉ ilakkaṇa vaṭiviṉmi
(1) ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉi
kuṟikkappaṭṭa ceyappaṭu poruḷ    viṭappaṭṭa ceyappaṭuporuḷ
kuṉṟāviṉi vaṭivam		kuṉṟiya viṉivaṭivam
uṇattu				uṇa
uṇattal kāytal		uṇattutal kāyavittal
ucumpu, ucuppu eṉṉum iraṇṭum, muṟiyē, oru poruḷ paṟṟiya taṉ viṉiyum piṟaviṉiyumākum. ucuppu eṉpataṟku eḻupputal eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, ucumpu eṉṉuñ coṟku tuyiluṇartal allatu tuyiluṇarat toṭaṅkutal eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
(2) nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccam
cila viṉikaḷiṉ nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccavaṭivam marapu viṉi yeccamāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. atu taṉiyāka eṭuttuk kūṟap paṭavilli.
e-ṭu:
aṇṇākka, mēlāka.
aṇṇākkak kuṭittāṉ, mēlāka eṭuttāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakku kaḷi nōkkuka.
4. kūṭṭuccolliṉ uṟuppit taṉiyākak kūṟāmi
aṇṭitaḷḷuki, aṇṭimāṅkoṭṭi, ekirk koḻuppu, toṇṇittaṭi eṉṉum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷ akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, aṇṭi (anus), aṇṭimā (muntiri), ekir (araṇi), toṇṇi (parumaṉ) eṉṉum taṉiccoṟkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
5. mikipaṭu coṟkaḷ (Redundant Words)
cilacoṟkaḷ mikipaṭu coṟkaḷāyiṉum, ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkattāṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiyum akarātiyil kuṟittal takkatē. āyiṉ, avaṟṟuṭ pala kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
e-ṭu:
ariñāṇ kayiṟu
ari ñāṇ koṭi
āṇpiḷḷip piḷḷi
peṇpiḷḷip piḷḷi
ivi, muṟiyē, araṇākkayiṟu (aṇṇākkayiṟu), araṇāk koṭi, āmpiḷappiḷḷi, pompiḷappiḷḷi eṉak kocci vaṭivil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
iv vaṭivik kuṟittal kūṭātu. ivaṟṟiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivik kuṟikkalām. cāval (cēval), vēṅku (vāṅku) mutaliya kocci vaṭivukaḷi vēṇṭātu kuṟittirukkum pōtu, mikipaṭu coṟkaḷiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivi ēṉ kuṟittal kūṭātu?
	6. viḷakka mēṟkōḷiṉ varuñcollik kuṟiyāmi
cila coṟkaḷ viḷakkamēṟkōḷil vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, avaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittu akarātic colvariciyil cērttilar.
e-ṭu:uṭumpōṭi - uṭumpu ōṭiṉatiṉāl ākāteṉṟu taḷḷiya nilam.
kuṟittuvarukiḷavi - puṇarmoḻiyil varumoḻi.
ciṟiya tiruvaṭi - aṉumāṉ.
āmitavaḻi eṉṉuñ colli viḷakkum mēṟkōḷ uṭum pōṭi āmitavaḻi puṟṟum eṉpatu. niṟutta col eṉpati viḷakka vanta mēṟkōḷ niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi eṉṟu eṉpatu. periya tiruvaṭi eṉpati, ciṟiya tiruvaṭiyiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭuttappaṭṭa teṉṟu āṅkilattiṟ kuṟittuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, mēṟkuṟitta muccollum viḷakkamēṟkōḷil vantiruppiṉum, avaṟṟi avaṟṟōṭotta allatu avaṟṟiṟ ketirāṉa coṟkaḷip pōl, colvariciyiṟ cērkkavilli.
	7. colliṉ kocci vaṭivic colvariciyiṟ cērttal
(1) kocci vaṭivumaṭṭum
cila coṟkaḷiṉ kocci vaṭiviyē col variciyiṟ cērttu. avaṟṟiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivi mūlak kuṟippil maṭṭum perum pālum arikuṟiyāyk kāṭṭiviṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e-ṭu:
kuṟitta col			kāṭṭiya mūlam
aṇṇāk kayiṟu		arināṇ +
appappō			avvappōtu
aruṇākkayiṟu		arināṇ +
āmakkaṉ			āṇmakaṉ
ippavum			ippōtum
kaṭiccavāytaṭiccāṉ	kaṭi +
cōttāṉ			cōṟu +
piṭiccarāvi			piṭi + arāvu
poṭṭacci			peṭṭi
iṉi, arṇāḷ eṉṉum kocci vaṭivik kuṟittu arināṇ eṉṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭi, arṇāṭkoṭi, arṇāṭ kayiṟu, eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantiruppatu akarātikku mikamika iḻukkuttaruvatākum. (Vol. I. g. 113).
(2) koccivaṭivum tiruttiya vaṭivum
tiruntiya vaṭivu 		kocci vaṭivu
akappi				āppi
añcaṟippeṭṭi		añcalappeṭṭi, añcārappeṭṭi

kaṭittēṟu		kaṭattēṟu

nirampa			rempa, rompa
paṭṭaṟi			paṭṭari
peṭṭi			poṭṭi

iṅṅaṉam, tiruntiya vaṭivip pōṉṟē kocci vaṭiviyum akarātiyiṟ cērttup poruḷ kūṟiyiruppiṉ, māṇavar eṅṅaṉam tiruntamuṭiyum? tiruttamā yeḻutum māṇavariyum kocciyāy eḻutumpaṭiyaṉṟō iv vaṭivukaḷ tūṇṭukiṉṟaṉa!
iṉi, varaccūli, varaṭci, varaṭcuṇṭi, varaṭkuli, varaṭcori, varaṭṭippāṉ varaṭṭi vāṭṭutal, varaṭṭu, varaṭṭuc cōki, varaṭṭuppacu, varaḷ, varaḷvāyu eṉa ṟakaram varavēṇṭiya coṟkaḷiyellām oḻuṅkāy rakaram vittu kuṟittiruppatu, ettuṇik kuṟumputtaṉamum tamiḻik keṭukkum cūḻcciyum ākum!

	8. colliṉ vaḻuvaṭivik kuṟittal
kuṟikkappaṭṭa vaḻuvaṭivam	kuṟikkappaṭāta tiruntiya vaṭivam
arivāḷmaṇippūṇṭu	arivāḷmuṉippūṇṭu
āmavaṭi			āmivaṭi
āḷvaḷḷi			āḻvaḷḷi
kiḻiyañcaṭṭi			kiḻiyañciṭṭi
nācamaṟṟuppōvāṉ		nācamuṟṟuppōvāṉ
pāḷiyarivāḷ			pāḷiyaṟuvāḷ
poṭṭikkāṭu		poṭṭaṟkāṭu
potu niṟam			putu niṟam
vīccarivāḷ			vīccaṟuvāḷ
veṭṭarivāḷ		veṭṭaṟuvāḷ
arivāḷ muṉippūṇṭeṉpatu, maṇiyarivāḷiṉ muṉiyi luḷḷa tēṅkāy tirukipōl, ōrattiṟ palvaṭivāṉa muṉi yuḷḷa vaṭṭa iliyuṭiya pūṇṭu. arivāḷmaṇi yeṉpatu arivāḷip patittirukkuṅ kaṭṭi. atip pōṉṟa pūṇ ṭeṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatu.
āmivaṭiyeṉpatu, uḻuntuvaṭi pōl taṭṭiyāyirātu āmi yōṭṭippōl veḷivaḷivāka vuḷḷatu. āmit tāli, āmippūṭṭu, āmimaṭi, āmiyāḻ mutaliya coṟ kaḷiyum avi kuṟikkum poruḷkaḷiṉ vaṭiviyum nōkkuka.
caṭṭi eṉpatu periyatu, ciṭṭi ciṟiyatu. akalukkuc ciṭṭiyeṉṉum peyarē poruntum. nelli māvaṭṭattilum kiḻiyañciṭṭi yeṉṟē colvar.
arivāḷ eṉpatu kāykaṟikaḷic ciṟitāy ariyum katti allatu maṇiyalaku. aṟuvāḷ eṉpatu peritāy aṟukkum allatu veṭṭum katti vaki. paṉṉaṟuvāḷ, pāṉiyaṟuvāḷ, vīccaṟuvāḷ, veṭṭaṟuvāḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟuvāḷ eṉṟu colvatē vaḻakkam. arivāḷ eṉṟu colvār oruvarumilli. katiraṟuppellām aṟuppu aṟuvaṭiyeṉṟē collappaṭum. aritāḷ eṉṉum kūṭṭuccollil maṭṭum, ari eṉṉum col vantuḷatu. atu nel, pul, tiṉi, varaku, cāmi pōṉṟa ciṟu payirkaḷiṉ tāḷiyē kuṟippatu. nākkariyun tayamukaṉār eṉṟu kampar kūṟiyatu ceyyuḷ vaḻakku. aṟuvāḷ eṉṉum col taṉittēṉum piṟa colloṭu cērntēṉum akarātiyil ōriṭattilum varātiruppatu, mika viyappāyirukkiṉṟatu.
kampar ceyyuḷoṉṟil, yāḻ, vāḻ, pāḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷuk kēṟpa, nāḷ eṉpatu nāḻ eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. atu ulakavaḻak kiṟ kēṟkātu. ceyyuḷilum nāḷ eṉṟiruppiṉ kuṟṟamaṉṟu. nāḻ eṉpatu ēṭṭuppiḻiyākavu mirukkalām. ātalāl, av vaṭivatti akarātiyiṟ kuṟittiruppatu vaḻuvām.
9. kuṟikkavēṇṭāta collik kuṟittal
kīḻvaruñ coṟkaḷiyum avi pōṉṟavaṟṟiyum akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkat tēviyilli.
aṇiyiḻi - peṇ.
aḻāal - aḻuki.
attattāveṉal - tantiyi appappā eṉṟaḻittal.
mukkuḻiccaṭṭi - mūṉṟu kuḻiyuḷḷa paṇiyāraccaṭṭi.
aṇiyiḻi eṉpatu ōr aṉmoḻittoki. iṅṅaṉam nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉavi uḷa.
aḻal allatu aḻāl eṉpataṉ aḷapeṭi vaṭivē aḻāal eṉpatu.
oṟṟikkuḻiccaṭṭi, nāṟkuḻiccaṭṭi, aiṅkuḻiccaṭṭi, aṟukuḻic caṭṭi, ēḻkuḻiccaṭṭi eṉap paṉṉirukuḻiccaṭṭivari yiruppatāl, mukkuḻiccaṭṭiyi vitantu kuṟikka vēṇṭiyatilli.

periyōr vāyilum nuḻiyāta raktiṣṭīli, cannipātacuram, pariyatu yōcciyō pēkṣaṇam, jahata jahalla kṣaṉi, paṭicajapākusuma niyāyam eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷi, enta vakiyil akarātiyiṟ cērttārkaḷō teriyavilli. ivaṟṟi yellām camaṟkiruta akarātiyi laṉṟit tamiḻakarātiyiṟ kuṟittal kūṭātu.
(3) īreḻuttilum vēṟṟuccol (camaṟkirutam)

iṅṅaṉam, pala vēṟṟuc coṟkaḷ palavaṭiviṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻ mikunta colvaḷamuḷḷa moḻiyātalāl, ataṟku mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟkaḷ tēviyēyilli. tēviyillātu piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koḷvatāl, tamiḻ taṉ tūymiyum ciṟappōciyum iḻantu kalavi moḻiyā vatuṭaṉ, nāḷaṭivil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ pōl āriya vaṇṇamāy māṟiviṭum. vēṟṟuccoṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkum pōtu, taṉ coṟkaḷ vaḻakkaṟṟup poruḷiḻappatuṭaṉ, nāḷaṭivil maṟintu pōy mīḷānili yaṭikiṉṟaṉa.
kīḻvarum vēṟṟuccoṟkaḷ pōṉṟavi tamiḻukku muṟṟum vēṇṭātavi.

tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamiyāta vēṟṟuccollāyiṉ, tamiḻil moḻi peyarttē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum. im muṟiyik kaṭippiṭittē, karumpu, miḷakāy, uruḷi(kkiḻaṅku), pukiyili, vāṉkōḻi, mitivaṇṭi, vittūṟṟi mutaliya coṟkaḷip potumakkaḷ puṉintirukkiṉṟaṉar.
moḻipeyarkka muṭiyāta ciṟappuppeyarāyiṉ, tamiḻil eḻuttup peyarttē vaḻaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻutteṭu puṇarnta collā kummē.  (884)
citintaṉa variṉum iyintaṉa variyār  (885)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar akkālattiṟkēṟpac curukkik kūṟiṉār.
naṉṉūlāciriyarāṉa pavaṇantiyārō, evvev vaṭaveḻutti evvevviṭattil evvet tamiḻeḻuttāyt tirittal vēṇṭu meṉpatip paṟṟi,
ēḻāmuyir iyyum iruvumi varukkat
tiṭiyiṉ mūṉṟum avvam mutalum
eṭṭē yavvum muppatu cayavum
mēloṉṟu caṭavum iraṇṭu catavum
mūṉṟē akavum aintiru kavvum
āvī ṟiyum īyī ṟikaramum  (147)
ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
lavviṟ kimmuta liraṇṭum yavviṟ
kiyyum moḻimuta lākimuṉ varumē.  (148)
iṇintiyal kāli yaralak kikaramum
mavvak kukaramum nakarak kakaramum
micivarum ravvaḻi uvvu māmpiṟa.  (149)
eṉṟu viḷakkamākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. innūṟpākkaḷ (cūttiraṅkaḷ) vaṭaveḻuttukaḷi māṟṟum muṟiyic coṉṉaviyēyaṉṟi, vaṭacoṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koḷḷumāṟu tūṇṭiyaviyalla.
tamiḻil ellāveḻuttukaḷum, piṟa moḻikaḷiṟpōl, collil mūviṭattum varuvaṉavalla. mutaṉili yeḻuttukaḷum iṭinili yeḻuttukaḷum iṟutinili yeḻuttukaḷum, iṉṉiṉṉaveṉṟu ilakkaṇattil variyaṟukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvarampumīṟi nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa ayaṟcoṟkaḷ akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. rakara uyirmeykaḷuḷ ri tavira maṟṟap patiṉoṉṟum, lakara uyirmeykaḷuḷ li tavira maṟṟap pati ṉoṉṟum, ṭakara vuyirmeykaḷuḷ ṭu tavira maṟṟap patiṉoṉ ṟum, moḻimutaleḻuttukaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itu tamiḻilakkaṇatti, aṭiyōṭu oḻittalaṉṟi veṟetaṉi nōkkamākak koṇṭiruttal kūṭum?
e-ṭu:	mutaṉili	iṭinili	iṟutinili
		ṭamḷar	kāptāri	cani
		trapsam	pālyaṉ	rap
		rahmat	raktam	rayat
		vōrakam	ratṉam	ṣaṭ
		talam	jihvā	ṣapāṣ
mukamatiya maṉṉarāṭciyil urutuc coṟkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉiṉ, avaṟṟit taṉiyākat tokuttu veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭumē yaṉṟi, tamiḻc coṟkaḷoṭu kalattal kūṭātu. ceṉṉi yakarātiyil avaṟṟiyellāñ cērttatu. vēṇṭu meṉṟu tamiḻiṉ tūymiyik kulittaṟkēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. ākavē, ataṟkut teṉṉintiya akarāti (South Indian Lexicon) eṉṉum peyarē poruttamām.

13. eḻuttukkūṭṭal vaḻu
cila coṟkaḷ tavaṟāka eḻuttuk kūṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: kuḻicīli, māṟāppu.
ivi; muṟiyē kuḷicīli, mārāppu eṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
Tumbler (kuṭinīrkkuvaḷi) eṉṉum āṅkilaccol, ṭampaḷar, tamiḷar, tamiḻar eṉṉum mūvaṭiviṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻar (tamiḻmakkaḷ) eṉṉum iṉappeyarāṉa tamiḻccolliyē potuvākat tamiḷar eṉṟu makkaḷ tavaṟāyp palukkum pōtu, tamiḷar (kuvaḷi) eṉṉum kalap peyarāṉa āṅkilac colliyā tamiḻar eṉṟu ciṟappuḻakaraṅ koṭuttut tavaṟiṉṟip palukkuvar! itu ettuṉik kuṟumputtaṉamāṉa kuṟippu!
14. āṅkilattil eḻuttuppeyarppu muṟittavaṟu
tamiḻ valliṉameykaḷ, tamiḻiluḷḷa piṟa meykaḷi nōkka, valliṉamēyaṉṟi, vaṭamoḻi valliṉam pōla attuṇi vallōci yuṭiyaṉavalla. tamiḻil iru kakaram cērntāl tāṉ vaṭamoḻiyil allatu āṅkilattil oru kakarattiṟkuc camamākum.
e-ṭu: tēkku - teak.
tamiḻ valliṉam melliṉattiṟku muṉvariṉ, eṭuppoli peṟum. iti nōkkātu, vaṭamoḻi valliṉavolivaki nāṉkaṉuḷ mutalvakiyeṉṟē karutikkoṇṭu, tamiḻ valliṉa meykaḷi āṅkilattil eḻuttup peyarttiruppatu, tamiḻukku māṟāṉatum ayalnāṭṭārkkut tavaṟāṉa vaḻi kāṭṭuvatu mākum.
e-ṭu:
tamiḻccol	āṅkilattil eḻuttup  	irukkavēṇṭiya
	peyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷa muṟi  	muṟi
aṅku	anku		angu
añcu	anchu		anju
aṇṭu	antu		andu
antu	anthu		andu
ampu	ampu		ambu
ṅkka, ñcca, ṇṭṭa, ntta, mppa, eṉṉum vaṉkūṭṭolikaḷ teluṅki laṉṟit tamiḻililli.
II. poruḷ vaḻukkaḷ
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiṉ iraṇṭām peruṅkuṟi, colliṟkut tarappaṭṭiṟkum poruḷiṉ vaḻuvām.
1. iyalviḷakkam allatu coṟporuḷ tamiḻiṟ kūṟappeṟāmi.
akaccuṭṭu eṉṉum colliṟku, āṅkila viḷakkattiluḷḷa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷittavira, tamiḻil viḷakkamāvatu poruḷāvatu kūṟappaṭavilli. ākavē, āṅkilam aṟiyātārkku ittakiya viḷakkam payaṉaṟṟatām. acākkirati yeṉṉum coṟku muṟṟilum āṅkilattilēyē poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. añcuruvāṇi yeṉṉum coṟku āṅkilattil viḷakkam kūṟi, tamiḻil accāṇi eṉṟa col maṭṭuṅ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷatu.

3. mikipaṭak kūṟal
karittal eṉṉum colliṟku, “to be saltish to the taste; uppuc cuvi mikutal, intak kaṟi uppukkarikkiṟatu eṉṟu poruḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭum tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
karittal eṉṉum viṉiccolliṟku, mikutal eṉpatu tāṉ poruḷē yaṉṟi uppukkarittal eṉpataṉṟu. uppukkarittal eṉpatē poruḷāyiṉ, eṭuttukkāṭṭu, intak kaṟi karikkiṉṟatu eṉṟaṉṟō iruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa miruppiṉ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭuviṭum. uppu eṉṉum colloṭu kūṭiṉālaṉṟi, karittal eṉṉum colliṟku uppuccuvip poruḷtarum āṟṟalilli. uppukkarittal eṉṉum marapuviṉi, taṉiyākavum akarātiyil ataṟkuriya iṭattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭu, uvarppu mikutal eṉṉum poruḷ kūṟuppaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
4. vaḻuppaṭak kūṟal
aḷimaṟipāppu eṉṉum coṟku.
“A mode of construing in which the expression at the end of a verse is conjoined with a word in the middle of a verse, or with one in the beginning of another verse, one of eight porulkol; pāṭṭiṉ īṟṟiṉiṉṟa col iṭiyilum mutaliluñ ceṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷap paṭum muṟi.
eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu tāppicip poruḷ kōḷukku allatu viḷakkaṇikku oruvāṟu poruntumēyaṉṟi, aḷimaṟiyāppup poruḷkōḷukkup poruntavē porun tātu.
aḷimaṟiyāppup poruḷ kōḷāvatu, tali muṉṉum vāl piṉṉumāka vaḷikkuṭ pukunta pāmpu, aṅṅaṉamē tirumpātu tirumpavum tali muṉṉāka vaḷintu māṟik koḷvatu pōl, oru ceyyuḷ talikīḻāy aṭiyaṭiyāka veṭuttup poruḷ koḷḷappaṭuvatu.
cūḻnta viṉiyākki cuṭaviḷintu nāṟkatiyiṟ cuḻalvār tāmum
mūḻnta piṇinaliya muṉceyta viṉiyeṉṟē muṉivārtāmum
tāḻnta vuṇarviṉarāyt tāḷuṭintu taṇṭūṉṟit taḷarvār tāmum
vāḻnta poḻutiṉē vāṉeytu neṟimuṉṉi muyalā tārē.
icceyyuḷil, 4ām aṭi mutalaṭiyākavum, 3 ām aṭi 2 ām aṭiyākavum, 2ām aṭi 3ām aṭiyākavum, mutalaṭi 4 ām aṭiyākavum, māṟiyamintu poruḷ paṭutal kāṇka.
īrṅki eṉṉum colliṟku,
“Wet hand, fig. hand that has been washed after taking one’s meal; uṇṭu pūciyaki. īrṅki vitirār kayavar (kuṟaḷ. 1077)
eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. īrṅki yeṉpatu ecciṟkiyē yaṉṟi uṇṭu pūciya kiyaṉṟu. ecciṟkiyiṟ kākki viraṭṭātavaṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum nōkkuka.
kacakarṇam eṉṉum coṟku.
1. Lit. elephants’ ear, term used to denote the art of moving or waving one’s ears in imitation of the elephants; kātāṭṭum vitti. 2 A task involving stupendous effort; perumuyaṟciyāl āka vēṇṭiya kāriyam. eṉṟum, ataṉi aṭuttuḷḷa kacakarṇam pōṭutal eṉṉum marapu viṉikku.
“> id. +. To put forth unusual or extrordinary effort to realize an object; perumuyaṟci ceytal. avaṉ anta vēliyip peṟac kacakarṇam pōṭṭāṉ eṉṟum viḷakkaṅ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itil, karṇam eṉpatu karaṇam (somersult); kātaṉṟu. kacakarṇam pōṭutalāvatu, yāṉi karaṇam pōṭṭāṟpōl arum perumuyaṟci ceytal. pōṭutal eṉṉum viṉi karaṇattiṟ kaṉṟik kātiṟkup poruntāmi, nōkkuka. yāṉi kātāṭṭuvatu pōl oruvaṉ kātāṭṭi eṉṉa karumatti niṟivēṟṟa muṭiyum? itu ettuṇi nakippiṟkiṭamāṉa ceyti!
poruḷ vaḻukkaḷ akarāti muḻuvatum āṅkāṅku palavuḷa.

vaḷḷi (puṟam. 63) 	vaḷiyal 	koṭi, taṇṭu.
vaḷḷi eṉpatu koṭi; vaḷintatu eṉṉum poruḷatu. oppu nōkka koṭu-koṭi. koṭumi = vaḷivu. āmpal vaḷḷit toṭikki makaḷir eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭiṟku, āmpaṟṟaṇ ṭāṟ ceyta vaḷiyaṇinta kiyiṉiyuṭiya makaḷir eṉṟu paḻiyavuriyāciriyar urittiruttal kāṇka. toṭi eṉpatē vaḷiyal. itik kavaṉiyāta ceṉṉi yakarātit tokup pāḷar, vaḷḷiyeṉṉuñ coṟku vaḷiyal eṉṟu poruḷ kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
varāl eṉṉum coṟku, keṇṭi yeṉṟum poruḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. varālmīṉ vēṟu; keṇṭimīṉ vēṟu.
iṉi, varāl 3 aṭiyum 4 aṭiyum vaḷarum eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu viyappiyum nakippiyum viḷikkiṉṟatu.
tāṉē tarakkoḷi ṉaṉṟit taṉpāl
mēlik koḷakkoṭā viṭattatu maṭaṟpaṉi  (naṉ. 33)
eṉṟu, māṇavar virumpumpōtu oṉṟic collāmal tāmāka virumpum pōtu atic collum āciriyarkku, tāṉākak kāṟṟaṭittu viḻuntālaṉṟi oruvaṉ virumpumpōtu ēṟip paḻam paṟittuk koḷḷa muṭiyāta karukku maṭṭiyaṟuk kāta peṇ paṉiyit teḷivāka uvami kūṟiyirukkavum, ati āṇpaṉi yeṉṟu nūṟpāviyum kāṭṭik kuṟippatu ettuṇip poṟup paṟṟa ceyalākum.

6. poruḷ variciyiṉmi
coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ varici, (1) coṟpiṟappiyal muṟi (Etymo-logical order), (2) varalāṟṟu muṟi allatu kālamuṟi, (Historical or Chronological order) (3) ēraṇa muṟi (Logical order) eṉa mūvakippaṭum. paṇṭi yilakkiyam aḻiyāta (camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa) moḻikaṭkum, (inti pōṉṟa) putiya moḻikaṭkum tāṉ, coṟpiṟappiyal muṟiyiyum varalāṟṟu muṟiyiyum kiyāḷamuṭiyum toṉmutu paḻaṅkālattil tōṉṟiyatum paṇṭi yilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻintu pōṉatumāṉa tamiḻukkō, ērāṇa muṟiyittāṉ kiyāḷa muṭiyum.
kaḷittal eṉṉum colliṟkuriya nāṟporuḷkaḷ ceṉṉi yakara mutaliṟ kīḻvarum variciyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
(1) makiḻvaṭital
(2) kaḷḷip paruki veṟi koḷḷutal
(3) matamuṭiyatātal.
(4) cerukkuṟutal.
kaḷi eṉṉum viṉi. kaḷ eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟik kaṭkuṭit taliyē uṇarttuvatāl mēṟkāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa nāṟporuḷum piṉ varumāṟu māṟiyamital vēṇṭum.
1. kaḷḷip paruti veṟi koḷḷutal
2. matamuṭiyatātal.

4. cerukkuṟutal.

7. vaṭamoḻiyiṟ poruḷ kūṟal
e-ṭu: āmeṉ ... Verily, sol be it, used by Christians usu. at the close of prayer or hymn; tatātu.

8. oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭāmi
ovvoru cemmoḻiyilum oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷ nirampavuḷa. avi parupporuḷil oṉṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum nuṇ poruḷil vēṟupaṭṭavi. colvaḷattiṟ ciṟanta tamiḻ oruporuṭ palacoṟkaḷilum ciṟantuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ nuṇporuḷ vēṟupāṭṭic ceṉṉi yakarāti potuvāy eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatilli. collutalippaṟṟip pala coṟkaḷ tamiḻil uḷa. avaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟiṉ ciṟappupporuḷi akarāti eṭuttukkūṟavilli.
