muṭṭuk kurumpi = iḷaṅ kurumpi.
moṭṭip piyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ, iḷiñaṉ.
muḷ - maḷ - maḻa - maḻavu = iḷami.
maḷ - maṇ - maṇi = ciṟiyatu, maṇikkāṭi, maṇikkākki, maṇikkuṭal, maṇippayaṟu, maṇippuṟā mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
maṇi - māṇi = ciṟuvaṉ, paḷḷic ciṟuvaṉ, maṇamākā iḷiñaṉ.
maṇi4 = oḷiyuḷḷatu.
maṇṇutal = kaḻuvutal, miṉukkutal.
maṇṇuṟu maṇiyiṉ  (puṟam. 147)
maṇ - maṇṇi - maṇi = oḷikkal.
11. coṟkaḷic cevviyāyp pirittal
uṭakkeṭuttup pōtal = uṭakku + eṭuttup pōtal (kūṭātal, tōlum elumpumātal, mika melital). iti uṭal keṭuttup pōtal eṉṟu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka akaramutali pirittiruppatu tavaṟām.
uṭaṅku - uṭakku = uṭampukkūṭu.
12. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpaṟital
ovvoru poruḷiṉ (pārttamaṭṭiṟ kaṇṇik kavarum) ciṟappiyalpi yum uṟṟunōkkiyē, atataṟkuriya peyari iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar muṉṉit tamiḻar.
e-ṭu: kākā(kākkā) eṉṟu karivatu kākam(kākki). mūṉṟu aṇikaḷi (varikaḷi) mutukil uṭiyatu aṇil. irumi (karumi) yāṉa māṭṭiṉam erumi. vaḻuvaḻuveṉṟirukkum maram vāḻi.
muṭivēynta pēraracaṉ vēntaṉ. vēytal = muṭiyaṇital. vēyntōṉ - vēntaṉ. koṉṟi vēyntāṉi (civaṉik) koṉṟi vēntaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya mukkuṭiyaracarkkē muṭiyaṇiyum urimi mutaṟkālattiliruntatu.
13. poruḷ variciyaṟital
e-ṭu: arukutal = ciṟuttal, oṭuṅkutal, aru - ari= ciṟutta allatu oṭuṅkiya iṭi. uṭampiṉ naṭu. pāti, kōl - kāl = kampam. tūṇ, tūṇpōl uṭampit tāṅkum uṟuppu. uṭampiṉ kāṟpakuti, kāṟpakuti (nāliloṉṟu).
uṭampiṉ pātiyaḷavāyiruppatu ariyeṉṟum, nāliloru pakutiyā yiruppatu kāleṉṟum, talikīḻāyp poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvatu uṇmikku nērmāṟām.
14. oppumiyamippu (Analogy) aṟital
e-ṭu: kuḻal - kuṭal. puḻal - puṭal - puṭali - ka. paṭala. te. poṭla. va. paṭṭōlikkā, kaṭi - kaṭali, viṭi - viṭali - L. vitula.
mi - mayir, ti - tayir, tittal kuttutal, muṭkuttiṉāl muḷ tittatu eṉpar. pāliṟ pirimōr ūṟṟutalip pirikuttutal eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. piri tittatu tayir - va. tati (dadhi), i. tahī.
15. varalāṟṟaṟiviyum ñālanūlaṟiviyum tuṇikkōṭal
e-ṭu: pāṇṭavar tōṉṟu muṉṉarē pāṇṭiyar teṉṉāṭṭi āṇṭu vantaṉar. pāṇṭavaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa aruccuṉaṉ tiru nīrāṭat teṉṉāṭu vantu. cittirāṅkataṉ eṉṟu pārataṅ kūṟum oru pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷi maṇantāṉ. āriyar intiyāviṟkuḷ aṭi vikku muṉṉarē, pāṇṭiyar varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmiyiliruntu kaṇakkil kālam teṉṟamiḻ nāṭṭi āṇṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. tirukkuṟaḷukku āriya aṭippaṭiyil uri varinta parimēlaḻakarum. paḻaṅkuṭikkup paṭippuk kālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭuvarum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar kuṭikaḷ eṉṟu mūvēntar kuṭikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉar. iṅṅaṉa miruntum, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar pāṇṭya (Pandya) eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu tirintatākac ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali neñcārap poykkiṉṟatu. iti ūḻiyiṟutic cūṟāvaḷipōṟ cuḻaṟṟi yeṟivatu kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaka varalāṟē. pāṇṭi = kāḷi, maṟavaṉ, pāṇṭi - pāṇṭiyaṉ.
cāmi paṭippukkālan toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭil viḷintu varum toṇ (oṉpāṉ) kūlaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākum. iṉṟum atu nāṭṭuppuṟa uḻavarkku uriya oḻuṅkāṉa uṇavu vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, ataṉ peyar cyāmā eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripāka, mēṟkuṟitta poykkaḷañciyam tuṇintu kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉi veṭṭi vīḻppatu ñāla nūlaṟivē.
cāmip payirpōl oru pullum uṇṭu. atu cāmippul eṉappaṭum. cāmi eṉṉum teṉcolliyē vaṭamoḻiyāḷar cyāmā eṉṟu tirittut tamiḻari ēmāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.
16. iṭukuṟi tamiḻil illiyeṉ ṟuṇartal
tamiḻtāṉē tōṉṟiya iyaṉmoḻi yātaliṉ, atiluḷḷa ellāc coṟkaḷum karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷē. cilavaṟṟiṉ vērp poruḷ pārttamaṭṭil teriyum.
e-ṭu: cuṭali < cuṭal < cuṭu < cuḷ.
cilavaṟṟiṉ vērpporuḷ āḻntu āyntālaṉṟit tōṉṟā.
e-ṭu: viṉi < viḷi. iv vuṇmikaḷiyē.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. 640)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol. 877)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kuṟittār.
naṉṉūluriyāciriyar iṭukuṟiyākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷellām karaṇiyak kuṟikaḷē. palā = parutta paḻatti yuṭiyatu. paṉi = kūriya palpōṉṟa karukkumaṭṭi yuḷḷatu. poṉ = poṟpu allatu polivuḷḷatu. maram = marattatu allatu uṇarcciyaṟṟatu pōṉṟiruppatu. mā = koṭṭikkuḷ vaṇṭuḷḷatu.
āriya moḻikaḷ tiripuṭi moḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟ pala coṟkaḷ vērpporuḷ tōṉṟāvāṟu tirintiruppatuṭaṉ, pala iṭukuṟic coṟkaḷum, ciṟappākac camaṟkirutattil amin tuḷḷaṉa. ataṉāl camaṟkirutatti aṭippaṭiyāka vit tāynta mēli moḻinūlāciriyar, ellā moḻikaḷum iṭu kuṟit tokutikaḷē eṉpati aṭippaṭi neṟimuṟiyākak koṇṭa vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) eṉṉum vaḻuppaṭṭa moḻiyiyal vakiyi ulakamuḻutum parappi varukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmiyāṉa varalāṟṟu moḻiyārāyc ciyāl camaṟkiruta iḻivum āriya ēmāṟṟamum ulakiṟku veḷip paṭṭuviṭumē yeṉṟu añcum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, putitāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷa vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl tamakkoru kēṭakamā yiruppatu kaṇṭu, ataṉi neñcāra niṉintu vāyāra vāḻtti vaḷarttu varukiṉṟaṉar. avaraṭiyārāṉa koṇṭāṉmārum tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭuppataṟku itu taliciṟanta vaḻiyā yiruppataṟintu, itaṉit talimēl tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
oru poruḷi eṅkeṅkun tēṭiyum kāṇamuṭiyāteṉṟu muḻu nampikki koṇṭavaṉukku, eṅṅaṉ tēṭaṉ muyaṟci piṟakkum? ellāc collum iṭukuṟikaḷē yeṉṟum, ellā moḻi kaḷum āyiram āṇṭiṟkorumuṟi aṭiyōṭu māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum nampukiṉṟavaṉukku eṅṅaṉ moḻiyārāycci vēṭki eḻum? ātalāl, vaṇṇaṉi moḻinūl aṟivu vaḷarccikku māperu muṭṭukkaṭṭiyeṉṟu aṟavē puṟakkaṇikka.
17. pakuttaṟivip payaṉpaṭuttal
e-ṭu: vaṭavi = vaṭamuṉiyil tōṉṟum oruvaki neruppu allatu oḷi. vaṭam - vaṭavi (piṅ) = vaṭavaṉal. vaṭavik kaṉali vittūti (antakak kavi vīrarākavar taṉippāṭal). 
vaṭam - vaṭanti = vaṭakāṟṟu. vaṭavit tī.
cuṭarnteri vaṭantit tīyum  (kāñcippu. irupat. 384)
akkaṭaliṉmītu vaṭavaṉal niṟka viliyō  (tāyumāṉavar)
o.nō: Aurora Borealis = Northern Light.
iṅṅaṉam vaṭavi eṉpatu teṉcolleṉṟum vaṭamuṉi neruppiṉ peyareṉṟum teḷivāyirukkavum, ati vaṭavā eṉṟu tirittup peṇkutiri eṉṟu poruḷurippar vaṭavar, ittakiya colliyalkaḷiṟ pakuttaṟivip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
18. añcāmi
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷi vaṭavar kaṭaṉkoṇṭirup patāl, avaṟṟiyellām teṉcolleṉṟu oppukkoḷḷiṉ, camaṟkirutattiṟkut tamiḻē mūlam eṉpatu veṭṭaveḷiyām. ataṉāl, ati maṟittaṟkup pala coṟkaṭkup poruntap pukalalākavum poruntāp poyttalākavum poruḷ kūṟiyum, cilavaṟṟi iṭukuṟiyeṉṟu muttiriyiṭṭum varukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟi maṟuttu uṇmi kūṟupavarkkup patavi allatu aluval pōyviṭum eṉṉum accam tamiḻar uḷḷattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruppatāl, vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟ kaṭku vērkāṇat taṉimaṟam vēṇṭuvatu orutali.
19. naṭunili
kāytal uvattal akaṟṟi oruporuṭkaṇ
āytal aṟivuṭiyār kaṇṇatē - kāyvataṉkaṇ
uṟṟa kuṇantōṉṟā tākum uvappataṉkaṇ
kuṟṟamun tōṉṟāk keṭum  (aṟaneṟi. 22)
kavi eṉpatu vaṭacol. pā, ceyyuḷ eṉpaṉavē teṉcol. ātalāṟ kaviñaṉip pāvalaṉ eṉṟum, kaviyaraṅkic ceyyu ḷaraṅku allatu pāvaraṅku eṉṟumē collutal vēṇṭum.
kavintukoḷvatu kavi eṉṟum, taruvatu tarumam eṉṟum, nāyi vittiruppavaṉ nāyaṉ eṉṟum, uḷtaṇamāyiruppatu uṭṭaṇam eṉṟum, iravukacital irakaciyam eṉṟum, periya vittu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum vāṉvittu eṉpatu vittuvāṉ eṉṟu talimāṟiya teṉṟum, ivi pōṉṟu piṟavum kūṟuva tellām aṟivu muṟippaṭṭaṉa vallaveṉṟu kūṟi viṭukka.
20. poṟumi
cila coṟkaṭku vērkāṭṭum uṟavuc coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭi ruppatāl, avaṟṟiṉ vēr kāṇṭaṟku nāṭpalavaṉṟi āṇṭu palavum ākalām. ātalāl, collārāyccikku mikunta poṟumiyum vēṇṭum.
āṇṭu palavākiyum vērkāṇa viyalātu pōkavum nērum. ataṉāl uḷḷan taḷartal kūṭātu.
neṟiyaṟintu āra amara ārāyiṉ, iṟṟit tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ nūṟṟiṟ keḻupattintiṟku vērkāṇa viyalum. eṉiṉum iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭiyē ovvoruvarkkum ovvoru tuṟiyil āṟṟalamiyum eṉpati niṉivil iruttutal vēṇṭum.
vāṉkuruvi yiṉkūṭu vallarakkut tolkaṟiyāṉ
tēṉcilampi yāvarkkuñ ceyyaritāl - yāmperitum
vallōmē yeṉṟu valimicola vēṇṭāṅkāṇ
ellārkkum ovvoṉ ṟeḷitu. 
(auviyār)  (centamiḻc celvi mārccu 1967)
colavam
colavam - clōka
col - colavu = marapuk kūṟṟu, paḻamoḻi, paḻamoḻi pōṉṟa ceyyuḷ toṭar.
colavu - colavam. colavu - colavaṭi.
vaṭamoḻiyil mutal vaṉappu (ātikāviyam) vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇam eṉpar. vāṉmīki muṉivar kāṭṭil oru vēṭaṉāṟ kollappaṭṭa paṟaviyik kaṇṭu varuntiyapōtu tam mutaṟ ceyyuḷip pāṭiyatāl, varuttattik kuṟikkum cōka(m) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu taṉic ceyyuṭkuc clōka eṉṟu peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟu vaṭavar kūṟuvatu marapu. mā. vi. a. iti maṟuttuc cru (kēḷ) eṉṉum colloṭu toṭarpu koṇṭatāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutu kiṉṟatu.  (va.va. 167).
coli
coli - jval (vē.)
cul - culli = aṭuppu. cuḷḷeṉal = veyil cuṭutal. cuḷ - cuḷḷi = maṭkalañ cuṭumiṭam, kāḷavāy. cul - col - coli. colittal = erital, oḷirtal. col - coṉ - coṉṉam = taṅkam. col = poṉṉiṟamāṉa nel. caṭiccennel poṉ viḷikkum (naḷa. cuyamvara. 68). o.nō: nil - nilā, nilavu. niṟṟal = viḷaṅkutal. nil - nel = viḷaṅkum poṉpōṉṟa kūlam.  (va.va. 167:168).
coṟkulamuṅ kuṭumpamum
makkaḷippōṉṟē coṟkaḷum kuṭumpaṅ kuṭumpamākavum kulaṅkulamāka vum toṭarpuṟṟu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru moḻiyum oru nāṭum. ovvoru vērvaḻic coṟṟokutiyum oru kulamum, ovvōr aṭivaḻic coṟṟokuti yum oru kuṭumpamum pōlvaṉa.
oru nāṭṭāṉ ayalnāṭu ceṉṟu pallāṇṭu taṅkik kuṭiyurimi peṟṟuviṭiṉum. avaṉ ayaṉmiyi maṟikka muṭiyātu. yārēṉum maṟikka muyaliṉ, varalāṟṟārāycci ati veḷippaṭuttiviṭum. aṅṅaṉamē, orumoḻic collum piṟamoḻic ceṉṟu vaḻakkūṉṟiṉum ataṉ ayaṉmiyi maṟikka muṭiyātu; yārēṉum maṟikka muyaliṉ, varalāṟṟā rāycci ataṉi veḷippaṭuttiviṭum.
iv vīruṇmikaḷiyum viḷakkumāṟu oru coṟkulattiyum mūṉṟu coṟkuṭumpaṅkaḷiyum īṇṭuk kāṭṭuvōm.

marakkaṇṇō maṇṇāḷvār kaṇ  (perunto. 634)
kaṇ - kaṇu. kaṇu - kaṇi = kaṇuppōṉṟa tiraṭci.
kaṇikkāl = kaṇukkāl. kaṇi - kaṇiyam = tiraṇṭamaram.
kaṇ = iṭam, pakkam. kaṇ + ci - kaṭci - cakṣyā (va.).
kaṇ - kaṇṭu = kaṇuppōṉṟa tuṇṭu. tuṇṭākiya kaṭṭi, nūluruṇṭi.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam - khanda (va.) = tuṇṭu, nilappakuti, pakuti.
uppuk kaṇṭam, kaṇṭaṅ kaṇṭamāy naṟukkutal mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷi nōkkuka.
o.nō: tuṇṭu - tuṇṭam.
kaṇṭam - kāṇṭam - kanda (va.) = nūṟpakuti.
kaṇṭam - kaṇṭiki - nilappakuti.
kaṇṭiki - kāṇṭiki = (va.) = khandika tuṇṭupōṟ curuṅkiyavuri.
kaṇṭu - kaṇṭi - khand (va.) o.nō: tuṇṭu - tuṇṭi.
kaṇṭittal = tuṇṭittal, tuṇṭittal pōlak kaṭital, variyaṟuttal, kaṇṭippu = variyaṟavu. kaṭumiyāṉa oḻuṅku.
kaṇṭi - kaṭi. o.nō: taṇṭi - taṭi(vi.).
kaṭital = kaṇṭittal, vilakkutal.
kaṇṭi + aṉam = kaṇṭaṉam kandana (va.)
kaṇṭi - kaṇṭitam - khandita (va.) = kaṇṭippu, variyaṟavu.
kaṇṭitam - kaṇṭicam.
kaṇ - kaṇṇi = kaṇkaṇṇākak kaṭṭiya māli, kaṇpōṉṟa tuvāramuḷḷa vali, tuvāra muḷḷatu.
(e-ṭu: : palakaṇṇi - palakaṇi = caṉṉal).
kaṇ - kāṇ - kaṇṇaṉ pār(vi).
kāṇ - kāṇam = mēṟpārvi. kaṇkāṇam eṉpatu vaḻakku.
kaṇkāṇi = mēṟpārppavaṉ.
kaṇkāṇiyār = attiyaṭcar (Bishop), capiyi mēṟpārppavar.

kaṇ = aṟivu, ñāṉam.
kaḷḷoṟṟik kaṇcāy pavar  (kuṟaḷ. 927)
kāṇ - A.S. cunnan, to know; E. cunning, knowing; M.E. con, to study carefully, Goth. kunnan, to know, O.H.G. chann.
kāṇ eṉṉum teṉcol kṇā - kṉā eṉṟu cila āriya moḻikaḷil māṟiyum. ‘gna’ eṉṟu cila āriya moḻikaḷil māṟit tirintum. jñā eṉṟu vēta āriyattil mēlum tirintum vaḻaṅkum.
A.S. cnawan; Ice. kna; E. know; L. gna, Gk. gno; Skt. jna; Russ. jna.
piṟkālattu āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ‘gna’ aṭiyum (e-ṭu: cognisance, prognosticate, ignorance) muṟkālattu āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ‘con’ allatu ‘kon’ aṭiyum iruppatiyum, jñāṉam eṉṉum camaṟkirutac col uṭpaṭa ‘know’ eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkiṉa māṉa ellāvaṟṟukkum ‘kon’ eṉpatiyē mūlamāka mēli moḻinūlār kuṟittiruppatiyum, kāṇ eṉṉum teṉ colliṉ iyalpiyum, ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa āriyac coṟkaḷiṉ tiripiyum, avaṟṟuḷ camaṟkirutac collum āriyac collum aṭintuḷḷa tiripiṉ kōṭiyiyum uṟṟu nōkkuka.
tamiḻk kaṇṇiliruntu camaṟkirutam ñāṉam peṟṟatu eṉpatu, collaḷa vilum poruḷaḷavilum uṇmiyāyiruttal nōkkattakkatu.
coṟkuṭumpaṅkaḷ
1. 	til eṉpatu, parumaṉāl ākiya tiṇmiyiyum, ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum valimiyiyum, atu koṇṭu ceyyappaṭum muraṇṭiyum kuṟikkum.
o.nō: muraṇ = perumi, valimi, māṟupāṭu, muraṇ - muraṭu = parumaṉ, karaṭu, vaṉkuṇam. muraṇ - muraṇṭu = māṟupāṭu. piṭivātam, ēṟumāṟu. muraṭu - muruṭu.

o.nō: oḷi - oḷir - oḷiṟu, karu - kaṟu.
tiṟam = tiṇṇam, uṟuti, valimi, valiya taṉmi, taṉmi, taṉmi pōṉṟa kūṟupāṭu, nūṟpakuti.

vaku eṉṉum aṭiyiṉkīḻk kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷellām. koṭpāṭṭaṉ - pāṭṭaṉ - makaṉ - pēraṉ - koṭpēraṉ eṉṟa toṭarpu pōlat toṭarpupaṭṭi ruppatiyum. vaku eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa paku eṉṉum aṭiyē vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅku vatiyum atuvum bhaj eṉṟa tirivaṭiviṟ kāṭṭappaṭuvatiyum. paku eṉpataṉ aṭippiṟanta vaṭamoḻic coṟkaḷ teṉ moḻic coṟkaḷ pōlap palkiyum toṭarpupaṭṭum irāmi yiyum. paku eṉṉum orē yaṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintuḷḷa pakkam, pāku, pākam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil muṟiyē paksha, bahu, bhaga eṉa vevvēṟu mutaleḻuttuṭaṉ vaḻaṅku vatiyum, cevvaṉ āyntu kāṇka.
3. vīḻ. vīḻtal = oruvarmēṟ kātalkoṇṭu viḻutal, aṅṅaṉ viḻumāṟu kātalittal, kātalittal, virumputal. ‘To fall in love with’ eṉṉum āṅkila vaḻakki nōkkuka.
tāmvīḻvār meṉṟōḷ tuyiliṉ iṉitukol
tāmarik kaṇṇāṉ ulaku  (kuṟaḷ. 1103)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
vīḻ - viḻu. viḻutal = virumputal. viḻuttal = piṟar virumpumpaṭi ciṟattal.
oruvarkku vēṇṭiyavaric ciṟantār eṉṟu colvatiyum, virumpap paṭukiṉṟavar ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭappaṭuvatiyum, nampu naya eṉṉum pakutikaḷiliruntu talimi kuṟikkum nampaṉ. nāyakaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttaliyum nōkkuka.
vaṭuka varacarkkuc ciṟantār cōḻiyavaracar eṉpatu paḻiya vaḻakku.
viḻukkaḻañcu, viḻuppuṇ, viḻuppēṟu mutaliya toṭarkaḷil. viḻuccol ciṟappuk kuṟittal kāṇka.
viḻu - viḻumu. viḻumutal = ciṟattal. viḻumiya = ciṟanta.
o.nō: kuḻu - kuḻumu - kuḻumiya.
viḻumu - viḻumam = ciṟappu.
viḻu - viḻuppu - viḻuppam = ciṟappu. viḻuppu + ariyaṉ = viḻuppariyaṉ.
viḻu - viḻā - viḻavu = virumpicceyyum koṇṭāṭṭam. viḻā - vrā (va.).
aṇṇāmali palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭākiya tamiḻ varalāṟu eṉṉum pōliyārāycci nūlil. vrā eṉṉum vaṭacolli ṉiṉṟu viḻā eṉṉum teṉcol piṟantuḷḷatākak kūṟiyuḷḷa kūṟṟiṉ puṉmiyiyum purimiyiyum nōkkit teḷika.
viḻu - viḻi. Gk. philos, desire.
Philanthropy, Philology mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷiṉ mutaṉili viḻivuc collē.
viḻi - viḻicci = iṉpanukarcci, puṇarcci.
viḻi - viḻiccu = puṇarcci.
viḻi - viḻāy - viṭāy = nīrvēṭki, viruppam.
viḻu - viḷ - viru - viruntu = virumpiyiṭum ciṟanta vūṇ, avvūṇukkuriya puttāḷ, putumi.
viru - virum - virumpu.
viḷ - veḷ - vem. vemmi = viruppam.
vemmi vēṇṭal  (tol. 817)
vemmi - veyyōṉ = virumpukiṉṟavaṉ. virumpappaṭat takkavaṉ.
o.nō: cemmi - ceyyōṉ - cēyōṉ.
veḷ - vēḷ = virumpu (vi.) virumpattakka talivaṉ. talivaṉ. ciṟṟaracaṉ, kātali yuṇṭupaṇṇum kāmaṉ. kuṟiñcit talivaṉākiya murukaṉ.
vēḷ + āḷaṉ - vēḷāḷaṉ = piṟari virumpi viruntōmpum uḻavaṉ.
vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka uṇṇātāṉ  (tirikaṭu. 12)
vēḷ + āṇmi (āḷ + mi) = vēḷāṇmi = vēḷāḷaṉ taṉmiyākiya viruntōmpal. avaḷ ceyyum payirttoḻil.
vēḷ - vēḷāṉ = murukaṉ kōyiṟ pūcāriyākiya kuyavaṉ.
vēḷ + kō = vēṭkō = kuyavar talivaṉ. kuyavaṉ.
vēḷ - vēṇ = viruppam. vēṇ + avā = vēṇavā.
vēḷ - vēṇṭu. o.nō: E. want.
vēṇṭu - vēṇṭum (taṉmip paṉmi uṭaṉpāṭu).
vēṇṭu - vēṇṭām - vēṇṭā (taṉmippaṉmi etirmaṟi).
vēḷ - vēṭki = viruppam. nīr viruppam (tākam).
vēḷ -vēḷvi = oṉṟi virumpic ceyyum yākam, viruntōmpal, tirumaṇam viḻā.
vēḷ - vēḷvu = yākam, tirumaṇam varici.
vēḷ - vēṭṭam = uyirikaḷi virumpip piṭittal.
vēḷ - vēṭṭi = uyirikaḷi virumpip piṭittal.
vēṭṭam - vēṭṭuvaṉ - vēṭuvaṉ - vēṭu - vēṭaṉ = vēṭṭit toḻilōṉ.
viḷ - piḷ - piṇ - piṇā - piṇavu - piṇaval = virumpappaṭum peṇ, peṇvilaṅku.
piṇ - piṇi = peṇ vilaṅku. piṇ - (piṇi) - piṭi.
veḷ - peḷ - peṭpu = viruppam, kātal.
peḷ - peṇ - āṭavaṉāl virumpappaṭupavaḷ.
peḷ - (peṭṭu) - E. Pet.
peṇ - peṇṭu. peṇṭu + āṭṭi = peṇṭāṭṭi.
peṇ - peṇṇi = kāykkum peṇmaram. peṇpaṉi, paṉi.
peṇṇi - peṇṇiyaṉ = peṇṭaṉmiyaṉ.
peṇ - pēṇ, pēṇutal = virumputal, virumpip pātukāttal.
peḷ - peṭṭu - peṭṭi - peṭi - pēṭi - pēṭu - pēṭaṉ. 
pēṭi.
itukāṟum, mūṉṟu coṟkuṭumpaṅkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
kal eṉṉum vēraṭic coṟṟokutikaḷ kāḻ kaṇ eṉṉum aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantu karumi eṉṉum vērkkaruttu maṟintu, viḷiyāṭu, eri, pār, kā mutaliya viṉip poruḷ kaḷip peṟṟa, kaḷi kāy kaṇ kāṇi mutaliya coṟkaḷik koṇṭa tokutiyātaliṉ, coṟkulameṉṟum, īṇṭuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa  til vaku vīḻ eṉṉum mūvaṭic coṟṟokutikaḷ, tiṇmi, pakuppu, viruppam ākiya mūlak karuttukaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟiyē taḻuviya coṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷātaliṉ, coṟ kuṭumpameṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
oru kuṭumpam pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittalum. ap pala kuṭumpamuṅ cērntu oru kulamātalum pōla, oru coṟkuṭum pamum pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷit tōṟṟavittalum, ap pala kuṭumpamuñ cērntu oru kulamātalum uṇṭu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tiṉ, tiri, tiruku, tirumpu, tiṟampu, timiṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷōṭu cērkkap paṭiṉ, til kuṭumpamum oru kulamāki viṭum.
kuṭumpu, kuṭumpam, kuṭi, kulam, pāl, iṉam, varaṇam eṉa mēṉmēl viriyum makkaṭ kūṭṭap pakuppu, coṟkaḷukkum ēṟkum, ip pakuppukaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum ciṟiyatu periyatu eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
coṟperuṅ kulam
makkaṭṭokuti, kuṭumpam mutal varaṇamvari palvakit tokutiyākap pakukkappeṟum.
kaṇavaṉum maṉiviyum maṇamākāta makkaḷumākak kūṭivāḻuṅ kūṭṭam kuṭumpākum. peṟṟōrum maṇamākip piḷḷipeṟṟa makkaḷumākak kūṭivāḻuṅ kūṭṭam kuṭumpamākum. ammīṟu perumip poruḷ vikutiyātaliṉ, kuṭumpiṟ periyatu kuṭumpameṉṟum, ivviru peyarkaḷum ulakavaḻakkil taṭumāṟi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum. ammīṟu perumipporuḷ vikutiyātali, kaṇṭu - kaṇṭam. kampu - kampam eṉṉum peyariṇikaḷiṟ kāṇka.
pala talimuṟiyākat toṭarntuvarum kuṭumpam kuṭiyākum. kuṭiyik kōttira meṉpar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. toṭarpuḷḷa pala kuṭikaḷ cērntatu kulamākum. ceṭṭiyār mutaliyār eṉpaṉa kulaṅkaḷ. kuṟavar. maṟavar, iṭiyar, kuṭiyāṉavar (uḻavar), cempaṭavar eṉpaṉa mutaṟkālattil aintiṇik kuṭikaḷāka viruntu, piṉpu aiṅkulaṅkaḷāka māṟiviṭṭaṉa.
taṉittavarum tokutiyāṉavarumākat toḻilotta pala kulaṅkaḷ allatu kulattār cērntatu pālākum. antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa tamiḻakattu nāṟpālkaḷ. antaṇar eṉpār, aruḷ mutirnta muṉivar, kuṭiyāṉavar, kavuṇṭar, akampaṭiyar mutaliya uḻutuṇṇuṅ kulaṅkaḷum mutaliyār. veḷḷāḷar mutaliya uḻuvit tuṇṇuṅ kulaṅkaḷum cērntatu vēḷāṇ pālākum, iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum. 
pala pālkaḷ cērntatu iṉam, tamiḻar, teluṅkar, kaṉṉaṭar, maliyāḷiyar mutaliyaṉa iṉaṅkaḷ, iṉattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭatu varaṇam, āriyar, tirāviṭar, maṅkōliyar mutaliyar varaṇaṅkaḷ, varaṇam eṉṉum colliṉ iyaṟporuḷ niṟam eṉpatē. ātalāl, niṟa vēṟupāṭṭi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatē varaṇap pakuppākum. āriyar veṇṇiṟattar; maṅkōliyar poṉṉiṟattar; tirāviṭar pukar niṟattar.
makkaṭ kalappālum taṭpaveppanili vēṟupāṭṭālum varaṇattāriṉ niṟam māṟuvatuṇṭu.
kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam vari ovvoru tokutiyum aḷavum toṭarpum paṟṟic ciṟiyatum periyatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum.
makkaḷpōṉṟē coṟkaḷum kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam vari palavakit tokutikaḷākap pakuttaṟ kēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām, tamiḻarippōl kuṭumpu mutal pāl vari aivakit tokutikaḷākap pakukkap peṟum.
tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, maliyāḷam mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻika ḷellām iṉamoḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiluḷḷa potuccoṟ kaḷellām iṉac coṟkaḷākum.
tirāviṭac collōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa āriyac coṟkaḷum, cittiyac coṟkaḷum. avi pōṉṟa piṟavum, varaṇam eṉṉum tokutippaṭṭaṉa. ātitirāviṭa makkaḷ taṭpaveppanili vēṟupāṭṭāl eṅṅaṉam āriya rāṉārō, aṅṅaṉamē avar coṟkaḷum āriyamāy māṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉka.
ik kaṭṭuriyiṉ toṭakkattil kāṭṭappeṟṟa coṟkulattil, mutaliliruntu kaṉṟu - aṉṟu eṉpatu variyum oru coṟ kuṭumpamākum.
ataṉoṭu kaḷ - kaḷam eṉpatu variyuḷḷa pakutiyic cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkuṭumpamākum.
ataṉoṭu kaṟu - kāṟu eṉpatuvari cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkuṭi yākum; ataṉoṭu iṟutivari cērttuk koḷḷiṉ oru coṟkulamākum.
kal eṉṉum vēr, kul eṉṉum vēriṉ tiripākum. collākkat tirivil ciṟupāṉmi ukaram akaramākat tirital uṇṭu.
e-ṭu: muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku kurum - karam (inti) kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu kuṭam - kaṭam (vaṭamoḻi).
Butt (muṭṭu) cuckoo (kukkū) mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷil ukaram akaramāka olippatu ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē.
kul eṉṉum vērccoṟ poruḷ poruntal allatu kalattal.
kul - kulavu = poruntu, kūṭu. kulavu - kulāvu.
kul - kulam = kūṭṭam, vakuppu.
kul - kuli = kūṭṭam, kottu.
kuḷ - kuḻu = kūṭṭam, tiraṭci, kuḻu - kuḻūu.
kuḻu - kuḻām, kuḻu - kuḻi = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.
kuḻu - kuḻumu - kuḻumam. o.nō: L. glomus, E. glomerate, globe, etc.
kuḻu - kuḻavi = uruṇṭa aṟikal.
kuḷ - kuṇṭu = tiraṇṭatu, uruṇṭiyāṉatu.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu pakutikaḷ oṉṟu cērum pōtu tiraṭciyum ataṉāl uruṭciyum uṇṭākum.
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu oṭṭāp poruḷāyiṉ tiraṭcimaṭṭum uṇṭākumeṉṟum, oṭṭum poruḷāyiṉ tiraṭciyōṭu uruṭciyum uṇṭākumeṉṟum pakuttaṟital vēṇṭum. makkaṭṭiraḷiyum maṭṭiraḷiyum niṉittuk kāṇka.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭi = uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa erutu.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭaṉ = tiraṇṭavaṉ, uṭal valimiyāl piṟari varuttupavaṉ.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭāṉ = tiraṇṭa taṭi.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭā = tiraṇṭa vaṭivāṉa kalam.
kuṇṭu - kuṇṭalam = tiraṇṭa kātaṇi.
kul - kal - kala = poruntu, kūṭu.
kal - kalvi, kalavi, kalappu.
kala - kalampu - kalampam - katampam.
kalampu - kalampakam.
kala - kalakam = palar kalantu ceyyum caṇṭi.
kikalattal eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
kala - kalām, o.nō: poru - pōr.
porutal = poruntutal, kalattal.
kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam.
kalaṅkal = pala poruḷkaḷ kūṭutal. ataṉāl uṇṭākum mayakkam. 
maṇ tūci mutaliya piṟa poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ kalanta nīr kalaṅkal nīrākum. pala poruḷkaḷ kalantirukkum pōtu. avaṟṟuḷ vēṇṭiyatip pirittaṟiya muṭiyāta kalakkam ēṟpaṭutaliṉ, kalattaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu kalakkak karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
atuvō, ituvō, aṉṟi vēṟetuvō eṉṟippaṭi oṉṟit tuṇiya muṭiyātavāṟu pala karuttukaḷ maṉattiṟ kalattal maṉakkalakkam eṉṟaṟika.
kavarvaḻikaḷik kāṇumpōtu vaḻip pōkkaṉukkum, orunilippaṭṭavar palar kūṭiyirukkum pōtu avaruḷ oruvarik kāṇa virumpum putiyōṉukkum maṉakkalakkam ēṟpaṭutal kāṇka.
naḷaṉ vaṭivil nālvarik kaṇṭa tamayanti kalakkattiyum niṉittuk kāṇka.
kalakkam eṉpataṉ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷāṉa mayakkam, maruḷ eṉṉum coṟkaḷum itē moḻipporuṭ kāraṇattik koṇṭaviyē.
mayaṅku - mayakkam, mayaṅkutal = kalattal, kalaṅkutal.
tiṇikaḷum, vēṟṟumikaḷum tammuḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalattal tiṇimayakkam eṉṟum vēṟṟumi mayakkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. pakalum iravum kalakkum māliyi mayaṅkupoḻutu eṉpar. itu nūlvaḻakkil maruṇmāli eṉappaṭum.
iṉi, oḷiyiṉmiyālō, kātalarp pirintamiyālō makkaḷ maṉaṅkalaṅkum māli maruṇmāli eṉiṉumām.
maruṭci = mayakkam. maru - maruḷ. maruvutal = poruntutal, kalattal.
maru - marumam - marumar - mammar = mayakkam.
Confusion eṉṉum āṅkilac collum kalattal eṉṉuṅ karuttaṭiyākavē mayakkap poruḷik koṇṭatākum. (con = together, fuse = mix).
kala - kaluḻ - kaluri. kaluḻtal = kalattal, kalaṅkutal. kaluḻi = kalaṅkal nīr, miyoṭu kalanta allatu maṉaṅkalaṅki yaḻum kaṇṇīr.
kalakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu iruṭkaruttum. iruṭkaruttiṉiṉṟu karumik karuttum tōṉṟum. aṟivu akavoḷi yātaliṉ, aṟiyāmi allatu mayakkam akaviruḷ eṉappaṭum. oṉṟaṉ uṇmi yaṟiyātu kalaṅkum kalakkam poruḷ teriyāta iruḷpōṉṟa niliyē. ‘To be left in the dark’ eṉpar āṅkilarum, iruḷ karuniṟam ākiyāl, kalakkap poruṭcol karumip poruḷit taḻuviṟṟu.
kal - kāl = karuppu. kal - kaṉṟu = veyilāṟ karuku.
oru coṟku oru poruṇmi. uttikkup poruntuvatōṭu oppumiyāl uṟutippaṭa vēṇṭum. kalattaṟporuḷ tarum kal eṉṉum col karumipporuḷ kuṟittal pōṉṟē. ataṉ oruporuṭ collāṉa maya eṉṉum collum karumip poruḷ kuṟikkum. mayaṅku eṉṉum colliṉ vēr maya eṉpatē. kalaṅku eṉṉum colliṉ vēr kala eṉṟiruttal kāṇka.
maya - mayaṅku - macaṅku.
maya = mayal - māl. mayal - miyal = kātal mayakkam.
māl = mayakkam, karumi, kariya mēkam, kariya viṇṭu (viṣṇu).
māl - maḻi, māl - māri = kariyāḷ, kāḷi, maḻi.
māl - mālam = mayakkam, ēmāṟṟam.
māl - māli = mayakkam, palaporuḷ kalanta varici, mayaṅkum vēḷi. māli - mālā (va.).
māl - mā - mi. māmi = karumi. māyōṉ = kariyaṉ. viṇṭu. 
o.nō: E. malbino = kariyaṉ.
mi = karuppu, mēkam. veḷḷāṭu (kārāṭu), kariya paci allatu kuḻampu, pacipperuḷ.
mi - maci - macaku = vaṇṭi mi.
kāṭcipporuṭ karuttu, karuttupporuṭ karuttu eṉṉum iruvakik karuttuḷ, muṉṉatiṉiṉṟu piṉṉatu tōṉṟuvatē iyalpāyiṉum, iṉṉatiṉiṉṟē iṉṉatu tōṉṟavēṇṭu meṉṉum yāppuṟavilli. ivviraṇṭuḷ etaṉiṉṟum etuvum tōṉṟalām. allākkāl collākkam peritum muṭṭuppaṭum.
ik kaṭṭuriyiṉ mutaṟ pakutiyil variyappeṟṟa kal eṉṉum coṟkulattōṭu, ataṉ muṟpaṭiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭappeṟṟa pakutiyic cērttukkoḷḷiṉ, orucoṟ peruṅkula mākum. ellāt tamiḻc coṟkaḷum perumpālum ā ī ū ē ō eṉṉum ainneṭilkaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟantiruppatāl avvintum coṟpālaṭikaḷākum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, makkaḷ pōṉṟē coṟkaḷum, kuṭumpu mutal varaṇam īṟākap palvakit tokutikaḷāka virintu iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟam, makkaṭkuḷḷa toṭarpu pōṉṟē avar coṟkaṭkum moḻi kaṭkum toṭarpuṇṭeṉṟum naṭuniliyākavum neṟippaṭavum nuṭpamākavum āyntu nōkku vārkkuc coṟṟoṭarpum moḻittoṭarpum pulaṉāvatu tiṇṇameṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
aṭiccol kāṇvaḻi
pala maraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṉṉip piṇintu koṇṭi ruppiṉ, pārttamaṭṭil avaṟṟiṉ aṭikaḷik kāṇpatu aritākum. avaṟṟiṉ nuṉikkoḻuntiliruntu iṇukkum. vaḷārum, kuccum, pōttum, ciṉiyum, kompum, kiḷiyum kaviyumāka ovvoṉṟāyk kīḻnōkkip pirittukkoṇṭē variṉ, iṟutiyil, nilattiṉmēl mutaṟ kīḻppakutiyākavuḷḷa aṭiyik kāṇalām. ataṉpiṉ avaṟṟiṉ vērik kāṇpatu tēṟṟamāyiṉam, nilattit tōṇṭiṉā laṉṟik kāṇa muṭiyātu, ip paṉmarap piṇiyal pōṉṟē. pala aṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu kavittuk kiḷittup palkip perukiyuḷḷa coṟṟokuti kaḷum, pārtta maṭṭil pirittuṇarappaṭāvāṟu, tammuḷ mayaṅkik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyik kāṇpataṟkum mēliruntu kīḻnōkki varal vēṇṭum. aṭiyik kaṇṭapiṉ, āḻnta ārāycciyiṉālaṉṟi vērik kāṇa muṭiyātu. itik kuṟittaṟkē.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. peyar. 1)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyar.
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol. uri. 96)
eṉṟu variyiṭṭār.
collaṭi kāṇvaḻiyik kāṭṭumukamāka, kōṭu eṉṉum colli eṭuttukkoṇṭu. ataṉ aṭiyi īṇṭu āykiṉṟēṉ.
kōṭu eṉṉum col, kōṇutal allatu vaḷital eṉṉum poruḷik koṇṭu, peyarākavum viṉiyākavum vaḻaṅkum col. itaṉ vaḻiyākac cila coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa.
vaḷitaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu vaṭṭak karuttum, vaṭṭak karuttiṉiṉṟu cūḻtaṟ karuttum tōṉṟum. orē poruḷ vaṭṭamāka, mēṉmēl vaḷintu celvatu tirukalmuṟukalāṉa puritalātaliṉ, vaḷivuk karuttiṉiṉṟu puritaṟ karuttum tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu:	vaḷi - vaḷiyam (vaṭṭam)
		vaḷi - vaḷical (cūḻvu)
		vaḷi - vaḷi (caṅkiṉ vala iṭap purivu)
kōṭutal = vaḷital, kōṭṭam = vaḷivu, matilāl vaḷiyappaṭṭa kōvil allatu ciṟiccāli, vēliyāl vaḷiyappaṭṭa māṭṭuk koṭṭil.
kōṭṭi = vaḷinta matil, vaṭṭamāṉa neṟkūṭu, matiyic cūḻntuḷḷa ūrkōḷ.
kōṭṭakam = vaḷinta kuḷakkari, kari.
kōṭu = vaḷivu, vaḷinta vari, vari.
kōṭu = vaḷinta marakkiḷi, kiḷi.
kōṭu = vaḷinta kompu, kompu.
kōṭu = vaḷinta kari, kari.
kōṭu = vaḷinta caṅku, caṅku.
maliyik kuṟikkum kōṭu eṉṉum col kuvaṭu eṉṉum colliṉ tiripātaliṉ vēṟām.
kōṭṭam eṉṉum teṉcollē, vaṭamoḻiyil kōṣṭa eṉṟum ilattīṉil castrum eṉṟum ataṉ vaḻiyāy āṅkilattil ‘caster’, ‘chester’ eṉṟum tiriyum. āṅkilat tirivukaḷ ūrppeyarīṟākavum vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu: doncaster, colchester, exeter (excester).
ip peyarkaḷi, aṟuppukkōṭṭi, paṭṭukkōṭṭi mutaliya tamiḻnāṭṭu ūrppeyarkaḷuṭaṉ oppunōkkuka.
kōṭu eṉpataṉ nērmūlam kōṇ.
o.nō: pāṇ - pāṭu, pēṇ - pēṭu.
kōṇ eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu, kōṇu, kōṇal, kōṇam, kōṇi mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
kōṇam eṉṉum teṉcollē, kirēkkattil ‘gonia’ (angle) eṉṟum, ataṉ vaḻiyāy āṅkilat toṭarccoṟkaḷil gon eṉṟum tiriyum.
e-ṭu: Trigonometry (tirikōṇa māttiri)
polygon (palakōṇam)
