itukāṟum kāṭṭappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ kaṇṭa vaḷavil toṭar paṟiyap paṭātu paṉmarap piṇiyal pōlap piṟa coṟkaḷuṭaṉ piṉṉik kiṭappiṉum. oru muṟi pirittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭapiṉ evarkkum toṭarpu viḷaṅkātirā. kōṭu eṉṉum uccik koḻuntiliruntu ul eṉṉum aṭi vari pirittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭatu. ul eṉpataṟku mūlamākiya vērō āḻnta ārāycci yiṉālaṉṟi aṟiyavum aṟivikkavum muṭiyātu.
ic collārāyccik kaṭṭuriyāl viḷakkappeṟum coṉṉūl moḻinūl uṇmikaḷum neṟimuṟikaḷumāvaṉa:
(1) ulakattiṟ pala moḻikaḷ, ciṟappākat tiruntiya moḻikaḷ, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉa.
(2)	tamiḻiluḷḷa paṉmoḻip potuc coṟkaṭkut tamiḻiltāṉ vēruṇṭu.
(3) orē peruṅ karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, oru mūlattiṉiṉṟu mēlum mēlum pala aṭikaḷum vaḻiyaṭi kaḷum tōṉṟip paṟpala coṟkaḷip piṟappikkiṉṟaṉa.
(4) oru karuttiṉiṉṟu maṟṟoru kiḷikkaruttut tōṉṟum.
(5) poruḷ tiriyum pōtu collum tiriya vēṇṭum.
(6) coṟkaḷ tiriyum pōtu palvaki vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum ēṟkum.
(7)	ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē. āṉāl, moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā.
(8) 	tamiḻ ōr iyaṉmoḻi; aḵtāvatu tāṉē iyaṟki neṟiyil tōṉṟiya tāymoḻi.
(9)	moḻi, oru cila mūlavolikaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi vaḷarnta vaḷarcciyē.
(10) moḻi māntaṉ amippē; kaṭavuḷ paṭippaṉṟu.  - centamiḻc celvi ceppiṭempar 1947.
coṟporuḷ varici
coṟkaḷ, poruṭṭoki paṟṟi 1. oruporuṭ col 2. palaporuṭ col eṉa iru vakippaṭum. āyiṉum, oru colliṉ mutaṟ poruḷ vaḻiyākap palvēṟu poruḷkaḷ ovvoṉṟāyt tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭamiruttalāl, oru kālattu oruporuṭ collāyiruntaṉavum, piṟkālattup pala poruṭ coṟkaḷākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattu oru coṟku oru poruḷē tōṉṟuvatu iyalpātalāl. palaporuṭ coṟka ḷellām mutaṟkaṇ oru poruṭ coṟkaḷāy iruntaṉavē.
coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷvarici, atāvatu poruḷkaḷ muṉṉum piṉṉum tōṉṟiya muṟi 1. varalāṟṟu muṟi (Historical Sequence). 2. ēraṇa muṟi (Logical Sequence) eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oru colliṉ poruḷkaḷi. mutal vaḻi cārpu nūlkaḷil allatu muṉṉiṭi piṉṉūlkaḷil avi āḷappaṭṭu vanta varaṉ muṟippaṭiyē oḻuṅkupaṭuttuvatu varalāṟṟu muṟiyām; aḵtaṉṟi uttikkup poruttamāka avaṟṟi oḻuṅkupaṭuttu vatu ēraṇamuṟiyām. inti pōṉṟa putumoḻikaḷilum āriyam pōṉṟa mutumoḻikaḷilum muṉṉi allatu paṇṭi ilakkiyam aḻiyātiruttalāl ataṉvāyilākac coṟporuḷkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu muṟiyi aṟital kūṭum. āyiṉ paṇṭi yilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭa tamiḻpōlun toṉmutumoḻiyil ataṉi aṟiyumāṟilli. ātalāl, attakiya moḻic coṟporuḷkaṭku ēraṇa muṟitāṉ iyalum. oru moḻiyiṉ vaḷarccik kālattuc coṟkaṭku ovvoṉṟāyt tōṉṟiya poruḷkaḷ. avi tōṉṟiya muṟiyē piṟkālattu uṇarttappeṟā: avvav iṭattiṟ kēṟpavē uṇarttappeṟum. oru vaḻaṅkumoḻi eṉṟum vaḷarntu koṇṭēyiruppiṉum. ataṉ peruvāric coṟkaḷ oru kuṟitta kālattiṟkuḷ amintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉpiṉ, tōṉṟum coṟkaḷum perumpālum putuccoṟkaḷā yirāmal paḻañcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu amiyum tiricoṟkaḷum kūṭṭuc coṟ kaḷumākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, mutu paṇṭiyilakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭatum. karuttiṟku meṭṭāk kālantoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvatumāṉa tamiḻil, coṟporuḷ varici ēraṇa muṟippaṭitāṉ amital iyalum. varalāṟṟu muṟi iyaṟki yākavum ēraṇa muṟi ceyaṟkiyākavum tōṉṟiṉum muṉṉatu uḷanūṉ muṟippaṭi nikaḻtaliṉ av uḷanūlit taḻuviya ēraṇa muṟiyum perumpālum iyaṟki yoṭṭiyē irukku meṉa aṟika. ivvuṇmikaḷi aṟiyātār tamiḻilum coṟ poruḷ variciyi āṅkilattiṟpōl varalāṟṟu muṟiyil amikka muyalvar. itaṉ purimiyi ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāṟ kāṭṭutum.
paḷḷi eṉṉum coṟkuc ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa poruḷvarici varumāṟu:
1. iṭam				2. ciṟṟūr
3. iṭiccēri			4. nakaram
5. muṉiva rācciramam		6. ciṉa puttak kōyil
7. araṇmaṉi			8. (taccaṉ) vēlikkaḷam
9. makkaṭ paṭukki		10. tūkkam
11. vilaṅku tuyiliṭam	12. paḷḷikkūṭam
13. aṟi			14. aṟaccāli
15. cāli			16. vaṉṉiyaṉ
17. paḷḷatti			18. kuṟumpar (ciṟṟaracar)
piṉṉiṇippil (aṉupantattil) kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iṉṉoru poruḷ (19) kiṟittavak kōyil eṉpatu, ivaṟṟuḷ mutalum iṟutiyum (1,19) varalāṟṟu muṟi taḻuviyavi; ēṉiya orumuṟiyun taḻuviyavalla. immuṟikēṭu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyiṉ ciṟappiyalpākum.
itupōluḷḷa tamiḻnūlkaḷuḷ paḻimiyāṉa tol kāppiyattil, paḷḷi eṉṉum col iṭam eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉum  (tol. eḻuttu. 100)
eṉpatu kāṇka. itaṉāl, iṭam eṉṉum poruḷ mutalilum, kiṟittuvam tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuttappeṟṟapiṉ tōṉṟiya kiṟittavak kōyil eṉṉum poruḷ iṟutiyilum, vikkap paṭṭaṉa.
mēṟkuṟikkapṭṭa pattoṉpāṉ poruḷkaḷoṭu, paḷḷam, vīṭu, kōyil, kallaṟi (camāti) eṉpavaṟṟiyuñ cērkkalām. kōyil eṉṟatu īṇṭu ellā mataṅkaṭkum potuvāṉa iṟiyakatti.
kaḻā akkāl paḷḷiyuḷ vittaṟṟāṟ cāṉṟōr
kuḻā attup pēti pukal  (840)
eṉṉum kuṟaṭku.
cāṉṟō raviyiṉkaṭ pētiyāyiṉāṉ pukutal, tūyavalla mititta kāli iṉpantarum amaḷikkaṇṇē vittāṟ pōlum. eṉa urittār parimēlaḻakar.
kaḻuvāk kāleṉpatu iṭakkaraṭakku. ataṉāl av vamaḷiyum iḻikkap paṭumāṟupōla ivaṉāl av vaviyum iḻikkappaṭu meṉpatām. eṉpatu avar ciṟappukkuṟippu.
iv vuriyil paḷḷiyeṉpatu paṭukki allatu paṭukkik kaṭṭil eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. cāṉṟōrit tēvaruk koppākak kūṟuvatu ilakkiya marapātalāl, iṅkup paḷḷi yeṉpatu teyvattaṉmiyuḷḷa iṭattik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatallatu iṉpan tarum iṭattik kuṟippateṉṟu koḷvatu poruntātu. ākavē, paḷḷi eṉpataṟkuk kōyil eṉpatē poruḷām. cāṉṟōr kūṭṭattuḷ ōr aṟivili pukutal oruvaṉ kaḻuvāta kāloṭu kōyiṟkuḷ pukuvatokkum eṉpatē vaḷḷuvar karuttātal vēṇṭum. kuḷittu viṭṭu allatu ki kāl kaḻuviviṭṭuk kōyiṟkuḷ pukutal. iṉṟum palvēṟu matattārkkum vaḻakkamāyiruttal kāṇka. tēvarip pulavareṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟippiṭuvatiyum.
aṅkaṇ vicumpiṉ akaṉilāp pārikkum
tiṅkaḷum cāṉṟōrum opparmaṉ  (nālaṭi. 151)
eṉṟu nālaṭiyār kūṟuvatiyum nōkkuka.
ākavē, ēṟkeṉavē kōyilip potuppaṭak kuṟitta paḷḷiyeṉṉum peyarē. piṟkālattut tamiḻakattup putuvatāka ēṟpaṭṭa camaṇa pavutta kiṟittava mukamatiyak kōyilkaḷiyum kuṟikka āḷappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. etupōleṉiṉ, āriya maṟiyiyē mutaṉmutaṟ kuṟitta vētam eṉṉum col. piṟkālattuk kiṟittava maṟiyiyē ciṟappākak kuṟittatu pōleṉka.
vittukaḷ cēṟṟiṟ patintu kiṭattalip paḷḷikkiruttal eṉṟu nāñcil nāṭṭār colvatāl. paḷḷam eṉṉum poruḷum; araciyal tīrvi celuttātavaṉ vīṭṭil oru ciṟu kampu naṭṭu avaṉ atic celuttum vari maṟiyal ceytalip paḷḷikampu vittal eṉa an nāṭṭārē vaḻaṅkuvatāl vīṭu eṉṉum poruḷum; iṟanta aracaṉ uṭali eritta allatu putitta viṭattuk kōyiṟ kaṭṭuvatip paḷḷippaṭi eṉṟu ilakkiyamuṅ kalveṭṭum kūṟuvatāl, kallaṟi eṉṉum poruḷum; paḷḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkuk koḷḷappeṟṟaṉa.
paḷḷi eṉṉum colliṉ vēr paḷ eṉpatum, vērpporuḷ paḷḷam eṉpatum ākum. paḷḷam allatu tāḻvāṉa iṭam eṉṉum poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, paḷḷamāṉa iṭattil vēli ceyyuṅ kulattāṉ. (kīḻē) paṭukki, (paṭuttat taṅkum) maṉi, (tēvamaṉiyākiya) kōyil, (mutaṟkālattuk kōvilil naṭatta peṟṟa kalviccāli, (palamaṉikaḷ cērnta) ūr, (maṉiyum ūrumākiya) iṭam eṉṉum eḻu parup poruḷkaḷum ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟi nilikkaḷamākak koṇṭa irupattiru nuṇporuḷkaḷum, paḷḷi eṉṉuñ coṟkut tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. in nuṇporuṭ pākupāṭṭup paṭṭi varumāṟu :
1. 	paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu : tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ aminta ciṟṟūr allatu mullinilattūr. mulli nilattūr pōṉṟa iṭiccēri, āḻiṭam.
2. 	paḷḷamāṉa vayalil vēli ceyyuṅ kulattār : paḷḷatti, vaṉṉiyaṉ, vaṉṉiyakulac ciṟṟaracarāṉa kuṟumpar.
3. 	paṭukki : makkaṭ paṭukki, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam, tūkkam.
4. 	maṉi (vīṭu) : vīṭu, aṟi, araṇmaṉi, vēlikkaḷam, aṟaccāli, muṉivar tavaniliyam, cāli.
5. 	kōyil : kōyil (potu), ciṉa pavuttak kōyil, kiṟittavak kōyil, mukamatiyar kōyil (paḷḷivācal), kallaṟi, kōyil eṉpatu aracaṉ maṉi pōṉṟa tēvamaṉi eṉṟumām.
6. 	kalvic cāli
7. 	ūr : nakaram, paḷḷi eṉpatu oru kōyil allatu araṇ maṉiyiruppatu paṟṟiyum oru nakarik kuṟikkum.
8. 	iṭam :
iccoṟporuḷ varici ēraṇamuṟi taḻuviyatākum, itil iṟutiyil vantuḷḷaporuḷ, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akarātiyil mutaliṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam talikīḻāka māṟiyatu. moḻi vaḷarccik kālattu ilakkiyam muṟṟum, maṟintapiṉ iṭikkālattu ilakkiyattip paṟṟuk kōṭākak koṇṭu, varalāṟṟumuṟi taḻuvamuyaṉṟataṉ payaṉē.
iṉi, ip paḷḷi yeṉṉuñ coṟporuḷ variciyi vaḻuppaṭa amitta tōṭamiyātu. iccolli vaṭacolleṉṟuṅ kūṟat tuṇintuḷatu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakac coṟ kaḷañciyam. iccol teṉcol eṉpati, paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu eṉṉum poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, itaṉoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa ēṉic coṟkaḷik kaṇṭu teḷika. aviyāvaṉa:
paḷ - paḷḷam - paḷḷaṉ = paḷḷamāṉa vayalil vēli ceypavaṉ.
paḷ - paḷku - paḷkutal = patuṅkutal.
paḷ - paḷḷi = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭṭuvaki
paḷḷiyaṉ = kuḷḷaṉ.
paḷḷi - paḷḷiyam = uṇkalam.
paḷḷiyam pōṭutal = teyvattiṟkup paṭittal.
paḷku - paḷaku = kuṟṟam (tāḻvu).
paḷ - paṇ = nīrnili.
paṇ - paṇṇi = kuḻi, neṟkuttumāṟu nilattiṟ patitta naṭuppaḷḷak kal, nīr nili, marattiṟku aṭiyil nīr pāycca amikkum pātti, marakkalam, vilaṅku tuyiliṭam.
paṇ - paṇi, paṇital = kīḻppaṭital, paṇi = toṇṭu, vēli, toḻil.
paṇi - pāṇi, pāṇittal = kālantāḻttal.
paṇ - paṭu = 1. (pe.) kuḷam, maṭu. 2. (pe.e.) iḻivāṉa.
paṭu - paṭukar = paḷḷam, vayal, marutanilam, nīrnili.
paṭu - pāṭu - pāṭi = tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷ cērnta mulli nilattūr allatu iṭiccēri.
pāṭu - pāṭi = kālkaḻi kaṭṭil.
paṭu - paṭi - paṭittal = uṇṇumāṟu kīḻiṭutal.
paṭuttal = tāḻakkiṭantu tūṅkutal.
paṭu - paṭi = paṭukki, tūkkam.
paṭutal = viḻutal, cātal, paṭuttal = kollutal.
paṭu - paṭi = kollum āyutam allatu cēṉi.
paṭu - paṭi paṭital = aṭiyil taṅkutal, taṅkutal, kīḻppaṭital, viḻuntu vaṇaṅkutal, amuṅkutal, taṇital, kuḷittal, tūṅkutal.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻil coṟporuḷ varici ēraṇamuṟip paṭitāṉ iyalum eṉpatiyum, paḷḷi eṉpatu paḷḷam allatu tāḻvu eṉṉuṅ karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa tūya teṉcol eṉpatiyum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
vaṭamoḻiyil (camaṟkirutattil), ḷakara miṉmiyāl, paḷḷi eṉṉuñ col palli eṉṟum pallī eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉataṟkuc ciṟṟūr. kāṭuvāḻ marapiṉar kuṭiyiruppu, kuṭici, vīṭu eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum; piṉṉataṟkuc ciṟṟūr, kuṭici, vīṭu, nakaram, oru kūla mukattalaḷavu, ciṟu vīṭṭuppalli eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum; māṉiyar uvilliyam camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyil tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv iru col vaṭivukaṭkum vērākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa col pall (pall) eṉpatu, iv vērpporuḷākak kuṟikkappaṭṭavi pōtal (to go) iyaṅkutal (to move) eṉpaṉa. iv vērik kuṟikkumiṭattu. orukāl piṉvarum coṟkaṭku mūlaṅkāṭṭum poruṭṭup pal eṉṉum vēriyoṭṭip paṭikkap paṭṭatu (Invented after ‘pal’, prob, to explin the following words) eṉṟu māṉiyar uvilliyam kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. piṉvaruñ coṟkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟikkappeṟṟavi palla, pallakka, palli, pallikkā, pallī eṉpaṉa, pal (pal) eṉṉum vērkkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa poruḷum, pōtal (to go) eṉpatē.
palla = peruṅkaḷañciyam, neṟkūṭu.
pallakka = takkāṇattil oru māvaṭṭap peyariṉ piṟpakuti.
pallikkā = ciṟṟūr, ciṟu vīṭṭuppalli.
paḷḷi eṉṉum col, paḷ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu tirintu pala poruḷkaḷik koṇṭiruppiṉum, ciṟappākap paṭukkiyi yuṇarttu meṉpatu, paḷḷi koḷḷutal, paḷḷikoṇṭāṉ, paḷḷikoṇṭa perumāḷ, paḷḷiyeḻucci, paḷḷimāṭam, paḷḷimaṇṭapam, paḷḷiyantulā, paḷḷiyampalam mutaliya coṟkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum, paṭukki eṉṉum karutti aṭip paṭiyākak koṇṭē paḷḷi eṉṉuñ col muṟiyē vīṭu, kōyil, paḷḷikkūṭam mutaliya poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum. iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, ic colli vaṭamoḻiyil ataṉ eḻuttumuṟik kēṟpap palli eṉṟum pallī eṉṟut tirittuk koṇṭu pōtal allatu iyaṅkutal eṉṉum poruntāp poruḷi ataṉ vērpporuḷāka poruttik kāṭṭuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, palli eṉṉum ūruyirip peyariyum paḷḷi eṉṉuñ colloṭu mayakki, ataṉiyum vaṭacol leṉa ēmāṟṟuvatu. aṟivārāycci mikka iv irupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku eṭṭuṇiyum ēṟkumō? kaṇṭu teḷika kaṭukaḷavēṉuṅ karuttuṭiyār.  (tiruccirāppaḷḷit tamiḻc caṅkam, maṇiviḻā malar. 1958.)
coṟporuḷārāycci
ennāṭṭilum emmoḻiyilum potumakkaḷ vāyilāyt tōṉṟiya iyaṟkic coṟkaḷ ellām ovvōr poruḷik koṇṭaviyē.
itaṉālēyē.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē  (tol. col. 640)
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
taṉiccollellām poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē yeṉiṉ, at taṉic coṟkaḷālāṉa toṭarccoṟkaḷum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē yeṉpatu collāmalē peṟappaṭum. mēṉāṭṭāriyarum kīḻnāṭṭāriyarum pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē tattam moḻiccoṟkaṭku mūlam kaṇṭu vittirukka. iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, takka tuṇiyiruntum. kālṭuvel pōṉṟa ayalnāṭṭaṟiñar vaḻikāṭṭiyum tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ vēr kaḷiyum mūlattiyum tamiḻar kāṇātum kavaṉiyātumiruppatu peritum varuntat takkatākum. tiripiṉāluruvāṉa āriya moḻikaḷilum collārāycci payaṉpaṭuvatāyiṉ. iḷaṅ kuḻanti vāyiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉāṟ pōl iyalpāyeḻunta tamiḻmoḻiyil mikamikap payaṉpaṭumeṉpataṟku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli.
taṉiccol toṭarccol ākiya iruvakic coṟkaḷil, muṉṉataṟku vēr kāṇpatu aritāyiṉum piṉṉataṟku mūlaṅ kāṇpatu eḷiteṉṟē kūṟattakum. ati meyppikkum aḷa vākac cila toṭarccoṟkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟik kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ.
cīrtūkkal : cīr = tulittaṭṭu. tūkkal = tūkki niṟuttal. cīr tūkkal = carakkeṭiyi niṟuttaṟintāṟ pōlap poruḷ kaḷiṉ iyalpi eṇṇiyaṟital.
aravaṇittal : aravu = pāmpu. aṇittal = taḻuval. aravaṇittal = pāmpu piṉṉit taḻuviṉāṟpōla neruṅkak kaṭṭiyaṇittal, aṅṅaṉam aṇittāṟpōlap pātukāttal.
kaṭṭāyam : kaṭṭu = celuttu. āyam = vari. kaṭṭāyam = vari celuttutal pōṟ kaṇṭippu.
ērāḷam : ēr = kalappi. āḷ + am = āḷam = āḷiṉ taṉmi. ērāḷam = uḻavaṉ varuvāypōl mikutiyāyiruttal. uḻavaṉ nēraṭiyāyum mikutiyāyum uṇavupporuḷi viḷivittaliṉ poruḷ mikuti ērāḷameṉappaṭṭatu. am mikutiyiṉālēyē uḻavaṉ vēḷāṇmi ceytu vēḷāḷaṉāyiṉaṉ. vēḷāḷa ṉeṉpāṉ viruntirukka uṇṇātāṉ (tirikaṭu. 12).
tārāḷam : tār = cēṉi. āḷam = taṉmi. tārāḷam = paṭimaṟavar pōl. viruppam pōlap piṟar poruḷik kiyāṭutal allatu vaḻaṅkal. iṉṟum paṭimaṟavar piṟar poruḷi maṉampōṟ payaṉpaṭuttalum koḷḷi yaṭittalum kāṇka.
ciṅkiyaṭittal : ciṅki = tāḷam. aṭittal = taṭṭutal. ciṅki yaṭittal = irappōṉ. tāḷam taṭṭi irappatupōl tiṇṭāṭutal.
tāḷakkaruviyi uruvaka(rūpaka) tāḷattil taṭṭiṉāl ciṅcīyāṉ eṉṟa oliyeḻum. itaṉāl, tāḷakkaruvikkuc ciṅkiyeṉṟum cīyāṉ eṉṟum irupeyaruṇṭu.
tāyamāṭal : tāy + am = tāyam = tāyiṉiṉṟu peṟum urimi. āṭal = viḷiyāṭal. tāyamāṭal = cīṭṭup pōṭuvatupōl muttum palakaṟiyum pōṭṭu uṭaṉpiṟantār pākam pirittal; muttiṉālum palakaṟiyālum viḷiyāṭip piṟar urimiyip peṟutal allatu paṟittal; tāyamāṭik kālatti vīṇ pōkkuvatupōl vēli ceyyāmaṟ kālaṅ kaḻittal allatu tāḻttal; vēli tāyamāṭukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
miṉukkiṭutal: > miṉakkiṭutal > meṉakkiṭutal, miṉukkiṭutal = alaṅkarittal. alaṅkārattilēyē karuttāyiruntu vēliyik ki nekiḻa viṭal. vēli miṉakkiṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkiyum tēvaṭiyāḷ ciṅkārikkumuṉ tēr nilikku vantuviṭṭatu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyiyum nōkkuka.  - centamiḻc celvi cuṟavam 1943.
coṉṉam 
coṉṉam - cuvarṇa
col - coṉ - coṉṉam = poṉ (tivā.). coṉṉa kāraṉ = taṭṭāṉ (piṅ.).
coṉṉatāṉap payaṉeṉac colluvar  (kamparā. ciṟappu.)
vaṭavar cu + varṇa eṉṟu pakuttu naṉṉiṟa muḷḷatu eṉṟu poruṭ kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
iraṭṭitta ṉakaratti rṇa eṉṟu tirippatu vaṭavar marapu. e-ṭu: kaṉṉam - karṇa = kātu.  (va.va.168).
cōkāttal
cōkāttal ciṟiyil kāttiruttal. atu kaṉakavicayar eṉṉum āriya maṉṉar tamiḻ vēntarip paḻittatāl, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉiṉ cīṟṟattiṟ kāḷākic ciṟippaṭṭatu pōlvatu.
cō eṉpatu ariya poṟikaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭa ciṟanta araṇ.
aḵtu irāvaṇaṉiṉ ilaṅkāpuriyilum vāṇaṉiṉ cōṇita purattilum iruntatu.  (ti.ma. 99).
cōṭi
cōṭi - cōṣa
cuṭu - cūṭu - cōṭu - cōṭi. cōṭittal = kāytal, vaṟṟutal (caṅ. aka.).
cōṭu - cōṭi = vaṟaṭci.
vaṭavar cuṣ eṉpati mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. atu cuḷ eṉpataṉ tiripu. (va.va. 168).
cōmpal vaki
neṭunīr oru viṉiyi nīṭṭittuc ceytal; maṟavi oru viṉiyi maṟantu viṭutal; maṭi oru viṉiyuñ ceyyātu iruttal : tuyil oru viṉiyuñ ceyyātu tūṅkutal.  (col. 60)
cōmpu
cōmpu - vap (i.vē.)
cum = amiti, oṉṟuñceyyāmi, cōmpal.
cummāyiruttal = amitiyāyiruttal, viṉiceyyātiruttal.
cintiyi yaṭakkiyē cummāyirukkiṉṟa tiṟamaritu  (tāyu. tēcō.)
cum - cumpu - cūmpu = cōmpal. cūmpu - cōmpu.
cōmputal = viṉiceyyātiruttal, cuṟucuṟuppiṉmi, kāla nīṭṭittal, tūṅka virumputal.
o.nō: tūṅkutal = uṟaṅkutal, kālantāḻttu viṉiceytal mantamātal.
vap = tūṅku, kaṉavukāṇ.
L. somnus. Slav supati, Lith sapnus, AS swef, Gk hupnos = sleep.  (va.va. 168)
cōḻar veṟṟic ceyalkaḷ
naḷiyiru munnīr nāvā yōṭṭi
vaḷitoḻi lāṇṭa vuravōṉ maruka  (puṟam. 66)
eṉṟu, cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉi veṇṇik kuyattiyār pāṭiyi ruppatāl, avaṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉ āḻ kaṭal kaṭantu mīṇṭami aṟiyappaṭum. vaḷitoḻilāḷva teṉpatu, paruvakkāṟṟaṟintu ataṉ vākkiṟ kalañceluttutal.
oru paruntiṉāl turattappaṭṭa puṟā, cempiyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉ kālaṭiyil vīḻntatu. avaṉ atik kāttaṟkum paruntiṉ paciyit tīrttaṟkum, taṉ uṭampiṉiṉṟu ap puṟāvaḷavu taciyaṟuttup paruntiṟ kiṭṭāṉ. icceyti piṉṉart tolkati muṟiyil virivākkappaṭṭatu.
nilamici vāḻna ralamaral tīrat
teṟukatirk kaṉali vemmi tāṅkik
kāluṇa vākac cuṭaroṭu koṭkum
avircaṭi muṉivaru maruḷak koṭuñciṟik
kūrukirp paruntiṉ ēṟukuṟit toḻīit
taṉṉakam pukka kuṟunaṭip puṟaviṉ
taputi yañcic cīri pukka
variyā vīki yuravōṉ maruka.  (puṟam. 43)
eṉṟu cempiyaṉ vaḻivanta cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi tampi mā vaḷattāṉ viḷikkappaṭṭami kāṇka. cempiyaṉ peyar. avaṉukkup piṟpaṭṭa cōḻarkku oru kuṭippeyarāyiṟṟu.
akkālattil ilaṅkiyil arakkar eṉṟum iyakkar eṉṟum iruvakuppār iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. avar māyakkaliyil vallavarāyiruntataṉāl, piṟkālattil, āriyat tolkatiñar, avari vāṉiyaṅkum allatu makkaḷiṉattiṟku appāṟpaṭṭa patiṉeṇ kaṇattāruḷ iru kaṇattārākak koṇṭaṉar pōlum! arakkar aracaṉ irāvaṇṇaṉ. avaṉ talinakar ilaṅki. iyakkar aracaṉ piṅkalaṉ (kupēraṉ). avaṉ tali nakar aḷaki. avaṉ māperuñ celvaṉ. caṅkam tāmari eṉṉum pēreṇkaḷiṉ aḷavukoṇṭa iru pokkacam (niti) īṭṭi vittiruntatākac collappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. iru eṉṉum colliṟkup peru eṉṟum iraṇṭu eṉṟum poruḷuṇṭu. accol niti eṉṉum vaṭa collit taḻuvumpōtu, iṭiyil makaramey tōṉṟātātalāl, iru poruṭkum potuvāka niṟkum. ataṉāl, iru vēṟu pokkacam eṉṟu tolkatiñar koṇṭirukkalām. uṇmiyil iruvēṟu pokkacamāyiṉ, oṉṟu mūlapaṇṭāramākavum iṉṉoṉṟu vaḻaṅkum paṇṭāramākavum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
arakkarukkum iyakkarukkum neṭunāṭ pakiyiruntu vantatu. iṟutiyil mūṇṭa kaṭupōril, iyakkar kulam vēraṟukkap paṭṭatu. piṅkalaṉ tappip paṉimalikku ōṭippōy viṭṭāṉ. piṟkālattil ōr iyakki (iyakkap peṇ) tamiḻakattil nāṭṭup puṟat teyvamum āṉāḷ.
puṟañciṟi mūtūrp pūṅkaṇ iyakkikkup
pāṉmaṭi koṭuttup paṇpiṟ peyarvōḷ
āyar mutumakaḷ mātari yeṉpōḷ  (cilap. 116-118)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka.
ilaṅki, toṉṟu toṭṭu, marumam mikka aruṅkaṭṭaṭa viṉikaṭkuc ciṟantatā yiruntu vantirukkiṉṟatu.
mānti (mātōṭṭam) eṉṉu miṭattil, irumpiṉāṟ ceyta kāntamali eṉṉum kāntak kōṭṭi yiruntatākavum, akkōṭṭi yiluḷḷār aṇmi yiṟ cellum kappalkaḷi yellām kāntattā liḻuttuk koḷḷi yaṭittatākavum, māntippaḷḷu, vicuvapurāṇam, vicuvakarma nāṭakam eṉṉum paṉuvalkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅki kaṭṭaḻitta  (cilap. āyc. paṭark. 1)
eṉpataṉāl, āreyil muḻumuta laraṇam oṉṟu aṅkirunta tākat terikiṉṟatu.
acurar eṉṟu tolkatiñar kūṟum ilaṅki arakkar, antarattil toṅkumāṟu mūṉṟu araṇāṉa kōṭṭikaḷi amittu iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟi oru cōḻaṉ aḻittu, tūṅkeyil eṟinta toṭittōṭ cempiyaṉ eṉṉum virutup peyar peṟṟāṉ.
oṉṉā ruṭkun tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟal
tūṅkeyi leṟintaniṉ ūṅkaṇōr  (puṟam. 39)
eṉpatu cempiyaṉ ceyali avaṉ piṉṉōrmēl ēṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
tūṅkeyil mūṉṟeṟinta cōḻaṉkāṇ ammāṉi (cilap. 29:19 vīṅkunīr) eṉpatum atuvē.
oṉṉār, ōṅkeyiṟ katavam urumuccuval coṟiyum tūṅkeyi leṟinta toṭi viḷaṅku taṭakki nāṭā nallici naṟṟērc cempiyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi (79-82).
cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē - viḷiyāṭṭu
mutiyār oruvar oru kuḻantiyiṉ kiyi akaṅki mēṉōkkap piṭittukkoṇṭu, ataṉ aintuviralkaḷiyum ovvoṉṟāka muṟiyē toṭṭu itu aiyāviṟku, itu ammāviṟku, itu aṇṇaṉukku, itu akkāviṟku, itu uṉakku, eṉṟu kūṟi, av vakaṅkiyiṟ paruppuk kaṭivatupōl tam kiyāl tēyttu, apparuppip palarkkum pakirvatupōṟ kiyāl naṭittukkāṭṭi, piṉpu mīṇṭum paruppuk kaṭintu, cōṟu koṇṭupōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉṟu collik koṇṭu tam kiyi ak kuḻantiyiṉ akkuḷḷavari mella iḻuttuc ceṉṟu, kiccuk kiccu eṉṟu collik kiccaṅ kāṭṭuvar. itu kuḻantikku makiḻcciyiyum cirippiyum uṇṭupaṇṇum.
paruppuk kaṭivatupōṟ ceyyumpōtu, paruppuk kaṭintu paruppuk kaṭintu eṉṟum, paruppip pakirvatupōṟ ceyyum pōtu, intā uṉakku, intā uṉakku eṉṟum collappaṭum.
vaḻakkamāyk kūḻuṅ kañciyum uṇpavar, cōṟu koṇṭu pōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpatiṟkup patilāka, kañci koṇṭu pōkiṟa vaḻiyilē eṉpar. ataṉāl avar attoṭarālēyē ivviḷiyāṭṭik kuṟippar.
tolivāṉa iṭattil vēli ceyyum aṇṇaṉukkup paruppum cōṟum koṇṭupōkum taṅkiyi, oruvar kiccuk kāṭṭiya viṉiyi iv viḷiyāṭṭuk kuṟikkumpōlum!  (ta.nā.vi.)



ñālamuntiya nili
ñālam eṉpatu makkaḷ vāḻum ulakamākiya immānilam, pūmi eṉṉum vaṭacolli vēṇṭātu vaḻaṅkiyataṉāl, ñālam eṉṉum teṉcol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. teṉ col leṉiṉum tamiḻc colleṉiṉum okkum.
ñālanilappākam iṉṟuḷḷavāṟu aintu kaṇṭaṅkaḷākavum āyirakkaṇak kāṉa tīvukaḷākavum toṉṟu toṭṭu irunta tilli. oru kālattil atu kāṇṭavaṉam (Gondwana), aṅkāram (Angara), pālatikkam (Baltica), amacōṉiyam (Amazonia) eṉṟa nāṟperu nilaṅkaḷākavum oru cila tīvukaḷākavum pakuntiruntatu. kāṇṭavaṉam āppirikkāviyum kaṭakat tiruppattiṟkut (Tropic of Cancer) teṟkiluḷḷa intiyāviyum ātirēliyā viyum, aṅkāram āciyāviṉ vaṭakīḻp perumpakutiyiyum, pālatikkam vaṭa amerikkāviṉ vaṭakīḻp pakutiyiyum kirīṉlāntu eṉṉum pintīviyum airōppāviṉ teṉpakuti yiyum, amacōṉiyam teṉṉamerikkāviyum, tammuṭ koṇ ṭiruntaṉa. arapik kaṭalum vaṅkāḷak kuṭāk kaṭalum aṉ ṟilli. intumāvāriyiṉ perumpakutiyum attilāntikka māvāriyiṉ vaṭapakutiyum nilamāyiruntaṉa. naṇṇilakkaṭal āciyāvi ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu amitimā vāriyoṭu cērntiruntatu. ataṉāl, paṉimalittoṭar (imayam) aṉṟu kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyiruntatu (V.R. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar eḻutiya varalāṟṟu muṉṉit teṉṉintiyāviṉ (Pre-Historic South India) mukappup paṭattip pārkka)
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tīvam eṉṉun teṉcollit tvīpa eṉṟu tirittu, irupuṟamum nīrāl cūḻappaṭṭatu eṉap poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟuvar. tīvu eṉpatu nāṟpuṟamum nīrāl cūḻappaṭṭa nilappakutiyē.
ovvoru tīvum nilittiṇiyāl (tāvarattāl) niṟintu oru māperuñ cōlipōl tōṉṟiyataṉāl, poḻil eṉavum paṭṭatu.
nāvalantīvu, iṟalittīvu, ilavantīvu, aṉṟiltīvu, kucittīvu, teṅkantīvu, tāmarittīvu eṉpaṉa eḻutīvukaḷ.
nāvalan tīvē iṟalit tīvē
kuciyiṉ tīvē kiravuñcat tīvē
cāṉmalit tīvē teṅkiṉ tīvē
puṭkarat tīvē eṉattī vēḻē
ēḻperun tīvum ēḻpoḻi leṉappaṭum.
eṉpatu tivākaram. kiravuñcam, cāṉmali, puṭkaram eṉpaṉa, muṟiyē, aṉṟil, ilavam, tāmari eṉpavaṟṟiṉ moḻi peyarppāṉa vaṭacollām. nāval eṉpatic campu eṉa moḻi peyarttaṉar vaṭavar. aṉṟil eṉpatu oru paṟavi vaki.
teṅkattīvu eṉpatiyē eḻutīvukaḷuḷ oṉṟākat tivākaramum piṅkalamum cūṭāmaṇiyum kūṟiyiruppavum, ataṟkup pakaramākat tēkkantīvu eṉa oṉṟik kuṟittatōṭu, atip piṅkala meṉṟuṅ kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali. teṅkan tīvu eṉpatit tēkkantīvu eṉat tavaṟākap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, ataṟkēṟpac cākatvīpa eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil tavaṟāka moḻipeyarttatic carippaṭutta vēṇṭic ceṉṉi yakaramutali avvaḻiyi mēṟkoṇṭatu pōlum! āriyar kumarināṭṭut tamiḻarkkuk kālattāl mikamikap piṟpaṭṭa varātaliṉ, eḻutīvuk karuttit tamiḻilakkiyat tiṉiṉṟē koṇṭi ruttal vēṇṭum.
tīvu eṉṉum peyar, nāvalantīvu āppirikkāviṉiṉṟum pirintupōṉa niliyik kāṭṭum.
takkaṇam
takkaṇam - takṣiṇa (i.vē.)
takku = tāḻvu. takkut toṇṭi = tāḻnta kural. 
takkilē pāṭukiṟār eṉṉum vaḻakki nōkkuka.
eccu (uyarvu) x takku.
vaṭatici paṉimaliyāl uyarntiruttalāl uttaram eṉap peṟṟatu.
“Uttara, ... northern (becuse the northern part of India is high)” eṉṟu mā. vi.a. kūṟutal. kāṇka.
takku - takkaṇam = vaṭaticiyi nōkkat tāḻntirukkum teṉṟici.
kiḻakku nōkkumpōtu teṉṟici valamum vaṭatici iṭamu miruppatāl, takkaṇam eṉṉum coṟku valaticip poruḷum uttaram eṉṉum coṟku iṭaticip poruḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
valamākac cuṟṟivarutal piratakṣiṇam eṉṟu collappaṭutal kāṇka.  (va.va. 169-170).
takkayākap paraṇi
takkayākap paraṇiyil
vaṭacollāṭci vakikaḷ
1.	iṇintiyal meykaḷ : e.ṭu. cakrapāṇi, vacramāli, patrakāḷi, mantram.
2.	meymmutaṟ coṟkaḷ : e.ṭu. tricūlam, nrupatīpaṉ, prantam.
3.	toṭarc coṟkaḷ : e.ṭu. nūpurātāracāraṇi, varkkat vātacātipar.  (ta.i.va. 42).
takkōlam
takkōlam - kakkōla
takkōlam = oru naṟumaṇaccarakku ka. takkōla.
takkōlam pañcavācam eṉṟum aimmaṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum muppat tiruvaki ōmālikikaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum kūṟappeṟum.
takkōlan tīmpūt takicā lilavaṅkaṅ
kappūrañ cātiyō ṭintu  (cilap. 6:77, uri).  (va.va. 168-169.)
taka
taka-tah - (d) i.vē
taḷataḷattal = viḷaṅkutal.
pacu narampu taḷataḷappa  (kuṟṟā. tala. tarumacāmi. 34).
taḷa - taḻa - taḻal. taḻalutal = 1. aḻalutal.
taḻaṉṟeri kuṇṭam  (tiruviḷi. nāka. 6) 2. viḷaṅkutal.
taḻalun tāmariyāṉoṭu  (tēvā. 1215:27).
taḻa - taka. o.nō: maḻa - maka. takatakaveṉac colikkiṟatu eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
taka - takam = erivu, cūṭu. takam - taṅkam = viḷaṅkum poṉ.
taka - tikaḻ - tiṅkaḷ - nilā. o. nō: tamiḻar - tikuḷar (ka.).
taka - taku - taki = tākam. takam - tākam - nīrvēṭki.  (va.va. 169).
taṭṭāṅkal viḷiyāṭṭu
perumpāṉmi iruvarum ciṟupāṉmi palarumāṉa makaḷir ciṟu kaṟkaḷik kiyāl taṭṭippiṭikkum viḷiyāṭṭu, taṭṭāṅkal. itu paṇṭik kālattil kaḻaṅku koṇṭu āṭappaṭṭatiṉāl, kaḻaṅku eṉa vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaḻaṅkāvatu kaḻaṟkāy allatu kaḻaṟcik kāy. kaḻaṟcik kāy eṉpatu iṉṟu keccakkāy eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ceṟiyaric cilampiṟ kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir
polañcey kaḻaṅkiṟ ṟeṟṟi yāṭum  (puṟam. 36)
makaḷir ... muttavārmaṇaṟ
poṟkaḻaṅ kāṭum  (perumpāṇ. 327-35)
taṭṭāṅkal kīḻvarumāṟu palavakippaṭum. aviyaṉittum vīṭṭuḷḷum vīṭṭumuṟṟattilum viḷiyāṭap peṟum.
I. mūṉṟāṅkal
āṭṭiṉ peyar : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu mūṉṟāṅkal.
āṭu muṟi : mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟik kīḻvittu ēṉiyiraṇ ṭiyum kiyil vittuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu iṉṉoṉṟik kīḻvittu muntik kīḻvittati eṭuttukkoṇṭu, mēleṟintatip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṉpu mīṇṭum oṉṟi mēleṟintu, kiyiluḷḷatik kīḻvittuk kīḻiruntati eṭuttuk koṇṭu mēleṟintatip piṭittal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟē paṉṉirumuṟi toṭarntu tavaṟātu āṭiviṭiṉ, paḻamām. paṉṉirumuṟikkum kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum.
(1)	oṉṟāvatu oṉṟāṅkāy.
(2) 	iraṇṭāvatu irattiṉakkiḷi (allatu īccaṅkāy).
(3) 	mūṉṟāvatu muttuccaram.
(4) 	nālāvatu nāṟkāli.
(5) 	añcāvatu pañcavarṇam.
(6) 	āṟāvatu pālāṟu.
(7) 	ēḻāvatu eḻuttāṇi.
(8) 	eṭṭāvatu koṭṭāram.
(9) 	oṉpatāvatu ōlippū.
(10) 	pattāvatu paṉaṅkoṭṭi.
(11) 	patiṉoṉṟāvatu teṉṉam piḷḷi.
(12) 	teṉṉimarat taṭiyilē tērōṭum piḷḷiyār.
orutti āṭumpōtu tavaṟiviṭiṉ, aṭuttavaḷ āṭal vēṇṭum. āṭiṉavaḷ maṟumuṟiyāṭumpōtu, mīṇṭum mutaliliruntē āṭal vēṇṭum.
II. aintāṅkal (oru vaki)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : aintu kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu āṭum āṭṭu aintāṅkal.
āṭu muṟi : muntiyāṭupavaḷ, inta āṭṭiṟkuriya aintu kaṟkaḷiyum oruṅkē citaṟi, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi eṭuttu mēlēpōṭṭuk, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyeṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittalvēṇṭum. piṉpu, kiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟi mēlē pōṭṭuppōṭṭu, ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟi yeṭuttuk koṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu mīṇṭuñ citaṟi oru kalliyeṭuttu mēleṟintu, kīḻiruppavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭi eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ, kiyiliruppavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu, kīḻirukkum ēṉiyiraṇṭiyum eṭuttuk koṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam aintumuṟi citaṟip piṭikkum pōtu, mūṉṟām muṟi oṉṟum mūṉṟumākavum, nālām muṟi nāliyum oruṅkēyum, aintām muṟi mūṉṟum oṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kāykaḷi eṭuttal vēṇṭum. immuṟikaḷ muṟiyē, oṉṟāṅ koṭṭi, iraṇṭāṅkoṭṭi, mūṉṟāṅkoṭṭi, nālāṅkoṭṭi, aintāṅkoṭṭi eṉa avvavviṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
ataṉpiṉ nālu kaṟkaḷik kikkuḷ vittuk koṇṭu, oru kalli iru taṭavi mēlē pōṭṭup piṭittal vēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavi āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttuk (it toṭar cēlattil kōḻippīyām eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.) kōḻi kokkām eṉṟum, iraṇṭām taṭavi kuttukkiyāl nilattiṟ kuttik kuttuviḷakkām eṉṟum, mēleṟinta kallip piṭikku muṉ colla vēṇṭum. piṉpum, avvāṟoru kalli iru taṭavi mēleṟintu piṭittal vēṇṭum. mutaltaṭavi piṭikkumuṉ ēṉi nālukaṟkaḷiyum kīḻē vittu vittu eṭuppōm eṉṟum, maṟutaṭavi piṭikkumuṉ annāṉkiyum vārikkoṇṭu vārikkoṇṭōm eṉṟum, colla vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, iru kikaḷiyum cērttuk kūṭṭukkiyāka vittuk koṇṭu, aiṅkallum kīḻē viḻātavāṟu, tappu tāḷam talivali mōḷam eṉṟu nāṟcīrpaṭac collik koṇṭu, valakkiyip puṟaṅkiyum akaṅkiyum puṟaṅkiyum akaṅkiyumāka iru taṭavi puraṭṭi vittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, aiṅkalliyum mēleṟintu piṭittu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟic cokkal vēṇṭum. cokkutalāvatu cilupputal.
piṉpu aiṅkalliyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri piṭikkaccoṉṉa kallip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittukkāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kāṭṭiyapiṉ atit taṉiyāy eṭuttu vittuviṭṭu, ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu mūṉṟik kīḻē vittuviṭṭup piṭittu, mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu kīḻē vitta kalli vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum; allatu, valak kuṭaṅkiyiluḷḷa nāṟkalkaḷiyum ivviraṇṭāka iru taṭavi caṟṟē mēleṟintu, avaṟṟip puṟammēṉōkkiya iṭakkuṭaṅkiyāl uṭaṉuṭaṉ piṭittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉañ ceyyiṉ paḻamām.
orutti āṭumpōtu, mēleṟintu piṭikkuṅ kal tavaṟiṉālum, kīḻirukkuṅ kalli yeṭukkumpōtu piṟakallit toṭṭu viṭṭālum, avaḷ niṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ aṭuttavaḷ āṭuvāḷ. orutti orē āṭṭiyil maṟumuṟi allatu vaḻimuṟi āṭumpōtu, muṉpu viṭṭatiliruntu āṭuvāḷ. ivi ellāvakikkum potuvām.
āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, veṉṟavaḷ tōṟṟavaḷiṉ kikaṭkiṭiyil oru kalli vittu, mēṟkimēl mūṉṟutaṭavi kuttuvatu vaḻakkam. itu ellā vakikkum potu.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : kiyum kinarampum inta āṭṭāl uram peṟum. ituvum potuvām.
aintāṅkal (maṟṟoru vaki)
nālāṅ koṭṭikkuppiṉ, aiṅkalliyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉṉatiṟpōl nāṉkutaṭavi mēlē pōṭṭup pōṭṭu ati ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻirukkuṅ kalli ovvoṉṟāy iṭappakkamākac caṟṟut taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, maṟupaṭiyum ellāvaṟṟiyum kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉpōṟ palataṭavi mēleṟintu, ati muṟpaṭṭa ovvoru taṭaviyum kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ nīṅkiyiruppavaṟṟi ovvoṉ ṟāka neruṅka vittup piṭittu, iṟutiyil kīḻiruppavaṟṟi yellām oruṅkē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ iru pātaṅkaḷiyum kūṭṭivittu, avaṟṟiṉmēl mūlik koṉṟāka nāṉmūlikkum nālu kal vittu, ēṉiyoṉṟi nāṉkutaṭavi mēleṟintu, ati ovvoru taṭaviyum pātaṅkaḷiṉ mēluḷḷa kalli ovvoṉṟāy irupāta iṭikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṟaku mīṇṭum, aiṅkalliyuṅ kīḻiṭṭu avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi muṉpōl nāṉkutaṭavi mēleṟintu, ati ovvoru taṭaviyum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷi ovvoṉṟāy iṭappuṟam nilattiṟ pottic caṟṟē tiṟantu vikkappaṭṭirukkum iṭakkuṭaṅkikkuḷ taḷḷit taḷḷip piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, aiṅkalliyum pōṭṭup puṟaṅkimēl tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ etiri cuṭṭiyatip piṟavaṟṟuṭaṉ mēleṟintu piṭittukkāṭṭi, atit taṉiyē eṭuttuvittuviṭṭu, ēṉi nāṉkaṉuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu, mūṉṟik kīḻē vittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟi vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. atōṭu paḻam.
III. ēḻāṅkal (oruvaki)
ēḻāṅkallil ivvaki ēṟattāḻa aintāṅkalliṉ mutalvaki pōṉṟatē.
ēḻu kalliyum uruṭṭi avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyeṭuttu mēleṟintu oṉṟāṅkoṭṭi mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭi variyāṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅ koṭṭiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇ ṭāṅ koṭṭiyil ivviraṇṭāka vum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭṭiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅkoṭṭiyil iraṇṭum nālumākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅ koṭṭiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅ koṭṭiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkap peṟum.
piṉpu, muṟiyē, iḻuttal kuttal vittal vāral nāṉkum tappu - tāḷam - talivali - mōḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum.
ataṉpiṉ, ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum mēleṟintu puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅka vēṇṭum. mūṉṟu kalmaṭṭum puṟaṅki mēl niṟpiṉ, avi kāṭṭāṉ karuṅkal eṉak kīḻē pōṭappaṭum. ataṟku mēluṅ kīḻum niṟpiṉ cokka vēṇṭum.
mīṇṭum ellāvaṟṟiyum muṉpōṟ puṟaṅkiyil tāṅki, etiri cuṭṭiyatip piṭittukkāṭṭit taṉiyāka vittuviṭṭu, eñciya vaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu aintik kīḻvittup piṭittu, mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu kīḻuḷḷavaṟṟi vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum; piṭittu viṭiṉ paḻam.
ēḻāṅkal (maṟṟoru vaki)
oru kalli vittukkoṇṭu eñciya āṟu kalliyum uruṭṭi oṉṟāṅkoṭṭi mutal ēḻāṅkoṭṭi vari āṭal vēṇṭum. oṉṟāṅ koṭṭiyil ovvoṉṟākavum, iraṇṭāṅ koṭṭiyil ivviraṇṭāka vum, mūṉṟāṅkoṭṭiyil mummūṉṟākavum, nālāṅkoṭṭiyil nālum iraṇṭu mākavum, aintāṅkoṭṭiyil aintum oṉṟumākavum, āṟāṅ koṭṭiyil āṟum orēyaṭiyākavum, ēḻāṅkoṭṭiyil oṉṟum iraṇṭum mūṉṟumākavum, kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ eṭukkappaṭum.
kīḻirunteṭukkuṅ kaṟkaḷi orē kikkuḷ aṭakka iyalātār, irukiyi yum payaṉpaṭuttik koḷvatu muṇṭu. āyiṉ, itu attuṇic ciṟappiṉataṉṟu; aṉivarālum oppuk koḷḷap paṭṭatumaṉṟu;
kīḻirukkuṅ kaṟkaḷ tūrattūra iruntāl, kikkallip pakkattil vittu viṭṭut toliviluḷḷa kalli eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
ēḻukoṭṭikkum piṉvarumāṟu pāṭṭup pāṭappaṭum.
1. 	poṟukki ciṟukki pō(ki)ṟāḷām taṇṇīrkkut taṇṇīrk kuṭameṭuttu.
2. 	(allatu, vēṟu ētēṉumoṉṟu). iraṇṭu irumpu, ēḻaṭik karumpu.
3.	mūṉṟu mukkōṭu, murukaṉ ceṅkōṭu.
4. 	nāṉku naṭalam, tēṅkāyp puṭalam.
5. 	aivar arikkum mañcaḷ tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ
6. 	ākkūr aṭivāḻi, aṇṇaṉ tampi peruvāḻi.
7. 	ēḻaṇṇaṉ kāṭṭilē, eṅkaḷaṇṇaṉ rōṭṭilē, mañcaḷ cāraṭṭilē.
iṉṉoru pāṭṭu piṉvarumāṟu :
1.	tūppoṟukki tūtuḷaṅkāy
	māppoṟukki mātuḷaṅkāy
	kal poṟukki kaṭāraṅkāy.
2.	īr īrttikkoḷ
	pūp paṟittukkoḷ
	peṭṭiyil vittukkoḷ.
3.	mukkōṇa vācalilē
	muttuttaṭṭup pantalilē.
4.	nāṉku ṭōṅku ṭammāram
	nāṅkaḷāṭum pamparam.
	(allatu)
	nāṉku ṭōṅku
	nāluveṟṟili vāṅku
5.	aivar arikkum mañcaḷ
	tēvar kuḷikkum mañcaḷ.
6.	kūṟu kūṟu cittappā
	kuṟukkē vanta periyappā.
7.	ēḻi eṇṇik koḷ
	eṇṇey maram cērttukkoḷ
	peṇṇi aḻittuk koḷ.
(ippāṭṭiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷiṉ kocci vaṭivam tiruttap peṟṟuḷatu). ēḻāṅkoṭṭikkup piṉ, oru kiyil mukkallum iṉṉoru kiyil nāṟkallumāka vittuk koṇṭu, nāṟ kalluḷ oṉṟi mēleṟintu eñciya irumūṉṟiyuṅ kīḻ vittu mēleṟinta kallip piṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum ati mēleṟintu atik kīḻvitta iru mūṉṟiyum irukiyālum vārikkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum.
itu ciṟuputi eṉappaṭum. iti āṭumpōtu pāṭum pāṭṭu puti putikkiṟa pamparam, ceṭṭi citamparam eṉpatākum.
itaṉpiṉ, irukiyilum mummūṉṟu kalli vittuk koṇṭu, ēṉiyoṉṟi mēleṟintu, āṭkāṭṭi viralāl nilattil iḻuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum.
piṉpu, oru kalli mēleṟintu āṟukallik kīḻ vittup piṭitta piṉ, mīṇṭum oṉṟi mēleṟintu ēṉi āṟiyum oruṅ kē vārip piṭittal vēṇṭum. itu perumputi eṉappaṭum.
itiyaṭuttut tappu - tāḷam - talivali - mēḷam nāṉkum nikaḻum. piṉṉar oru kallik kīḻiṭa, ati etiri eṭuttuk koṭuttal vēṇṭum. itu paḻattiṉ oppakkuṟiyām.
IV. pala nāloru kal
oṉpatum patiṉmūṉṟum patiṉēḻum irupattoṉṟum pōl pala nāloṭu oṉṟu cērnta kaṟkaḷi mēleṟintu, puṟaṅkiyil tāṅkip piṭikkumaḷavu vittuk koṇṭu mikutiyik kīḻiṭṭu viṭṭu, puṟaṅkiyiluḷḷavaṟṟi mēlēpōṭṭu akaṅkiyiṟ piṭittu, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu nannāṉkāy iṭakkiyāṟ piṭittuk kiḻē nannāṉkāy vittal vēṇṭum. ivvakiyiṟ perum pālum oru nāṉkittāṉ piṭittal kūṭum.
piṉpu, valakkiyiluḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oru kalli mēleṟintu atik kīḻē yuḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiyō palavaṟṟiyō eṭuttukkoṇṭu piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam kīḻē kalluḷḷavari (allatu tavaṟum vari) tirumpat tirumpa āṭavēṇṭum. kiyiṟ palakaṟkaḷ cērntuviṭṭāl, uṭaṉē iṭakkiyāl oru nāṉki allatu pala nāṉkip piṭittu nannāṉkāyk kīḻē vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum piṭittu nan nāṉkāyk kīḻē vittapiṉ, iṟutiyil eñciyuḷḷa oṟṟikkalli mēlēpōṭṭup puṟaṅkiyil tāṅki, ati mīṇṭum mēleṟintu nilattit toṭṭu, atu kīḻēviḻumuṉ atik kiyālaḻutti nērē vīḻtti mūṭiviṭal vēṇṭum. itu amukkutal allatu mūṭutal eṉappaṭum. itōṭu oru paḻamām.
mutalāvatu puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅkumpōtu ellāk kaṟkaḷiyum kīḻē viṭṭu viṭṭālum, piṭikkumpōtu kal tavaṟiṉālum, nāṉkāy allatu nannāṉkāyp piṭikkum pōtu kūṭak kuṟiyap piṭipaṭṭālum, kīḻuḷḷa kalli eṭukkumpōtu maṟṟakkal aluṅkiṉālum, āṭṭam niṉṟuviṭum. piṉpu aṭuttavaḷ āṭa vēṇṭum.
orē āṭṭiyil, aṭuttavaḷ āṭiṉālum, āṭiṉavaḷē maṟumuṟi āṭiṉālum nannāṉkāyp piṭittu vikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷi viṭṭuviṭṭu maṟṟak kaṟkaḷik koṇṭutāṉ āṭavēṇṭum. orutti kaṭicik kalli amukkumpōtu tavaṟippōy aṭuttavaḷ atic cariyāy amukkiviṭṭāl, avaḷukkuttāṉ paḻam.
V. paṉṉirukal
paṉṉiru kaṟkaḷi mēleṟintu avaṟṟip puṟaṅkiyil tāṅki, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟimaṭṭum iruviraṟ kiṭiyil iṭukkik koṇṭu ēṉiyavaṟṟik kīḻē viṭṭu viṭṭu, avaṟṟi ovvoṉṟākavō ivviraṇṭākavō mum mūṉṟākavō, oṉṟum palavumākavō, vēṟiru viralāl iṭukkip piṭittuk kīḻē vittu ellāvaṟṟiyum piṭittapiṉ puṟaṅkiyiluḷḷati amukki, atiyum maṟṟavaṟṟoṭu cērttu mummūṉṟāka nāṟkūṟiṭṭu, ovvoṉṟiṉiṉṟum ovvoru kalli eṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. ikkūṟukaṭku uṭṭikaḷ eṉṟu peyar nāluṭṭiyiṉiṉṟum ovvoru kalli nīkkiyapiṉ, eṭṭuk kal eñci niṟkum. avveṭṭiyum muṉpōṉṟē āṭi, mīṇṭum mummūṉṟāka uṭṭi vittu ovvoru kalli nīkkiyapiṉ, āṟu kal eñciniṟkum. ivvāṟē toṭarntu āṭiṉ, iṟutiyil irukal eñcum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi mēlē yeṟintu iṉ ṉoṉṟik kīḻē vittuppiṭittu, piṉpu mīṇṭum ati mēlēyeṟintu kīḻēvittati eṭuttup piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam mummuṟi ceytapiṉ, mēleṟinta kalli, mūṉṟu taṭavi ciluppiyum, mūṉṟu taṭavi mēleṟintu nilantoṭṭup piṭittum, piṉṉum mūṉṟu taṭavi mēleṟintu nilamum mārpun toṭṭum piṭittum, muṭippiṉ paḻamām.
cilupputalāvatu, akaṅkiyiluḷḷatip puṟaṅkiyiliṭṭu veṭṭippiṭittal.
āṭumpōtu tavaṟum vakiyum, ataṉpiṉ nikaḻuñ ceytiyum, muṟkūṟiyaviyē.
VI. palakal
oṉpatu mutal irupattintuvari oṟṟippaṭiyāṉa ētēṉum oru tokik kaṟkaḷi, mēlē pōṭṭup puṟaṅkiyāl tāṅki orukal tavira maṟṟavaṟṟi yellāṅ kīḻē pōṭṭuviṭṭu, avvoru kalli mēlēyeṟintu uḷḷaṅkiyāṟ piṭittu, ati mīṇṭum mēlēyeṟintu, kīḻē kiṭakkuṅ kaṟkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu eṉa iraṭṭippaṭiyāka eṭuttakkoṇṭu, mēlēyeṟinta kalliyum piṭittal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ovvōr eṭuppiṟkum, muṉṉum piṉṉum, oru kalli mēleṟitalum atip piṭittalum muṟiyē nikaḻum.
irukal eṭuppiṉ kāy; nāṉku āṟu eṭṭu āyiṉ paḻam. paḻakkaṟ kaḷellāvaṟṟiyum taṉ paṅkil vittuk koṇṭu, kāykkaṟkaḷiṟ pātiyi viḷiyāṭṭiṟ pōṭṭu viṭalvēṇṭum. āṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, kūṭutalāṉa kaṟkaḷip piṭittiruppavaḷ kelittavaḷāvaḷ.
piṟa iyalpukaḷum ceytikaḷum muṟkūṟiyaviyē.
VII. patiṉāṟāṅkal
patiṉāṟu kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu āṭuvatu patiṉāṟāṅkal. itu palavakiyāy āṭappeṟum. vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaṭṭārattār iti āṭuvar.
taṭṭu
taṭṭu-taṭ(d)taṭṭutal=aṭittal,puṭittal,tākkutal.  (va.va. 170).
taṭam
taṭam - taṭa (t|)
taṭam = vaḷivu, vaḷintakari, karicūḻnta nīrnili.
taṭaveṉ kiḷavi kōṭṭamuñ ceyyum  (tol. 804)
taṭam - taṭākam. taṭam - taṭṭam. taṭṭattu nīrilē tāmari (tiruman. 2904)  (va.va.170).
taṇṭam
taṇṭam - taṇṭa (i.vē.)
taḷ - taḷam = kaṉam. te. taḷamu. 2. kūṭṭam. 3. paṭi. taḷam - taṭam = perumi, akalam. taṭam - taṭa - taṭā - taṭavu.
taḷ - tāḷ = 1. tiraṇṭa aṭittaṇṭu. tāṇeṭuṅ kuvaḷi (cīvaka. 2802).
2.	aṭittaṇṭu pōl tāṅkuṅ kāl.
eṇkuṇattāṉ tāḷi vaṇaṅkāt tali  (kuṟaḷ. 9)
3. 	maliyaṭi. tāḷvarip puṟattu (tiruvālavā 44:36)
viritāḷa kayilāya maliyē  (tēvā. 11 : 56)
taḷ-taṇṭu = 1. tiraṇṭa aṭi. e.ṭu. kīrittaṇṭu, vāḻittaṇṭu, viḷakkut taṇṭu.
2. 	tiraṇṭa ūṉṟukōl.
tali naṭunkāt taṇṭūṉṟā  (nālaṭi. 14).
3. 	tiraṇṭa ulakkippaṭi.  (va.va. ti.ma. 742).
taṇṭu mutaṟ cakkaramu ṉēntum  (tiv. periyati. 3 : (9:10)
4. 	tiraṇṭa taṭipōṉṟa poruḷ.
e.ṭu. mutukantaṇṭu, vīṇittaṇṭu.
5. 	tiraṇṭa paṭi (cēṉi). taṇṭeṭuttal = paṭiyeṭuttal.
6. 	koṭittaṇṭu pōṉṟa uṟuppu. e.ṭu. kātut
taṇṭam - taṇṭaṉ.
3. 	cēṉi. e.ṭu. taṇṭa nāyakaṉ = cēṉittalivaṉ.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi = taṇṭāl tūkkappeṟum pallakku. taṇṭi - taṇṭiki.
vālnīṇṭa karikkuruvi valamiruntu iṭañceṉṟāl
kālnaṭiyāyc ceṉṟavarum kaṉakataṇṭi yēṟuvarē
paḻamoḻi :
taṇṭu - taṇṭiyam = taṇṭiyappaṭikkal allatu kaṭṭi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi = tāmarikkoṭit taṇṭu pōṉṟa kālaṇi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi. taṇṭittal = paruttal. taṇṭi - taṭi. taṭittam = paruttal. taṭi = paruttakōl. taṭimaṉ = parumi. taṭiyaṉ = pūcaṇi.
taṇṭu - taṇṭi. taṇṭittal = taṭiyālaṭittal, taṇṭaṉi ceytal. taṇṭi - taṇṭam, taṇṭaṉam, taṇṭaṉi.
taṇṭam - taṇṭā = mūvakit taṇṭaṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
taṇṭam = 1. taṇṭaṉi.
taṇṭamun taṇiti paṇṭiyiṟ peritē  (puṟam. 10 : 6)
2. vīṇ. e.ṭu. taṇṭaccōṟu.  (va.va. 170-171).
taṇṭikkum muṟi
maṉṟupāṭu - paṇattaṇṭal
taṇṭā - tuṉpat taṇṭaṉi, uṟuppaṟit taṇṭaṉiyum kolit taṇṭaṉiyum
kuṟṟam - kōyil viḷakkerittal pōṉṟa tirukkaṭamit taṇṭaṉi.  (ti.ma.315). (col: 60).
taṇṭu
taṇṭutal = poruḷ tiraṭṭutal. taṭiyeṭuttava ṉellām taṇṭaṟkāraṉā? (paḻamoḻi).
taṇṭu - taṇṭam = 1. peruntaṭip paṭikkalam.
taṇṭamuṭit tarumaṉ  (tēvā. 1055 : 6)
2. taṭipōṟ kīḻē viḻuntu vaṇaṅkutal (taṇṭaṉiṭutal).  (va.va).
taṇṇīr cēntukiṟatu viḷiyāṭṭu
nāṉku piḷḷikaḷ, caturamāka neruṅki oṟṟik kālāl niṉṟu maṟṟik kālik kālmēṟpōṭṭu, naṭuviṟ kiṇaṟupōṉṟa paḷḷam tōṟṟi,
taṇṇīr cēntit taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟu, 
pūppaṟittup pūppaṟittuk kūṭiyilē pōṭu,
viṟṟuviṟṟup paṇattiyeṭuttup peṭṭiyilē pōṭu,
eṉṟu pāṭikkoṇṭu, taṇṇīr cēntik kuṭattilē ūṟṟuvatu pōṉṟum, pūppaṟittuk kūṭiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, pū viṟṟa paṇattip peṭṭiyilē pōṭuvatu pōṉṟum, avvav vaṭikkēṟpa avinayañ ceyvar.
oṟṟik kālil niṟkum payiṟciyi ivviḷiyāṭṭāṟ peṟalām.  (ta.nā.vi.)
tapu
tapu - tap (dabh) - i.vē.
taputal = keṭutal. ataṅkōṭ ṭācāṟ kariltapat terintu (tol. ciṟappup)
kātali yiḻanta taputāra niliyam  (tol. 1025)
taputtal = keṭuttal.
uḷḷa maḻiyavūkkunar miṭaltaputtu (patiṟṟup 13 : 18)
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ keṭuttal eṉṉum piṟaviṉi vaḻakkē yuḷḷatu.  (va.va.171).
tamar
tamar eṉpār tam kuṭumpattārum, tam uṟaviṉarum, tam vakuppārum, tam kūṭṭattārum, tam ūrārum, tam nāṭṭārum, tam moḻiyārum eṉap pala tiṟattār.  (ti.ma. 881).
tamiḻ uyartaṉi ilakkiyac cemmoḻi
cāṉṟukaḷ
1.	paḻantamiḻ tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyākavum āriyattiṟku mūlamākavum iruttal.
2.	tamiḻukkum piṟamoḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ tamiḻilēyē tiruntiya allatu tūya vaṭiviliruttal.
3.	tamiḻiṉ colvaḷam.
4.	tamiḻilakkaṇam, poruḷkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ pakuttaṟivuṇ miyum iṉmiyum paṟṟi, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi eṉa iru vakuppāka vakuttal.
5.	paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyamellām, uriyum akaramutaliyum uṭpaṭa, ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntami.
6.	veṇpā, kalippā eṉṉum ceyyuḷvakikaṭkotta yāppu vēṟem moḻiyilumiṉmi.
7.	ilakkaṇap pulavaṉiṉ matinuṭpa mutircciyik kāṭṭum poruḷilak kaṇam eṉṟum tamiḻukkē taṉicciṟappāyiruttal.
8.	mutaṉ mutalākat taṉiyoliyaṉkaḷi (Phonemes)yellām vakuttatum, uyiriyum meyyiyum pirittatum, uyir meykkut taṉi varivaṭiva mittatum, uyirkaḷik kuṟil neṭilākavum meykaḷi valimeli yiṭiyākavum muṟip paṭuttiyatumāṉa tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku, kumarināṭṭi lēyē ki.mu.10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumuṉ tōṉṟiyamiyum, tamiḻeḻuttuk kaṭkellām aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi piṟappiyal kūṟappaṭṭamiyum.
9.	tamiḻilakkaṇap pākupāṭukaḷum kuṟiyīṭukaḷum ciṟanta ēraṇa muṟi yilum meypporuḷiyaṟ pōṅkilum amintiruttal.
10.tamiḻ ilakkiya vakiyīṭum nūṟpirivukaḷiṉ muṟi vippum vaṇṇaṉiyiyalpum perumpālum iyaṟkiyiyum uṇmiyiyum oṭṭiyiruttal.
11.	tamiḻ aṟanūlkaḷellām vakuppu vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi naṭu nilimiyākavum aṉpākavum aṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷiyum taṇṭaṉikaḷiyum vakuttiruttal.
12.	tamiḻoṭu ici nāṭakak kalikaḷiyuñ cērttu iyal ici nāṭakam eṉat tamiḻi muttamiḻāka vaḻaṅkiyami.
13.	makkaḷ vāḻkkik kuṟikkōḷi aṟam poruḷiṉpam vīṭeṉa nāṉkāka vakuttu, immiyilum maṟumiyilum vīṭupēṟaṭitaṟkēṟṟa muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷiṉ uruvamillā vaḻipāṭṭi mutaṉmutaṟ kaṟpittami.
14.	tamiḻic centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟum, centamiḻc coṟkaḷi iyaṟcol tiricolleṉṟum, pakuttu moḻi nūṟ kūṟṟiyum collilakkaṇattoṭu cērttami.
15.	tamiḻ eṉṟuṅ keṭātirukkumāṟu centamiḻiyē niliyāṉa aḷaviyāka varampiṭṭami.
16.	centamiḻik kākkumāṟum putu nūlkaḷiṟ centamiḻiyē kaṭippiṭikkumāṟum pāṇṭiyarāl mukkaḻakaṅkaḷ niṟuvap peṟṟami.



	epirēyam (Hebrew) ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yaṉmi.
cāṉṟukaḷ
1.	epirēya moḻittōṟṟam ki.mu. 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭatu. epirēyam eṉpatu icaravēlar allatu yūtar eṉappaṭum iṉattār moḻi. icaravēl eṉṉum yākkōpiṉ kālam ki.mu. 1837-1689. avaṉ makkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ yūtā. icaravēl vaḻiyiṉar icaravēlar. yūtā vaḻiyiṉar yūtar.
	icaravēlum avaṉ makkaḷum ekipatiṟkuc ceṉṟa āṇṭu ki.mu. 1706. avarkaḷ aṅkuk kuṭiyamarnta piṉṉarē epirēyam uruppeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
2.	epirēyam oru vallolimoḻi.
3. 	epirēyam tamiḻpōl oru tāymoḻiyaṉṟu.
4.	epirēyac coṟkaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷil tamiḻpōṟ kalakka villi.
5.	nōvā kālattiṟkumuṉ mēliyāciyā muḻutum cēmiya mūla moḻiyē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. 
	nōvā kālam ki.mu. 25 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. 
	ulaka muḻutum orē moḻiyum orē pēccumāyiruntatu  
	(tirumaṟi (Bible), mutaṟ pottakam. 11:1)
6.	epirēyam vaḻaṅkiya nāṭu kumarikkaṇṭam pōl mutupaḻan toṉṉāṭaṉṟu.
7.	icaravēlar nīṇṭakālamāka nāṭōṭikaḷākavē yiruntaṉar.
8.	icaravēlar iṟutiyāka vantu kuṭiyūṉṟiya kāṉāṉ nāṭum kumarināṭu pōl peruvaḷamuṟṟataṉṟu.
9.	kāṉāṉ nāṭṭu nilapparappu mutaṟkālattil kaṭalāyiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
10. icaravēlariṉ muṉṉōṉāṉa āpirakām kālattiṟku muṉpē pala nāṭukaḷum pala iṉaṅkaḷum pala moḻikaḷum tōṉṟi viṭṭaṉa. avaṉ peyariṉ mutaṟ collāṉa āp eṉpatu appaṉ eṉṉum teṉcoṟ citivē.
āpirakāmiṉ kālam ki.mu.20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. avaṉ kālattu nāṭukaḷ, pāpilōṉiyam, acīriyam, kāṉāṉ, pilittiyam, pāracīkam, ekipatu mutaliyaṉa.



āriyam ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yaṉmi
cāṉṟukaḷ 
1.	āriyam tamiḻiṉ allatu tiraviṭattiṉ tiripēyaṉṟi oru taṉimoḻi yaṉṟu.
	āriyam eṉṉum iṉappeyar moḻittiripiṉālaṉṟi iṉa vēṟupāṭṭāl ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu.
2.	āriyattiṉ aṭippaṭi tamiḻē.
	āriyac coṟkaḷiṉṟit tamiḻi vaḻaṅkamuṭiyum. āyiṉ, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉṟi āriyatti vaḻaṅkamuṭiyātu.
3.	āriyarkkeṉṟu oru taṉiyiṭamilli.
	intiyac cintuveḷiyiṉiṉṟu airōppiyak kāṇṭiṉēviyam vari vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷ āriyariṉ piṟantakamāka allatu mutaliruppākak kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum uṇmiyāṉataṉṟu; allatu ellām cariyē.
	tiraviṭam āriyamāka mutaṉmutal tirinta iṭam vaṭamēli airōppā. aṅkiruntu teṉkiḻakkākap paṭimuṟiyāyt tirintu vantu intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntuḷḷatu.
	camaṟkirutam eṉṉum aric ceyaṟkiyāṉa ilakkiya naṭimoḻi uruvāṉatu intiyāvilēyē. atil aintiliru pakuti tamiḻē.
	āriyam eṉṉum peyarum mutaṉmutal intiyāvilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. atu piṉṉar mākkacu mullarāl mēliyiṉa moḻikaṭkum virivuṟuttap peṟṟatu.
4. 	āriyam melloli moḻiyaṉṟu.
	ulaka moḻikaṭkuḷ valloli mutirccipeṟṟatu āriyamē. ataṉ vallolittaṉmi tiyūttāṉiyattil toṭaṅki intiya āriyattil mutirkiṉṟatu.
5.	āriya moḻiyilakkaṇa vamiti ceyaṟki vaḷarcci peṟṟatu.
	irumiyeṇ, ilakkaṇappāl, īṟupiriyāmi eḻuvāyurupu mutaliyaṉa itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭām.
	irumiyeṇ tiyūttāṉiyattilum ilattīṉilum illi.
6.	āriyattiṉ muntuvaṭivameṉṟu tavaṟākak karutappaṭum camaṟkirutat tiṉ neṭuṅkaṇakkum puṇarcciyum eṇ vēṟṟumiyum tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavē.
7.	āriyamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa teṉcoṟkaṭkellām tiruntiya vaṭivum vērpporuḷum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
8.	tolkāppiyam āriya vētaṅkaṭkuk kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭa tēṉum poruḷāl muṟpaṭṭatē.
	talikkaḻaka nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇattiyē cārpiṟ cārpāṉa tolkāppiyam eṭuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
	āyirakkaṇakkāṉa mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum āriyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭamiyāl, iṉṟu paṇṭit tamiḻilak kiyam camaṟkiruta ilakkiyattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu.
9.	nāṉkām maṇṭalak kālattil (ki.mu. 25,000 - 475,000) airōppāviṟ paṉikkaṭṭip paṭalap paṭarcci yiruntamiyāl, ki.mu. 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya tamiḻē ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
oṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu 
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ
	eṉpatē aṟivuṭit tamiḻar koḷki.
10.	āriyamoḻi tamiḻpōl moḻivaḷarcciyiṉ muntu nilikaḷik kāṭṭavilli.
11.	āriya moḻivaralāṟu tamiḻpōṟ kuṟiñci mulli marutam ākiya muntiyal māntar nākarika vaḷarcci niliyaṅkaḷi kāṭṭavilli.
12.	āriyar veḷḷiyar; tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum, kariyarum ceyyarum poṉṉarumāvar.
muntiyal māntarveḷḷiyarallar. ātalāl veḷḷiyar moḻi ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyā yiruttal muṭiyātu.
tamiḻ eḻuttu māṟṟam
tamiḻeḻuttiṉ cīrmiyum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷiṉ kuṟipāṭum
itupōtu vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ arivariyip piṟamoḻiyarivari kaḷōṭu oppunōkkiṉāltāṉ, muṉṉatiṉ cīrmi viḷaṅkum.
vaṭamoḻi yarivariyil, gh, bh; ṅa, ṭa: bh, m ākiya iv vīreḻuttukaḷ ciṟitē tammuḷ vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa. kh eṉṉum eḻuttum va eṉṉum kūṭṭeḻuttum. rava pōlum tōṉṟum. muṉṉum piṉṉum kīḻum cērtteḻutum kūṭṭeḻuttu kaḷ (camyuktākṣaram) 160-kku mēluṇṭu. itaṉāl mika nuṇukkiyum varinerukkiyum eḻutamuṭiyātu. kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkut taṉi ac curukkaḷ (types) vēṇṭum. uyirmey varikaḷ (33 x 12) 462.
vaṭamoḻi yarivariyiyē koṇṭa inti yeḻuttukaḷil ikkuṟi  pāṭṭōṭu iṭi kaṭi varum akara vūmiyeḻuttu kaḷum (Silent Letters) uṇṭu. uyirmey varikaḷ (33 x 12) 396.
teluṅkarivariyil, ō. bh; pa, va; na. sa ākiya ivvīreḻuttukaḷ tammuḷ mikacciṟitē vēṟupaṭṭu mayakkattiṟkiṭamāvaṉa. muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kīḻum cērtteḻutuṅ kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ iṭatti aṭippaṉa, mo, mō, yi, yī, yo, yō, ho, hō ākiya uyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷ ēṉiyavaṟṟiṉum vēṟu paṭṭa uyirkkuṟiyuṭiyaṉa. uyirmey varikaḷ (35 x 35) 455. ivi yallātu. nakārap pollu eṉṉum oru taṉi ṉakaramey variyum. paṇṭira allatu cakaṭṭirēppa eṉṉum ṟakarameyyum. oru meykku allatu uyirmeykkuppiṉ eḻuti muṉ palukkap peṟum velappala kilaka eṉṉum rakaravariyum uḷḷaṉa.
kaṉṉaṭa arivari teluṅkarivariyip piṉpaṟṟiyatātaliṉ, atilum ittakiya kuṟipāṭuṇṭu. kaṉṉaṭa uyirmey varikaḷ (34 x 14) 476.
maliyāḷa arivariyil, (kuṟṟiyalukara) lu, ña eṉṉum īreḻut tum kh, ch, p, v ākiya nāleḻuttum, mayakkattiṟkiṭa māvaṉa, ku, kū eṉṉum īreḻuttum nuṇukkeḻuttil kūrṅkaṇṇaruk kaṉṟi vēṟupāṭu teriyā. ukara ūkāramēṟṟa uyirmeykaḷ (uniform) ōriyal vaṭivu koṇṭaṉa valla. muṉṉum piṉṉum kīḻum mēlum cērtteḻutum kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ 300 kku mēṟ paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟukkēṉum taṉi accurukkaḷ vēṇ ṭum. ch, b, y, v ākiya nāleḻuttum iraṭṭikkum kūṭṭeḻut tukaḷil kīḻeḻuttu oṉṟākavē irukkum. uyirmey varikaḷ (36 x 16) 576..
tamiḻilō ovvōr eḻuttum eḷitāy vēṟupaṭuttaṟiyakkūṭiya taṉi vaṭivuḷḷatu. meykaḷ patiṉeṭṭē. itaṉālum, kūṭṭe ḻuttiṉmiyālum ettuṇiyō kuṟikiṉṟatu. uyirmey varikaḷ (18 x 12) 216. āytam oṉṟu. āka, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mottam 247 tām.
cila uyirmey varikaḷ tamiḻil ōriyal vaṭivu koṇṭaṉavalla veṉiṉ, ik kuṟipāṭu teluṅku kaṉṉaṭa maliyāḷattilum uḷḷatē! ataṟkeṉ ceyvatu?
urutuviṟkuriya pāracīka - arapi yarivariyil uyirmey varikaḷ illā viṭiṉum, palaveḻuttukaḷ orē vaṭivukoṇṭu mēṟkīḻp puḷḷittoki vēṟupāṭṭālēyē vēṟupaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. perumpālum, ovvō reḻuttiṟkum colliṉ mutaliṭi kaṭikkuriya mūvēṟu vaṭivuṇṭu. yakaravari ikarattiyum, vakaravari ukarattiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
nuṇukkiyum nerukkiyum aḻakākavum teḷivākavum maṇipōl eḻutappeṟum eḻuttukaḷuḷ talimiyāṉatu rōma (Roman) eḻuttu. ataṟkaṭuttatu tamiḻē. tamiḻ eḻuttu vaṭivi māṟṟi ṉāl, pala iṉṉalum aḻakiḻappum nērum.
piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷil ettuṇiyō kuṟipāṭirukkum pōtu. avaṟṟiyellām kavaṉikkāmal tamiḻeḻutti maṭṭum māṟṟuvatu eṟṟukku? mēlum, oru allatu cila ceytit tāḷil eḻutti māṟṟa virumpiṉ, atupaṟṟip pāṭapputtakaṅ kaḷilum ilakkiyattilum ēṉ māṟṟa vēṇṭum? ēṟkeṉavē tamiḻeḻuttup palamuṟi takunta muṟiyil cīrtiruttap peṟṟē iṟṟi vaṭiveytiyuḷḷateṉpati. tamiḻ eḻuttu māṟṟak kōṭpāṭṭiṉar aṟivārā? avar virumpuvatu cīrtiruttamā? allatu (cīrkēṭāṉa) māṟṟamā?
tamiḻeḻuttil ceyya vēṇṭiya tiruttam
tamiḻeḻuttil ippōtu ceyya vēṇṭiya tiruttam iraṇṭē. oṉṟu ī yi nīkki (mēṟcuḻiyiṭṭa) i yi vittukkoḷ vatu; iṉṉoṉṟu aukāra uyirilum ataṉiyēṟṟa uyirmey kaḷilum ḷa variyic caṟṟuc ciṟitākkuvatu. (mēṟcuḻiyiṭṭa) i varitāṉ mutalāvatu iruntu. piṉpu iṭikkālattil atu vilakkappaṭṭuk kiranta variyākiya ī pukuttappaṭṭatu. aukāra variyiluḷḷa ḷa variyum ū variyiṟpōl mutalāvatu ciṟitāka vēyiruntu piṟkālattil tavaṟākap peritāka eḻutappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻppulavar ceyta tavaṟu
in nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuttuppaṟṟit tamiḻukku iruvakiyil ūṟu nērnta pōtu tamiḻppulavar taṭuttilar. oru moḻiyil pala karuttukaṭkum collirukkum pōtu, avaṟṟukkup patilākap piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷiyum, eḻuttukaḷiyum vēṇṭātu pukuttuvatu, oru moḻiyik keṭukkum vaḻiyākum.
tamiḻil orē yuyirkoṇṭa uyirmey varikaḷellām ōriyal vaṭivukoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu tiruntiya karuttaṉṟu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka aikāra uyirmeykaḷi eṭuttakkoḷḷiṉ, avi ōriyal vaṭivukoṇṭi rāmikkat takunta kāraṇamuṇṭu. ṉa  eṉpataṟkup patilāka ṉi eṉṟu eḻutiṉ, kūṭṭeḻuttā yeḻutuṅ kiyeḻuttil atu iru ṉakarampōl tōṉṟum. im mayakkatti nīkkutaṟkē. aikāra vuyirkkuṟi, pala cuḻikaḷum vaḷivukaḷuṅ koṇṭa eḻuttukaṭkellām mēliṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉamē āyntu nōkkiṉ. ōriyal vaṭivu peṟāta piṟa uyirmey varikaṭkum kāraṇam tōṉṟum. accuvaṭivu oṉṟiyē kavaṉittuk kiyeḻuttu vaṭivi nōkkātavarkkē, tamiḻ uyir mey varikaḷ oḻuṅkillātaṉa vākat tōṉṟalām. kiyeḻuttum mukkiyam; āṅkilattil ataṉiyum accu vaṭivil amittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
 eṉṉum paḻiya aikāra vuyirmeyk kuṟiyi  eṉṟu iṇittum. ku ṅu cu ñu ṭu ṇu mutaliya uyirmey varikaḷi orē muṟiyil variyum oṟṟik kuṟiyākavum eḻutuvatellām eḷimiyum teḷivum maṭṭumaṉṟu, kālac cikkaṉamum paṟṟiyatākum. ātalāl, evvakiyilum (mēṟkāṭṭiya iru varikaḷi yaṉṟi) māṟṟam ceyyat tēviyilli. putiya eḻuttumāṟṟam ceytittāḷkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyātatāyiṉ. avimaṭṭum ati āḷaṭṭum. ēṉiyarkku vēṇṭā.
iṉṟu ceyya vēṇṭiyatu
putiya eḻuttu māṟṟam pukuttappaṭiṉ, palkalikkaḻakap pāṭap puttakak kuḻuviyum, taṉippaṭṭavarkkum potumakkaṭkumuriya ellā nūlniliyaṅ kaḷiyum, tamiḻnūl veḷiyīṭṭāḷa riyum, tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷiyum, palavaki āvaṇaṅkaḷiyum, māṇavariyum, tākkip pala iṭarppāṭṭi viḷivikkum. ātalāl, itip pukuttātavāṟu araciyalāri vēṇṭik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
yāḻppāṇat tamiḻar putiya eḻuttumāṟṟatti oppukkoḷḷa villi yeṉṟum, paḻiya eḻuttiyē kaṭippiṭikkat tīrmāṉittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟum collappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻccoṟ kaḷip pōṉṟē tamiḻ eḻuttiyum tūymiyākap pēṇat tuṇinta yāḻppāṇam taḻittōṅka!  (centamiḻc celvi navampar 1951)
tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar varalāṟu
tamiḻ kumarikkaṇṭattil tāṉē tōṉṟiya moḻiyātalāṉum, āriyar varum vari vēṟṟumoḻiyoṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭil evvakiyilēṉum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum, tamiḻavaṇikar moḻi peyar nāṭu cellum vari allatu ayalāroṭu araciyal allatu vaṇikattoṭarpu koḷḷum moḻi allatu pēccu eṉṉum potuppeyartavira yātoru ciṟappuppeyarum tamiḻukku vaḻaṅkiyirukka muṭiyātu.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ciṟappup peyar, tamiḻ (tamiḻam, tiraviṭam eṉṉum iruvaṭivil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭum vevvēṟu coṟpōl tōṉṟiṉum uṇmiyil orē colliṉ iruvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiyatu tamiḻ eṉpatē. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa :
1.	tirāviṭam eṉṉum col mutalāvatu tamiḻ eṉṉum poruḷilēyē vaḻaṅkiyami.
vaṭa nūlār tamiḻi oru pirākirutamākak koṇṭa pōtu, atit trāviṭi eṉa aḻittaṉar.
pākavata purāṇattil, cattiyavirataṉ eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum oru tamiḻaracaṉ, tirāviṭapati eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.
ki.pi. 470 ām āṇṭil vaccirananti eṉṉum camaṇar maturiyil niṟuviya tamiḻk kaḻakam tirāviṭa caṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu.
piḷḷi lōkācāryacīyar (500 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ) periya tirumoḻic ciṟappup pāyiravuriyil, tamiḻilakkaṇattit tirāviṭa cātiram eṉak kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟār.
nālāyirat teyvappaṉuval tirāviṭavētam eṉṟum, civañāṉa muṉivariṉ meykaṇṭāṉ nūl akalavuri tirāviṭamāpāṭiyam eṉṟum, capāpatināvalariṉ tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟu tirāviṭap pirakāciki eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tāyumāṉavar : (18 - ām nūṟ) kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ eṉṉum pāṭṭil, tamiḻit tirāviṭam eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
kiṟittuviṟku muṉ tamiḻilaṉṟit tiraviṭamoḻiyetilum ilakkiya miṉmiyāl, tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyarāl tamiḻiyē taliyākak kuṟittu vantaṉar, vaṭavar. ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tāṉ, kumarilapaṭṭar āntira - tirāviṭapāṣā eṉṉun toṭarāl teluṅkit tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pirittuk kūṟiṉar.
tirāviṭam eṉṉum tirinta vaṭivic cemmiyāṉa vaṭivākak koṇṭu, āriyattāl teṟkē turattappaṭṭatu eṉṟu tamiḻppakivarum, tīviṉiyit turattuvatu eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivarum, tiruviṭam (tiru+iṭam) eṉṟu pērā, pūraṇa liṅkam piḷḷiyum, turuvitarōṭu toṭarpupaṭutti ñāṉappirakācak kuravarum, vevvēṟu poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
(2)	tamiḻ eṉṉum colliyotta peyarkaḷē veḷi nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiyami.
ki.mu. 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta perippuḷucu (Periplus) eṉṉum kirēkka nūl tamirāy (Tamiri) eṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷatu. piyūttiṅkar aṭṭavaṇi (Peutinger Tables) eṉṉum urōma ñālappaṭaṅkaḷil (Atlas) tamiḻakam tamirikkē (Damirice) eṉakkuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tālami (Ptolemy) eṉṉum ekiptiya ñāla nūlār (ki.pi. 139-161). piyūttiṅkar aṭṭavaṇiyiṭap peyarkaḷip peyartteḻutum pōtu, kirēkkarakaratti lakaramākak koṇṭu, timirikkē (Dymirice) eṉṉum peyarit tavaṟutalāka lumirikkī (Lumiriki) eṉṟu eḻutiviṭṭār. āyiṉum avarukkup piṉṉar vanta rēvaṇṇā (Ravenna) ñālanūlār tamirikka (Damirica) eṉattiruttik koṇṭār.
tamiḻ eṉṉuñcol, ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāviṟku vanta yuvāṉcāṅ (Hwen Thsang) eṉṉum cīṉa vaḻippōkkar kuṟippil, cimelo (Tehi-molo) eṉṉum vaṭivil uḷḷatu.
itit timala (Dimala) allatu timara (Dimara) eṉṟum paṭikka lām eṉpar kālṭuvelār. pālimoḻiyil uḷḷa makāvamicam (Mahavamso) eṉṉum ilaṅki varalāṟṟil, tamilō (Damilo) eṉṉum vaṭivamē uḷḷatu. airōppiyar potuvākat tamul (Tamul) eṉṟaṉar. avaruḷ tēṉiyaviṭi yūḻiyar (Danish Missionaries) maṭṭum tamulikka moḻi (Lingua Damulica) eṉa ilattīṉ vaṭivil kuṟittaṉar. āṅkilattil tamil (Tamil) eṉṉum vaṭivam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. mākkacu mullar teṉmoḻik kuṭumpattiṟkut tamulikku (Tamulic) eṉap peyariṭṭār.
(3)	tirāviṭam eṉṉum col, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ colliṟku neruṅkiya tiramilam (allatu tiramiḷam) eṉṉum vaṭivil vaḻaṅkiyami.
ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, varākamikirar nūliṉ paḻaṅkiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷil tramiṭa eṉaṉum vaṭivum maṅkalēca aracaṉiṉ paṭṭa yaṅkaḷil tramila eṉṉum vaṭivum, kāṇappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, tāranātar tipēttil eḻutiya intiyāvil putta matattip parappiṉa varalāṟu (ki.pi. 1573) eṉṉum nūlil, tirāviṭar tiramilar (Dramilas) eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar eṉṟum, purāṇaṅ kaḷiṉ paḻiya moḻi peyarppukkaḷilellām ivvaṭivē peruvaḻakkāyiruppatākak kuṇṭerṭṭup paṇṭārakar kūṟukiṟāreṉṟum, tirāviṭam eṉpataṉ mikap paḻiya vaṭivam tramiṭa (Dramida) eṉpatu eṉṟum; kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇi yār varintuḷḷār. (â.x.ï., pak.9.)
nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻippeyarkaḷum paṇṭik kālattiṟ perumpālum am īṟupeṟṟut tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiyati nōkkum pōtu, tamiḻ eṉṉum collum ciṟupāṉmi tamiḻam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyatākak karuta iṭam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻam, tramila(m) tramiḷa(m), tramiṭa(m), traviṭa(m) eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷi muṟiyē nōkkiṉ, tamiḻ eṉpataṉ tiripē tiraviṭam eṉpatu pulaṉām. āyiṉum, kālṭuvelār ivveḷiya muṟiyil uṇmiyik kāṇāmal, iyaṟkikku māṟākat talikīḻāy nōkki, tirāviṭam eṉṉuñ collē tamiḻeṉṟu tirintatāka muṭivu ceytu viṭṭār. āyiṉ, kiriyarcaṉ ittavaṟṟit tirutti viṭṭaṉar.
tramiḷam eṉpatu tamiḻ eṉat tiritalum atu. eṉṟu pirayōka vivēka nūlār (pak.4) kūṟiyatu, avaratu tamiḻ veṟuppik kāṭṭum cāṉṟēyeṉa aṟika.
(4)	tirāviṭam eṉṉum vaṭivam tamiḻnāṭṭulakavaḻakkil arukiyum vaḻaṅkāmi.
(5)	tramiṭam, traviṭam eṉṉum rakara mēṟṟiya vaṭivukaḷēyaṉṟi, tapiḷa taviṭa eṉṉum mika neruṅkiya vaṭivukaḷum vaṭamoḻi nāṭakaṅkaḷilum camaṇa nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkiyiruttal.
iṉi, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ col, kirēkka nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiya termilē allatu termilar allatu tiramilar (Dravids tiraviṭar) eṉṉum iṉappeyariṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu ñāṉap pirakācak kuravarum, vaṅka nāṭṭut tamralitti eṉṉum nakarppeyariṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya teṉṟu kaṉakacapip piḷḷiyum; tāmam (ñāyiṟu), ellām (ilaṅki) eṉṉum iru col, muṟiyē tām īḻam eṉa maruvip puṇarntavaṭiveṉṟu kantiyā piḷḷiyum; tami eṉṉum mutaṉiliyaṭiyāyp 
piṟantu oppaṟṟateṉap poruḷpaṭuvateṉṟu tāmōtaram piḷḷiyum, taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭa moḻi eṉṉum poruḷ taruvateṉṟu oru cārārum, valimeli yiṭiyākiya mūviṉa meykaḷik koṇṭa nilimiyik kuṟippateṉṟu maṟṟoru cārārum, ataṟkup poruṭ karaṇiyaṅkāṭṭuvar.
iṉimiyāṉatu eṉṟu poruṭkaraṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvāruḷ, (S) cīnivāciyaṅ kār oruvarē tam + iḻ eṉṟu pakuttu, iḻ eṉṉum īṟu iṉimip poruḷpaṭum iḻum eṉṉum colliṉ citivēyeṉak kāṭṭuvar. piṟarellām, iṉimiyum nīrmiyun tamiḻeṉalākum. eṉṉum piṅkalanti nūṟpāvit taḻuvi, tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟkē iṉimip poruḷuṇṭeṉak koḷvar.
kirēkka nāṭṭil, eṉṟēṉum tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṟkō tamiḻar vāḻntiruntār eṉpataṟkō oru cāṉṟu miṉmi yāṉum; tamiḻc coṟkaḷum tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum, kirēkka nāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi ulakattiṟ pala iṉattāriṭiyum kāṇappaṭuvatālum, kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum intiyāviṟkum iṭiyil oru tiraviṭa moḻiyum vaḻaṅkāmiyāṉum; termila eṉṉum collit tira - il - ar eṉṟu pirittu, kaṭaṟkariyil kuṭikoṇṭavar eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvatu, kēcavak kiruṭṭi ṇaṉē ēcukkiṟittu eṉṟu colvatokku mātalāṉum; orē colliṉ orupuṭi yoppumi paṟṟi oru nāṭṭāri aiyāyiram kal toli viṟkappāṟpaṭṭa vēṟoru nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantavarākak koḷvatu, moḻi nūṉ muṟikku muṟṟum muraṇāṉatākalāṉum; tōṟṟam mutal ituvarippaṭṭa vaḷarcci niliyellām toṭarpākak kāṭṭik koṇṭu toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē tamiḻ vaḻaṅki vantirutta lāṉum; kirēkka nāṭṭu mūlakkoḷki mikattavaṟāṉa teṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
vaṅka nāṭṭil tiraviṭarēyaṉṟit tamiḻar orukālum vāḻn tirāmiyāṉum, iṟṟi vaṅkamoḻi āriya vaṇṇamāy māṟi yiruttalāṉum, tamiḻ eṉṉuñ coṟkut tamralitti eṉṉuñ collōṭuḷḷa toṭarpu. kāci eṉṉum peyarkkuk kāñci yeṉṉum peyarōṭuḷḷa toṭarpē yātalāṉum; tamralitti (allatu tamilapti allatu tamilūk) eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar vantateṉpatu ciṟitum poruntātu.
tamiḻar nākarikamaṭinta kālan toṭṭut tamiḻakam cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarāṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa muttamiḻnāṭā iruntuvantami yāṉum, ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tolkāppiyarum,
vaṇṭamiḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ 
nāṟpeyar elli yakam  (tol. 1336)
eṉṟu kūṟutalāṉum, varalāṟṟuk kālam neṭukalum ilaṅki ayalārāṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa oru kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭākavē karutappaṭṭu varutalāṉum; tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar kumarikkaṇṭattilēyē tōṉṟiviṭṭamiyāṉum; tāmamellām allatu tāmīḻam eṉpatu ilaṅkiyi nōkkip putuvatākap paṭitta poruntāp puṇarppātalālum; appuṇarppuc colliṉiṉṟu tamiḻeṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, ilaṅkiyi nōkkip putuvatākap paṭitta poruntāp puṇarppātalālum; appuṇarppuc colliṉiṉṟu tamiḻeṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu, nāṭṭuppaṟṟiṉāleḻunta viruppak karuttēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu.
tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟateṉṉuṅ karuttu ēṉiya moḻika ḷellān tōṉṟiyapiṉ, avaṟṟōṭu tamiḻi oppu nōkki ataṉ uyarvu kaṇṭu ataṟkut tamiḻ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉareṉṟu, appeyarīṭṭi mikappintiya nikaḻcciyākak kāṭṭuvatāl, atuvum koḷḷat takkataṉṟu.
mūviṉamey ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvātaliṉ, tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar atu kuṟittut tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatum tavaṟāṉatē.
ellā moḻiyāḷarum tattam moḻiyē iṉiyateṉṟu koḷvatu iyalpātalāṉum; māṭu eṉṉum coṟkuc celvam eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiyatupōl, tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟku iṉimi eṉpatu matippum paṟṟum paṟṟit tōṉṟiya vaḻip poruḷē yātalāṉum; tam + iḻ (iḻum) eṉṉum coṟpakup pil, tam  eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭu toṭarpaṟṟum, iḻum eṉṉum talimiccol īṟākak kuṉṟiyum, irukka vēṇṭum nilimi yēṟpaṭutalāṉum; iṉimip poruṭ karaṇiyamum ēṟṟataṉṟām.
iḻum = iṉimi. iḻumeṉal = iṉitāyiruttal.
iḻutu = tēṉ, tittippu.
taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉuṅ karuttup porutta muḷḷatākat tōṉṟiṉum atuvum ēṉi moḻikaḷuṭaṉ oppunōkkutali vēṇṭutaliṉ ēṟkat takkataṉṟu. ki.mu.10,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē kumarik kaṇṭattil tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
tamiḻ eṉṉum collil ḻa iyalpīṟaṉṟu. amiḻ, imiḻ, umiḻ, kumiḻ, cimiḻ eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl, tamiḻ eṉpatilum iḻ eṉpatē īṟām, atu il eṉpataṉ tiripu. taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉum poruḷil, tamiḻ eṉpatu tami+ḻ eṉṟu pirital vēṇṭum. tami=taṉimi ḻakaram iṉitā yolippatāl, taṉimiyāka ḻakarattik koṇṭateṉṉuṅ kūṟṟum, iṉimik karuttit taḻuviyatē.
iṉi, iṉimiyeṉpatu tamiḻukku ḻakarattāl maṭṭum ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu. eḻuttiṉimi pōṉṟē, colliṉimi, ceyyuḷiṉimi, poruḷiṉimi aṇiyiṉimi, ilakkiya viṉimi, iciyiṉimi, kūttiṉimi, eṉap piṟaviṉimikaḷu muṇṭu.
ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cintiyuḷḷum
uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāynta voṇ ṭīntamiḻiṉ
tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉi yōvaṉṟi yēḻicic cūḻalpukkō
iṟivā taṭavarit tōṭkeṉ kolāmpukun teytiyatē.
eṉṉum tirukkōvic ceyyuḷil, oṇṭīntamiḻiṉ tuṟi eṉṟu piṟamoḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilakkaṇamum, ēḻicic cūḻal eṉṟu icit tamiḻum, iṉpat tuṟikaḷāka māṇikka vācakarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttaliyum, nōkkit teḷika.
aṇmiyil tamiḻppērāciriyar oruvar. (vē.vē. irāmacāmi pārāṭṭu malar, pak. 93-95). tamir eṉṉuñ collit ta+miḻ eṉṟu pirittum. tam+miḻaṟṟu eṉṟu virittum, namatu iṉiya moḻi eṉṟu poruḷ kuṟittum, āṅkilattil oru kaṭṭuri varintuḷḷār.
muṟiyē, ēm (yām), nām, nūm, tām eṉṉum mūviṭap peyarkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷāṉa em, nam, num, tam eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, em, nam eṉṉum taṉmic coṟkaḷē arumip pāṭṭi yuṇarttum. itu ārvak kōḷi (Dative of Interest) eṉṉum āṅkila vilakkaṇa amitiyi orupuṭi yottatu.

nampiḷḷi, nam āḷ, namperumāṉ, namperumāḷ, nammāḻvār.
tam eṉpatu tamatu eṉṟu paṭarkkiyi yuṇarttumēyaṉṟi, namatu eṉṟu uḷappāṭṭut taṉmiyi uṇarttātu. oruvar tamakku arumiyāṉavarit tammoṭupaṭuttik kūṟuvataṉṟip piṟaroṭu paṭuttik kūṟuvatu, iyalpaṉṟeṉpatum aṟika.
maḻavuṅ kuḻavum iḷamip poruḷa
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (uriyiyal, 14)
iḷami meṉmiyiyum uṇarttum.
maḻa - maḻal - maḻali, maḻal - maḻaṟu - maḻaṟṟu - miḻaṟṟu.
miḻaṟṟutal = kuḻanti pōl meṉmiyāy allatu iṉimi yāyp pēcutal.
miḻaṟṟu eṉṉum col tamiḻ eṉak kuṟuki īṟāyiṟṟeṉpatu poruntātu.
āyiṉum, nam pērāciriyariṉ naṉṉōkkam makiḻntu pārāṭṭaṟ pālatē.
iṉi, vēṟu evvakiyil tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyiruk kalāmeṉiṉ, kūṟuvēṉ.
moḻikaṭkup peyar mutalāvatu nāṭu paṟṟiyum, piṉpu makkaḷ paṟṟiyum, ataṉpiṉ moḻiyiṉ taṉmi paṟṟiyum tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa. karunaṭam (kaṉṭam). kuṭaku mutaliyavi nāṭu paṟṟiyum; maliyāḷam, āṅkilam mutaliyavi makkaḷ paṟṟiyum pirākirutam, camaṟkirutam mutaliyavi taṉmi paṟṟiyum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. vaṭamoḻi, teṉmoḻi eṉat tici paṟṟiyum; urutu (pāḷiyam) eṉa iṭam paṟṟiyum peyarpeṟuvatu nāṭṭiṉāṟ peyar peṟuvatu pōṉṟatē. vaḻakkaṟṟa moḻiyāyiṉ, vētamoḻi eṉpatupōl nūlālum peyar peṟum. tēvamoḻi eṉpatu ēmāṟṟup paṟṟiyatām.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakattu
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu, ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkil tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavilli yeṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. kaṭikkaḻakat kālattilum innilimiyē yiruntami. kaḻakac ceyyuṭkaḷālum kaḻakamaruviya vaṉappukkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum. tirāviṭam (tamiḻ), āntiram, kaṉṉaṭam, makārāṭṭi, kūrccaram eṉṉum aintiyum pañca tirāviṭam eṉṟu paṇṭikkālattil vaṭavar vaḻaṅkiyatāl, āriyar vanta piṉpum vintiya malivariyum tamiḻum ataṉ tiripāṉa tiraviṭamumē vaḻaṅkiyami peṟappaṭum. avar varukik kālattilō, vaṭa intiyāvilum tiraviṭam vaḻaṅkiyati, pirākuvīyum, irācamakālum iṉṟum kāṭṭum.
oru nāṭṭil orē moḻi vaḻaṅkumāyiṉ, ataṟkuc ciṟappup peyar tōṉṟātu. pēccu allatu moḻi eṉṉum potup peyarē ataṟku vaḻaṅkum. ōr ūril orē ōr āṟiruppiṉ. ati āṟeṉṟu potup peyarālēyē kuṟippār. iṅṅaṉamē mali, kuḷam, maram mutaliya piṟavum oṉṟēyoṉṟāyiruppiṉ potup peyarālēyē kuṟikkappeṟum. oru moḻi āyiraṅ kaltoliviṟku appāṟpaṭariṉ, palvēṟu karaṇiyam paṟṟit tirital iyalpu. attiripu tiṭutippeṉṟu tōṉṟātu mella mellap paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟum. atu moḻi peyartal eṉappaṭum. toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻiyaṭuttu vaṭapāl vaḻaṅkum tiraviṭamoḻi teluṅkē. ataṉāl, atit tamiḻar vaṭaku eṉṟaṉar. atu piṉpu uyiricivu māṟṟattāl (Harmonic Sequence of Vowels) vaṭuku eṉat tirintatu. nīlamaliyil vāḻum oru cār kaṉṉaṭat tiraviṭarik kuṟikkum paṭakar eṉṉum peyar, vaṭakar eṉpataṉ tiripē. teluṅkiyaṭuttut tirinta perun tiraviṭamoḻi kaṉṉaṭamē.
tamiḻa vaṇikar vaṭakkiluḷḷa moḻi peyar nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntapōtu, annāṭṭār avvaṇikar pēccit tammil (tam+il) moḻi eṉṟu kuṟittirukkalām. il eṉpatu vīṭṭiyuṅ kuṭiyiyum ūriyum uṇarttum. iṟpiṟantār (kuṟaḷ. 915) = kuṭippiṟantār.
aṉpil, kiṭaṅkil, poruntil eṉpaṉa ūrp peyarkaḷ. ūr, nāṭu eṉpaṉa oṉṟiyoṉṟuṇarttalumuṇṭu.nāṭṭāṇ mikkāraṉ eṉpavaṉ ūrāṇmik kāraṉ. āḷmaṟi nāṭu, urattanāṭu, piṅkā nāṭu eṉpaṉa ūrppeyarkaḷ.
tam il moḻiyāvatu tam vīṭṭil allatu nāṭṭil pēcum moḻi. tammil eṉpatu tamil eṉat tokkut tamiḻ eṉat tirintirukkalām. veyyil eṉpatu veyil eṉat tokku vaḻaṅkutali nōkkuka. tamiḻ eṉṉum vaṭivu orucoṟ ṟaṉmippaṭṭu, tamiḻ eṉṉum tiripil moḻippeyarttaṉmi muṟṟiviṭṭatu.

eṉṉum kaṭikkaḻakac ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ. tamiḻavaṇikariṉ vaṭatici moḻipeyar tēyac celavik kūṟutal kāṇka.
teluṅkeṉṉum vaṭuku mutaṟkālattil tamiḻiṉiṉṟum mika vēṟupaṭṭirukka muṭiyātu. vēṅkaṭat tumpar moḻi peyar tēyam eṉṟatu vaṭuka nāṭṭiyē eṉpatu teḷivuṟu tēṟṟam. tām eṉpatu tāmu eṉṟum, tam eṉpatu tama eṉṟum, tampiṉ eṉpatu tammuṭu eṉṟum, il eṉpatu illu eṉṟum, iṉṟum teluṅkil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, tammil allatu tamil eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col akkāla vaṭukiṟku muṟṟum iyalpāṉatē.
alarmēlmaṅki eṉṉum toṭarccol piṉṉar alarmēl eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkuvatupōl, tamil moḻi eṉpatu nāḷaṭivil tamil eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. lakarattiṉiṉṟē ḷakaramum ḷakarattiṉiṉṟē ḻakaramum tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, lakaram nēraṭiyākavō ḷakara vāyilākavō ḻakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭu.
e.ṭu. māl - maḻi, ēlā-ēḻā (paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ maṉiviyi viḷikkum col), kal-kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḻ (karuppu).
nāliki (mūṅkil - nāḷam (uṭṭuḷi) - nāḻi (uṭṭuḷip paṭi).
ḻakaram lakarattiṉ mikap pintiyatātaliṉ, tamil eṉṉum lakara vīṟṟu vaṭivam appeyariṉ toṉmiyiyum uṇarttum.
iṉi, taṉmāṉam tamar eṉṉum coṟkaḷil, tamil eṉṉum lakara vīṟṟu vaṭivam appeyariṉ toṉmiyiyum uṇarttum.
iṉi, taṉmāṉam tamar eṉṉum coṟkaḷil, taṉ tam eṉpaṉa paṭarkki cuṭṭātu conta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal pōl, tamiḻ eṉṉum collilum tam eṉpatu conta eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumāṟu, tamiḻarē appeyarit tam moḻikku iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar eṉak koḷḷalum oṉṟu. cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu cāyaṅkālam eṉṟu tirintapiṉ. cāyam eṉṉum peyareccamē vaṭamoḻiyil cāyuṅkālattik kuṟittalpōl; tamil eṉṉum peyaraṭi peyarttaṉmip paṭṭu moḻiyik kuṟittateṉka. t.saha« - ï.sah«.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl tamiḻ eṉṉum peyarukkuk kūṟappaṭṭa poruṭ karaṇiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, taṉiyāka ḻakarattiyuṭiyatu, tannāṭṭu moḻi, eṉṉum iraṇṭē poruttamāṉavi yeṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉum, ciṟantatu tōṉṟum vari iviyē koḷḷattakkaṉa veṉṟum eṇṇik koḷka.
vaṭuku koṭuntamiḻ nili kaṭantu moḻinili yaṭintapiṉ, vaṭukar (teluṅkar) tamiḻukkiṭṭa peyar aruvam eṉpatu. atu aravam eṉat tirintatu. centamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta koṭun tamiḻ nāṭukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭeṉat tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. piṟkālattil centamiḻ nilapparappu mikac curuṅki viṭṭataṉāl, ataṟkēṟpak koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅ kaḷum paṇṭic centamiḻ nilattiṟkuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiviṭ ṭaṉa. annilimiyiyē,
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭam kuṭamkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
paṉṟi aruvā ataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
cītam malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ.
eṉṉumiṭikkāla veṇpā kāṭṭum.
aruvā ataṉ vaṭakku eṉṟatu, aruvā nāṭṭiyum aruvā vaṭatali nāṭṭiyum. ivi tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa kōṭiyil teluṅka nāṭṭi aṭuttiruntaṉa. ataṉāl nāṭupaṟṟit tamiḻari aruvar eṉa aḻittaṉar teluṅkar. ellippuṟap pakutip peyari nāṭu muḻuvataṟkum iṭuvatu ayalār iyalpu. cintu veḷi paṟṟi nāvalan tēyattip pāracīkar hintu eṉṟum kirēkkar intō eṉṟum kuṟittatiyum; mukalāya aracar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭiyuñ cērttuk karunāṭakam eṉa aḻittatiyum, nōkkuka.

apayam apayam eṉāna ṭuṅkiyē
eṉpatu kaliṅkattupparaṇi.
oruva roruvarmēl vīḻntuvaṭa nāṭar
aruvar aruvar eṉa añci - veru vantu
tīttīttī eṉṟayarvar ceṉṉi paṭivīra
pōrkkaliṅka mīteḻunta pōtu.
eṉpatu paḻiya veṇpā.
aruvar moḻi aruvam.
karunaṭar (kaṉṉaṭar) tamiḻar eṉṉuñ collit tikuḷar eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkuvar. vaṭamoḻi teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉ, tamiḻi ataṉoṭu oppunōkkit teṉmoḻi eṉṟaṉar.
tiraviṭam, eṉpatu tamiḻam eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu muṉṉarē viḷakkap peṟṟatu. tiraviṭam teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellāvaṟṟiyum taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭirunta tamiḻiṉ paṇṭit talimiyi uṇarttum.
tamiḻt tōṟṟam
(tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000)
mutaṟkālam, ā = poruḷ, iṭam. ī-ū
mūvakic cuṭṭolikaḷiṉiṉṟuñ coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyatē tamiḻt tōṟṟamām. itu ceyaṟki moḻi (Artificial Language) allatu iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉappaṭum.
ētu ēṅku ēṇṭu ēṉ.
muntiyal māntarmoḻi yellām icippāṭṭākavē tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatu ecuppercaṉ karuttu. atu tavaṟām. uṇarcci viñciya kātal, iṉpam, veṟṟi makiḻcci, tuṉpam mutaliya maṉa nilikaḷilēyē muntiyal māntar - avaruḷḷum ici vuṇarcciyum, moḻiyāṟṟalum mikkavarē - ici moḻi vāyilāyt tam karutti veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ik kālattum, iṉpa tuṉpa vuṇarcci viñciyapōtu, tattam aṟivunilikkēṟpa icippāṭṭākavum ceyyuḷākavum tam karuttic cilar veḷiyiṭutal kāṇka. mēlum poruḷum oḻuṅkumaṟṟa orucila iciyolit tokutikaḷiṉiṉṟu vevvēṟu poruḷum ilakkaṇa voḻuṅkumuḷḷa peyar viṉiyiṭic coṟkaḷum, mūviṭap peyarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭa īṟukaḷum, vēṟṟumi urupukaḷum kālaviṭi nilikaḷum, piṟavum, amintaṉa veṉṟal, uttikkup poruntāta kūṟṟām.
uri, ceyyuḷ (allatu pāṭṭu) eṉṉum iruvaki moḻi naṭiyuḷ, mutalil tōṉṟiyatu muṉṉatē. atuvum taṉittaṉic collākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ākavē, collē moḻiyalakām (Unit of Speech). uḷḷattil tōṉṟum karuttu, atit terivikkum coṟṟoṭarkku muṟṟum ottataṉṟu. amiya vēṟu pāṭṭiṟ kēṟpa, oru collē pala coṟṟoṭarkkuriya pala karuttit terivikkalām. oru kuḻanti allatu nōyāḷi taṇṇīr eṉṟāl, atu
(1) eṉakkut taṇṇīr vēṇṭum;
(2) atō pār! taṇṇīr;
(3) veḷḷam vīṭṭiṟkuḷ vantuviṭṭatu;
(4) taṇṇīr koṇṭuvā;
(5) intappāl taṇṇīr kalantatu;
(6) iccol taṇṇīr;
(7) itaṉ poruḷ taṇṇīr;
(8) ivviṭukati viṭi taṇṇīr;
(9) ippaṭattiluḷḷatu taṇṇīr;
(10) (Cold) Water eṉṉum āṅkilaccoṟku nēr teṉ col taṇṇīr.
eṉṟu pala karuttit terivippatuṭaṉ, ‘nī kuṭippatu eṉṉa?  eṉpatu pōṉṟa viṉāviṟku viṭiyākavumiruttal kāṇka. naṉṟāyp pēcat teriyāta kuḻantiyum, pēccu valimiyaṟṟa nōyāḷiyum, tāymoḻiyoṉṟē terinta ayal nāṭṭā ṉum, perumpālum taṉiccoṟkaḷālēyē tam karuttit 
terivittal kāṇka. inniliyilēyē moḻi vaḷarcciyuṟāta muntiyal māntaṉum iruntāṉ.
moḻi eḷiya niliyil tōṉṟiya paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarnta māntaṉ amippēyaṉṟi, iṟivaṉāṟ paṭikkap peṟṟu iyaṟkiyāka aṟiyappaṭṭataṉṟu, moḻi iyaṟkiyāṉatāyiṉ ellā makkaḷum kallāmalē orēmoḻi pēcutal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamilli. makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ nākarika nilikkut takka vāṟu, moḻikaḷ vevvēṟu vakippaṭṭum nilippaṭṭum amippukkoṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. cila anākarika māntarmoḻikaḷ paṟavi vilaṅkolikaḷiṉum caṟṟē uyarntaviyākum. ettuṇi uyartaṉic  cemmoḻiyāyiṉum. taliciṟanta aṟiviyal vaḷarcci peṟṟa makkaḷāyiṉum. ovvoru collāy mella mellak kaṟṟālaṉṟi oruvarum tam tāymoḻiyip pēcaviyalātu.
moḻittuṇiyiṉṟiyum māntarkkuk karuttu nikaḻum. ūmiyar nili itaṟkut taliciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭām. aḵṟiṇiyuyiri kaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyum itaṟku oruvakic cāṉṟākum. moḻi yiṉṟik karuttu nikaḻāteṉpatu. ārāycci yillār kūṟṟē. cilar moḻiyik karuttiṟkut tuṇikkoḷvatu, cilar matuvi viṉikkut tuṇik koḷvatu pōṉṟatē. uṇarcci, vēṭki, niṉippu, cūḻvu, tīrmāṉam, iṉpam (uvaki), tuṉpam (aḻuki) mutaliya eṇ allatu toṇ (oṉpāṉ) cuvinili, aiyam (cantēkam, teḷivu ākiyaviyē maṉattoḻilkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṟku moḻittuṇi tēviyilli. kāṭcipporuḷ kaḷellām maṉattilum tōṉṟumātalāl, niṉippum cūḻvum uruvalip pākavē (Imagination) nikaḻum.
moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭinta piṟkālattilum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷi luḷḷa ellā ilakkaṇakkūṟukaṭkum otta pakutikaḷ uḷḷak karuttililli. pāṟkuṭam eṉpatip pāliyuṭiya kuṭam eṉa virippar ilakkaṇiyar. avvirippiluḷḷa aikāravēṟṟumi yurupum uṭiyaveṉṉum kuṟippuppeyar eccamum, uḷḷak karuttililli. atiluḷḷaviyellām pālum atik koṇṭa kuṭamumē. iṅṅaṉamē, viṉi muṟṟiluḷḷa aṟuvaki yuṟuppukkaḷuḷ, mutaṉili yiṭinili iṟati nilikkuriya karuttukkaḷē uḷḷattiluṇṭu. aviyum moḻiyil oruṅkē tōṉṟavilli. mutalil mutaṉiliyē irutiṇi aimpāl mūviṭaṅkaṭkum uriya ellā viṉivaṭiviṟkum potuvā yiruntatu; piṉpu, pālīṟum kālaviṭi niliyum eccamuṟṟu vēṟupāṭum muṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. ivvuṇmiyellām tamiḻ pōṉṟa iyaṉmoḻi vāyilākavē aṟiyamuṭiyum; camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa tirimoḻiyiyum ceyaṟkimoḻiyiyum aṭippaṭi yāy vittārāyiṉ, airōppiyarāyiṉum amerikkarāyiṉum kāriruḷiṟ kāṭṭuvaḻic celvārpōl oṉṟuṅ kaṇṭaṟiyār.
cuṭṭaṭiccollākkam
muccuṭṭukkaḷuḷ, cēymic cuṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēymik karuttut tavira vēṟoṉṟum piṟattaṟkiṭamilli; aṇmic cuṭṭiṉiṉṟu, aṇmi, piṉmi, iḻiki mutaliya oru cila karuttukkaḷē piṟakkiṉṟaṉa; āyiṉ, muṉmiccuṭṭiṉ niṉṟō, tōṉṟal (muṉ varutal) muṉmi, muṟcellal, neruṅkal, poruntal, vaṉital, tuḷittal, turuval ākiya eṇperuṅ karuttuk kaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟku iṭippaṭṭaṉavum ivaṟṟiṟ kiḷittaṉavu māṉa ettuṇiyō nuṇ karuttukkaḷum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa.
tōṉṟal eṉpatu, tāyiṉiṉṟu kuḻaviyum marattiṉiṉṟu tuḷirum tōṉṟuvatu pōṉṟa iyaṟkit tōṉṟalum; vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu māntaṉum vaḷiyiṉiṉṟu eliyum tōṉṟuvatu pōṉṟa ceyaṟkittōṉṟalum, āka iruvakippaṭum. tōṉṟutal muṉvarutalātaliṉ, tōṉṟaṟkaruttilēyē taṟkiḻami piṟitiṉ kiḻami ākiya iruvaki muṉmik karuttum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. muṉkiḷiyum muṉkiyum pōṉṟavi taṟkiḻami muṉmi; muṉpoṟiyum (muṉ pāramum) muṉtūtaṉum pōṉṟavi piṟitiṉ kiḻami muṉmi.
tōṉṟiyapiṉ, uṭaṉēyō kālañceṉṟō, ōr iṭam nōkkic celavu allatu pala ticiyum paṭarcci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. iyaṟkiyāṉa celavellām muṉṉōkkiyatēyātaliṉ, tōṉṟalum celavum, muṟiyē, muṉvarukiyum muṟcelavumē yākum. ivviruporuṭkum aṭippaṭiyāṉa muṉmikkarutti ūkāraccuṭṭē uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ūkāratti allatu ukaratti olikkumpōtu, itaḻ kuvintu muṉṉiṭattic cuṭṭuvati allatu nōkkuvatik kāṇka. cēmparār āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akara mutaliyil (Chamber]s Etymological Dictionary) eṉṉum coṟku moḻipporuṭkaraṇiyam itaḻ muṉṉōkkik kuvintolittal eṉṟu kuṟittiruttalik kūrntu nōkkuka.
muṟcellaccella, cēravēṇṭiya iṭattiṟku nerukkam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. cērntapiṉ poruntal nērkiṉṟatu. iṭi vaḻiyiṟ cuvarum kallum maliyumpōlat taṭi ēṟpaṭiṉ, vaḷi allatu pakkamākat tirumpa nērkiṉṟatu. taṭutta poruḷiyum iṭattiyum tuḷikka muṭiyumāyiṉ, eli cuvariyum māntaṉ maliyiyum tuḷittal nērkiṉṟatu. tuḷittu maṟupuṟaṅ kāṇiṉ atuvē turuval. ataṉpiṉ tōṉṟal mutaliya paḻiya nilimikaḷē mīṇṭum nikaḻum. maṇṇil vēr iṟaṅkutalum, marattil āṇi patitalum pottakattiṟ puḻuvarittalum pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷāyiṉ, vaḷitaliṉṟiyē tuḷittalum turuvalum nikaḻum.
irutiṇiyuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyiyum nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ ceyalkaḷellām ivveṇvakik karuttu vaṭṭattiṟ kuḷḷēyē amikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟik kūrntu kavaṉitta paṇṭit tamiḻar, tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattiṉāl, itupōtuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟṟokutiyuḷ ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟ paṅki ūkārac cuṭṭaṭiyākavē tōṟṟuvittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
ukaraccuṭṭu
ū,u
uṅku,		(pēcukiṟavaṉukku) muṉṉiṭattil
utōḷ, utōḷi 	=	(pēcukiṟavaṉukku) muṉṉiṭattil
uva				
uvviṭam	=	pēcukiṟavaṉukku muṉṉiṭam
unta	=	pēcukiṟavaṉukku muṉṉiṭattiluḷḷa
uvaṉ	=	pēcukiṟavaṉukku muṉniṟpavaṉ
utu	=	pēcukiṟavaṉukku muṉniṟpatu
ukaraccuṭṭuc coṟkaḷellām paṭarkkipōṟ payaṉ paṭuntappaṭiṉum pēcuvāṉukku muṉṉiṭattiyum atiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyumē cuṭṭum. ātalāl ukaraccuṭṭu eṉṟum muṉmic cuṭṭēyaṉṟi, cēymikkum aṇmikkum iṭip paṭṭa iṭimiccuṭṭaṉṟu, itu nīṇṭa kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil ōraḷavu vaḻakkiluḷḷatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ukaraccuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷ
ukaraccuṭṭu vēr taṉittum, ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma eṉṉum aṟumoḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷōṭu kūṭiyum varum. ñavaya meykaḷ ukarat tōṭu kūṭi moḻimutal varum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa, ukaraccuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷiṉ tiripākavēyirukkum. uyirukaram mutalāka varuvatu mutalaṭi; uyir meyyukaram mutalāka varuvatu vaḻiyaṭi. collākkattil, poruḷ mayakkatti nīkkutaṟku, uyirum meyyum palvēṟeḻuttāyt tirikiṉṟaṉa. iṅku, uyirttiripil u-a, u-i, u-o, eṉpavaṟṟiyum, meyttiripil l-r, l-ḷ, ḷ-ḻ, ḻa-ka eṉpavaṟṟiyum, uḷḷattil iruttutal vēṇṭum.
1. tōṉṟutal (muṉvarutal)
u-ul-ur-uru
uru : uruttal = tōṉṟutal, uru - (aru) - arumpu.
arumputal = tōṉṟutal. aruppam = tōṉṟum mīci.
uru = karu. uru = tōṟṟam. vaṭivam, niṟam. uru - uruvu - uruvam. uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumi vaṭivam.
kuru : kuruttal = tōṉṟutal, kuru - kuruttu = tōṉṟum iḷavōli.
kuruttu - kuruntu = veṇkuruttu, kuḻanti. kuruntu - karuntu = marakkaṉṟu (kōṭi). kuru - kurumpi = teṉṉi paṉiyiṉ iḷaṅkāy. kuru - kuruku = kuruttu, kuṭṭi.
kuru - karu = 1. cūl. karuccitittōrkkum  (puṟam. 34).
2. kuḻanti. cērartaṅkaruvi  (pārata. niri. 116).
3. kuṭṭi, kācaṟikkaruvum (cilap. 25:52).
4. piṟappu. karuvit tuṭippa (purapu. kokki. 15).
5. muṭṭikkaru. puṟavukkaruvaṉṉa (puṟam. 34).
6. nilattil tōṉṟum poruḷ. karuveṉa moḻipa (tol. 964).
turu : turu - turuttu eṉpatu iṉṟu muṉtaḷḷutalik kuṟikkum.
nuru : nuru = piñcu. nuru-noru = iḷaṅkatir, piñcu.
puru : puru = kuḻanti. puru - (piru) - piruku = muṟṟāp paṉaṅkiḻaṅku. piruku-pirukku.
ciṟu piñcukaḷip piñcum pirukkum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
muru : muru-muruku = iḷami, iḷamiyāṉa murukaṉ. muruku - murukaṉ. muru-muruntu = kuruttu, taḷir.
muru-muṟi = taḷir. muṟital = tuḷirttal. muṟinta kōlamukiḻ (cīvaka. 2358).
muru - maru - maruvu. maruvutal = tōṉṟutal.
uru : eṉṉum aṭikku muntiyatu ul eṉpatākum. lakaram ḷakaramākat tirivatu pōṉṟē ḷakaram ḻakaramākat tiriyum.
ul - ullari = taḷir. ulavi = taḻi. īyulaviyiṉōppi (iraku, tēṉu, 35). ilirttal = taḷirttal.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷaku = taḷir, taḻi. kuḷ - kuṭṭi. kuḷ - kuḻa - kuḻi = taḷir. kuḻa - koḻuntu. kuḻa - kuḻavu - kuḻaku - kuḻakaṉ. kuḻa - kuḻanti. kuḻavu - kuḻavi.
cul - cūl = karu, muṭṭi. cilirttal = taḷirttal cul - cil - ciṉ - ciṉi. ciṉittal = tōṉṟutal, arumputal, karukkoḷḷutal, taḻittal. ciṉi = moṭṭu, marakkiḷi, cūl, muṭṭi.
kaḷḷukkāy = iḷaṅkāy.
tul - tuḷ - tuḷir - taḷir. tuḷ-taḷ-taḻu-taḻukku. taḻukkutal = taḻittal. taḻu-taḻi=kuḻi.
nul-(nuḷ)-nuḻu-nuḻuval = iḷampākku. nuḻu-nuḻāy = iḷampākku, nul-(nuṉ)-nuṉi-naṉi=arumpu; naṉital = arumputal, tōṉṟutal.
pul - (pal) pallavam = (va.) = taḷir, pul-(puḷ)-piḷ-piḷḷi, piḷ=piḷḷimiyaḻaku, piḷ-pīḷ=iṉaṅkatir. iḷami, karu puḷ-pūḷ-pūṭṭi = iḷaṅkatir. pūṭṭi-pīṭṭi.
mul-muḷ-muḷi. muḷittal = tōṉṟutal. muḷiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ - muḷ - muṭṭu - moṭṭu. muḷ-maḷ-maḷḷaṉ = iḷiñaṉ. maḷ-maḻa-maḻavu-maḻavaṉ=iḷiñaṉ. maḻavum-kuḻavum iḷamip poruḷa (tol. uri. 14). maḻa-maṭa-maṭam = iḷami. maḻa-maḻali-matali=iḷami. piḷḷi.
ḻakaram cilaviṭattuk kakaramākat tiriyum.
e.ṭu. toḻuti - tokuti, muḻi - muki.
ivvakiyil uḻu eṉṉum aṭi uku eṉṟākum.
uku-(uki) - aki. akital = taḷirttal. koy kuḻi akikāñci (kalit. 74).
kuku -
cuku -
tuku -
nuku - nukumpu = paṉaṅkuruttu. nuku - nuṅku = paṉañcuḷi. iḷampaṉaṅkoṭṭi.
puku - pokil = arumpu. pokil - pōkil = arumpu. pōku - pōkku = marakkaṉṟu. (piṅ.).
puku - pō - pōttu = iḷaṅkiḷi. pō pōntu (piṅ.) = iḷampaṉi. pōntu - pōnti = (tivā.) iḷampaṉi.
puku - pū-pūttal = tōṉṟutal.
muku - mukiḻ - arumpu. mukiḻtal = arumputal. mukiḻttal = tōṉṟutal, arumputal, īṉutal. muku-muki = arumpu. muku - mokku - mokkuḷ = arumpu.
mūvakiyulakum mukiḻttaṉa muṟiyē.
mukiḻ-mukiḻam = pērarumpu. mukiḻ-mukiḻi. mukiḻittal = arumpupōṟ kuvital.
mukiḻam eṉṉum col, arumpu eṉṉum poruḷil, mukula mukura, makula, makura eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
kumarikkaṇṭa muḻukkiṉālum, mutaliru kaḻaka vilakkiya muḻutum kaṭikkaḻaka vilakkiyap perumpakutiyum aḻintu pōṉami yālum nīṇṭakālamākat tamiḻar tamiḻip pēṇāmiyālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉālēyē, pala mutalaṭiccoṟkaṭkum vaḻiyaṭiccoṟkaṭkum eṭuttukkāṭṭa iyalavilli yeṉṟaṟika. iṟantupaṭṭa coṟkaḷellām piṟik kōṭṭuḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷa. oru poṟiyaṟiñaṉ oru poṟiyil illāta uṟuppik kaṇṭukoḷvatupōṉṟē, oru moḻi aṟiñaṉum ammoḻiyil iṟantupaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟi avaṟṟoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa piṟacoṟkaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu aṟiyaviyalum eṉka.
2. muṉmi
ūṅku = muṉpu.
ummi = muṟpiṟappu. ummiviṉi .. oḻiyātu  (maṇi. 26: 32).
muṉi = kūriya muṉpakkam allatu mēṟpakkam. nuṉimuṉi. nuṉi - nuti = muṉ. naṭantāḷnuti (cīvaka. 1933 uri). nuti - tuti = muṉ, nuṉi. tutikki = yāṉiyiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa allatu kūriya nuṉiyuḷḷa ki.

tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅkumā ?
kumarik kaṇṭa muḻukkiṉālum paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiya aḻiviṉālum ettuṇiyō ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭiruntum, iṉṉum tamiḻit taṉi moḻiyāy vaḷarkkak kūṭiya pallāyirac coṟkaḷum paṟpala coṟkaruvikaḷum tamiḻil iruntē varukiṉṟaṉa. vaḻakkaṟṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷi vaḻakkāṟṟup paṭuttu vatum ik kālattiṟku vēṇṭiya putuc coṟkaḷip puṉintu koḷvatumākiya iru vaḻikaḷik kiyāḷiṉ, tamiḻ taṉittiyaṅka eḷḷaḷavun taṭiyilli.
1. vaḻakkaṟṟa teṉ coṟkaḷ
āyirak kaṇakkāṉa vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vēṇṭātu pukuttap paṭṭataṉ viḷivāka avaṟṟiṟku nērāṉa viḻumiya teṉ coṟkaḷ ciṟitum peritum muṟṟum vaḻakku vīḻttappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
e.kā. 
vaṭacol			teṉcol
aṉṉam			ekiṉ, ōtimam
āṉmā			ātaṉ, uṟavi, pulampaṉ
āṉantam, 			kutūkalam,
 cantōcam			ulaki, kaḷippu, makiḻcci

lāpam			ūtiyam

mittuṉaṉ			aḷiyaṉ
varucam			āṇṭu
tirī, puruṣar		āṭavar, peṇṭir
iṅkuk kuṟikkappaṭṭa vaṭa coṟkaḷ viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭup perumpālum taṟpava vaṭiviṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nakiccuviyi hāya racam eṉpatum, aviyic cata eṉpatum. paṇiviṭiyic cicuruṣi eṉpatum, tirumaṇattip pariṇayam eṉpatum, kuṭamūkku (kuṭanti), kuraṅkāṭutuṟi, ciṟṟampalam (tilli), paḻamali (mutukuṉṟam), mayilāṭutuṟi, maṟikkāṭu mutaliya centamiḻ nāṭṭūrt taṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷi, muṟiyē, kumpakōṇam. kapitalam, citamparam, viruttācalam, māyūram, vētāraṇiyam eṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷāy ēṟkeṉavē, māṟṟiyiruppatum, ittakiya iḻi ceyali iṉṟum kiyāḷvatum, teṉṉāṭṭu vaṭamoḻi yāḷariṉ variyiṟanta vaṭamoḻi veṟiyi yaṉṟi vēṟetik kāṭṭum?
iṉṟu tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum vaṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṟku vēṇṭiyavu malla; tamiḻar virumpiyavu malla. uṭampiṟkuṭ pukuntu ataṉukku ūṟuceyyum naccup puḻukkaḷiyum iṉṉāp poruḷkaḷiyum ataṉ nalattiṉ poruṭṭu nīkkuvatu pōṉṟē. vēṇṭā vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu vilakkutal vēṇṭum. itaṉit taṭuppavar puṟappakivarum uṭpakivarumāyēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
vaṭacoṟkaṭku iṅkuk kūṟiyatu piṟa coṟkaṭkum okkum.



2. putuc coṟpuṉivu
pala poruḷkaḷ pal vakiyil vēṟupaṭṭiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṟ kellām potuvāka oru taṉmi kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. uyirikaḷ oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi allatu palavaṟṟip piṟappippatu pōṉṟē karuttukaḷum oṉṟu iṉṉoṉṟi allatu palavaṟṟit tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟaṉa. iv vīruṇmikaḷiyum kaṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar. ōraṭiyiṉiṉṟē īṟṟu vēṟu pāṭṭāṟ palvēṟu coṟkaḷit tirittu. avaṟṟāṟ paṟpala poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
