iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷellām, veṇṇiṟattik kuṟikkum veṇ eṉṉum orē yaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantu. veṇmik karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa palvēṟu kāṭcip poruḷkaḷiyuṅ karuttup poruḷkaḷiyuṅ kuṟippaṉavākum. iv vaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta vēṟu cila coṟkaḷumuḷa. avi virivañci iṅku viṭappaṭṭuḷa.
cila poruḷkaṭkup piṟavaṟṟiṟ killāta ciṟappiyalpuṇṭu. ataṉiyum nam muṉṉōr kaṇṭu. ataṟkēṟpap peyarkaḷi amittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.kā. vāḻi (aṭi vaḻavaḻaveṉṟiruppatu).
iṅṅaṉamē piṟa moḻiyāḷarum tattam moḻiyiṟ coṟkaḷi ākkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉ vērp poruḷiyō avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpiyō aṟintukoḷḷiṉ. aṟiviyalum kammiyamum paṟṟiya ellāc coṟkaḷiyum eḷitāyt tamiḻil amittuk koḷḷalām. coṟṟiripiṟ kēṟṟa īṟukaḷ tamiḻil ērāḷamāka vuḷḷaṉa. toḻiṟ peyarīṟukaḷ maṭṭum tal, al, am, ai, ki, vi, ku, pu, u, ti, ci, vi, uḷ, pāṭu, kāṭu, aravu, āṉi, mi, tu, il, āl, aṉi, aṉam, iṉam, aṇam, iṇam, aṭam, iṭam, aram, itam, mutaliyaṉavāka muppatiṟkum mēluḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟoṭu. viṉi mutalīṟukaḷ ceyappaṭu poruḷīṟukaḷ mutaliya īṟṟuvakikaḷiyum, kuṟumip poruḷ. perumip poruḷ mutaliyaṉapaṟṟiya muṉ piṉ ōṭṭu vakikaḷiyum, cērppiṉ ettuṇiyō palavākum. ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya pala moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu āṅkilam kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa īṟukaḷiyum oṭṭukaḷiyum otta colluṟuppu vaḷam, tamiḻ oṉṟilēyē iyalpāka amintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
electricity eṉṉum āṅkilac col, ampar eṉṉum naṟumaṇap  poruḷik kuṟikkum electron eṉṟa kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatu. ampar eṉpatu tamiḻil ōrkkōli eṉavum peṟum. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa aṭiyārkku nallāruriyil ampar eṉṉuñ col vantiruttalāl. atiyum teṉ colleṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu. miṉṉāṟṟalik kuṟittaṟku ampar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu āmpariyam eṉṟoru collit tirittuḷḷaṉar putuc cērivāṇar. ceṉṉi nāṭṭut tamiḻil miṉcāram eṉṉuñ col ēṟkeṉavē puṉiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ati miṉ(ṉam) eṉṟum māṟṟikkoḷḷalām.
Radio eṉṉum āṅkilac col. Radius eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintatu. ilattīṉ poruḷ.  taṇṭu, āri, katir eṉpaṉa. katir eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu katiram allatu katiriyam eṉṟoru colli nām tirittukkoḷḷalām.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ allatu kali muṟikaḷiṉ peyar, avaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭitta āṭpeyarāl allatu iṭappeyarāl amin tuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi appaṭiyē varipeyarttu vittuk koḷḷalām.
e.kā.
galvanism eṉṉum miṉṉākka muṟi kālvaṉi (Galvani) eṉpavarāṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.
galvanization = kālvaṉittal. galvanism kālvaṉiyam. buxite eṉṉum maṇ vaki pirāṉcu nāṭṭil pākkacu (Baux) eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. atip pākkaci eṉṟu varipeyarttuk koḷḷalām.
chemistry eṉṉum āṅkilac col. alckemy eṉpataṉiṉṟu tirintatu. itu al-kimia eṉṉum arapic colliṉ tiripu. al = the (anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum muṉṉoṭṭu); kimia eṉpatu khemia eṉpataṉ tiripu. itu ekiptu (Egypt) nāṭṭup peyariṉ kirēkka vaṭivam. ākavē chemistry eṉpatik kemiyam eṉṟu collalām.
chemist = kemiyaṉ, kemiyar
chemical = kemiya.
cila poruṭ peyarkaḷ aḷavupaṟṟi amintuḷḷaṉa. duoderum eṉpatu paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉṉum ilattīṉ eṇṇup peyar. atu āṅkilattil paṉṉiru viral (aṅkulam) aḷavuḷḷa ciṟu kuṭaṟ pakutiyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu. itip paṉṉīram eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkalām.
Furlong eṉṉum āṅkilac col paṭiccāl nīḷam eṉṉum poruḷatu. fur = furrow (paṭiccāl). paṭiccāl eṉpatu aṇippu eṉṟum collappaṭum. ākavē, furlong eṉpatip paṭiccāl eṉṟō, aṇippu eṉṟō, cāṉīḷam eṉṟō moḻipeyarkkalām.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamiyāku peyarāyuḷḷaṉa. bacterium eṉṉum āṅkilac col ciṟu kuccuppōl tōṉṟum oru vaki nuṇ puḻuviṉ peyar. itu kuccu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum baktron eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉ kuṟumip poruḷ vaṭivāṉa baktron eṉpataṉ tiripu. il eṉpatu tamiḻil oru kuṟumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu (diminutive suffix).
e.ṭu. toṭṭi - toṭṭil = ciṟu toṭṭi
puṭṭi - puṭṭil = ciṟu puṭṭi
kuccu eṉpataṉoṭu ip piṉṉoṭṭic cērppiṉ, bacterium eṉpataṟku nērāṉa kuccil eṉṉum tamiḻccol ām. ciṟu vīṭṭik kuṟikkum kuccil eṉpatu kuṟṟil eṉpataṉ koccit tiripu. cila peyarkaḷ paṉmaṭi yākupeyarkaḷāyuḷḷaṉa.
Mail eṉṉum āṅkilac col pi eṉṉum aṭippaṭip poruḷatu. atu piṉpu, muṟiyē. pikkuḷ iṭum kaṭitaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟik koṇṭu cellum puki vaṇṭiyiyum kuṟittatu. ākavē, Mail eṉpati añcal allatu añcali eṉṟu kuṟikkalām.
ellāc coṟkaḷiyum vērpporuḷ paṟṟiyē moḻipeyarkka vēṇṭiyatilli. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpu nōkki ataṟkēṟpa oru peyariṭalām. im muṟiyilēyē, trin eṉpatip pukiviṭum ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟip pukivaṇṭi eṉak kuṟittaṉar tamiḻp potumakkaḷ. Trin eṉṉum āṅkilac col iḻu (draw) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum trab eṉṉum ilattīṉ mutaṉiliyiṉiṉṟu tirintatu. cycle ai mitivaṇṭi eṉpar nelli nāṭṭār. ati īruruḷi eṉṉat tēviyilli.
paṇṭi nāṭkaḷil ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vanta ellāp poruḷkaṭkum. avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟit tūya teṉ coṟ peyarkaḷi iṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar paṇpaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻp potu makkaḷ. karumpu cālit tīviṉiṉṟum, pukiyiliyum uruḷik kiḻaṅkum amerikka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum, vāṉkōḻi turukki nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantavi. amerikkat taṉṉāṭṭuc coṟkaḷiṉ tiripāṉa (E. tobacco), patata (E. potato) eṉṉum icuppāṉiyac (spanish) coṟkaḷit tamiḻ ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli. im moḻiyuṇarcci piṟkālattil āriyattāṟ keṭṭatu.
cila coṟkaḷ āṅkilattilum tamiḻilum aṭipporuḷum vaḻip poruḷum ottuḷḷaṉa. pen eṉṉum āṅkilac col, tūvu (feather) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum penna eṉṉum ilattīṉ coṟṟiripu. tūval eṉṉum tamiḻc collum attakiyatē.
āṅkilac coṟkaḷi moḻipeyarkkumpōtu vaṇṇaṉi (descriptive) muṟiyik kiyāḷakkūṭātu. collector eṉpatit taṇṭalāḷar eṉṟē moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭum. māvaṭṭa āṭcit talivar eṉṟu kuṟippiṉ, collector eṉpatiyum district administrative head allatu district chief administrator eṉṟu māṟṟal vēṇṭum. atu poruntāmi kāṇka. kāṭciyum karuttumākiya iruvakip poruḷ kaṭkum, coṟkaḷ orumaruṅku kāraṇak kuṟiyāy iruntāṟ pōtum.
ittakiya muṟikaḷik kiyāḷiṉ, ellā āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭu kaḷiyum tamiḻil moḻipeyarttu viṭalām. āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭu kaḷellām mika eḷiya muṟiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻarkku vēṇṭuvatu tamiḻppaṟṟu oṉṟē.  (teṉmoḻi)
tamiḻ tiraviṭa vēṟupāṭu
orukālattil oru nāṭṭil orē moḻiyip pēcik  koṇṭirunta makkaṭkūṭṭam, piṉṉarp palvēṟiṭattiṟ paravip palvēṟiṉa moḻikaḷip pēcum palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyp  (nations) pirintu pōkalām. māntaṉ varalāṟṟu mutaṟkālattiṟ kumari nāṭṭil tamiḻoṉṟē pēcivanta makkaḷiṉam, iṉṟu intiyā muḻutum paravi, tamiḻum teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya irupatiṟkuk kuṟiyāta iṉamoḻikaḷum pēcum makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷākap perukiyum pirintum uḷḷatu.
tamiḻ tirintatu pōṉṟē, tamiḻam eṉṉum collum tiraviṭam eṉattirintuḷḷatu. attiricol mutaṟkaṇ tamiḻiṉ maṟupeyarāka vaḻaṅkip piṉpu tamiḻiyum ataṉ tirimoḻikaḷiyum orē iṉamāka aṭakkuṅ kuṭumpap peyarāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu; itu piṟanāṭṭu moḻinūlaṟiñarāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭiṉum, iyaṟki vēṟupāṭṭālum āriyakkalappālum nērntuḷḷa cila iṭarppāṭu kaḷāl, tamiḻit tirimoḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu pirittu, attiri moḻikaḷi maṭṭum tiraviṭameṉṟu kuṟikkavum, avviraṇṭiyum potuppaṭakkuṟikkat teṉmoḻi eṉṉum colli āḷavum, vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. immuṭipiṟkut tūṇṭum tamiḻ tiraviṭavēṟupāṭukaḷum māṟupāṭukaḷum varumāṟu :

tamiḻ tirāviṭattiṉ ciṟanta vaṭivamātal
1. tamiḻiṉ toṉmi : tamiḻaracariṉ paḻimi.
kiṟittuvukku 1000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa pāratap pōril, utiyaṉ cēralātaṉ eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉ, iru paṭikaṭkum cōṟu vaḻaṅkiṉatākap puṟappāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu (puṟam. 2).
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ pāratattilum, kiṟittuvukku 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vālmīki irāmāyaṇattilum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vaḻaṅkuva tuḷvīḻntak kaṇṇum paḻaṅkuṭi
paṇpiṟ ṟalippirita liṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 955)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷuriyil, paḻaṅkuṭi - toṉṟutoṭṭu varukiṉṟa kuṭiyiṉkaṭ piṟantār ... toṉṟutoṭṭu varutal cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya reṉṟāṟpōlap paṭippuk kālan toṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varutal eṉṟu kūṟiṉār parimēlaḻakar.
cūravātittaṉ, cipi, mucukuntaṉ, tūṅkeyileṟinta toṭit tōṭ cempiyaṉ, kāntamaṉ mutaliya cōḻamaṉṉarellām carittira kālattiṟku mika muṟpaṭṭavar. cōḻarukku muṟpaṭṭavar pāṇṭiyar. mutaṟkālattil, pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉē tamiḻulakam muḻuvatiyum āṇṭāṉeṉṟum, piṉpu oru pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ tampimārāṉa cōḻa cērar tamiyaṉōṭu pakimi pūṇṭu, vaṭakkē pirintu vantu cōḻacēra araciyaṅkaḷi niṟuviṉareṉ ṟum oru vaḻimuṟic ceyti vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.



tamiḻ tirinili
(ki.mu. 20,000 iṉṟuvari)
kumarikkaṇṭa māntar, tamiḻiṉ aivakic coṉṉilik kālattilum, makkaṭperukkam, iyaṟki viḷivukkuṟivu, pañcam, koḷḷi, pōr, paki, vēṟṟiṭaviruppu, tuṇi ceyal vēṭki, vaṇikam, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya palvēṟu karaṇiyaṅka ḷāl, kiḻakkum vaṭakiḻakkum vaṭakkum vaṭamēṟkum mēṟku mākak kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp pirintupēyiṉar. vaṭakiḻakkuc ceṉṟa turēṉiyar (cittiyar) acinilik kālattilum puṇar nilik kālattilum. vaṭamēṟkiṟ ceṉṟa āriyar pakucoṉ ṉilik kālattilum, pirintupōṉatākat terikiṉṟatu. nēr vaṭakkuc ceṉṟavar tamiḻiṉ muṉṉilik kālattilum piṉ ṉilik kālattilum pirintavarātaliṉ. avar ceṉṟaviṭamel lām, palavūrp peyarkaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻc collāyum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāyumiruppatuṭaṉ, avar moḻikaḷum tamiḻoṭu ciṟitum peritum toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
1. ūrppeyarkaḷ
teluṅkanāṭu : ciṟṟūr (cittūr), nellūr, kuṇṭūr, orukal allatu ōrāṅkal (Warangal).
oṭṭaram (oricā) : kaṭakam (Cuttack) = vaḷinta matil, matil cūḻnta nakaram.
pampāy : vēḷūr, vēḷūrakam (Ellora), vēḷāpuram (Velapur), vēḷakam, vēḷ (kirāmam) - (Belgum), vēḷpaṭṭi (Belhutti). vēḷā eṉpatu palavūrkaḷiṉ potup peyarāyuḷḷatu. caḷukkiyar āṇṭa pampāy maṇṭalappakuti vēḷpulam eṉappaṭṭatu.
kuccaram : tuvāraki (Dwaraka). itu erumiyūr (micūr) nāṭṭi luḷḷa tuvariyiṉ (tuvāracamuttiram) peyarāl aminta nakar. tuvar = civappu, cempu. tuvar - tuvari = ceppukkōṭṭiyuḷḷa nakar.
cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici
yuvarā vīkit tuvari yāṇṭu  (puṟam. 201)
akattiyar tuvarāyatippōntu nilaṅkaṭanta neṭumuṭi paṇṇal vaḻikkaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmariyum. patiṉeṇ vēḷiruḷḷiṭ ṭāriyum aruvāḷariyuṅ koṇṭu pōntu eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟu, tuvāraki, vēḷpulam, aruvānāṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ aṇmiyi nōkkumpōtu naṉṟāy viḷaṅkum.
uttaramaṇṭalam : maturi (Muttra), itu talikkaḻaka maturiyiṉ peyar koṇṭatu. ivvaṭamaturiyiṟ piṟanta kaṇṇaṉ (kiruṭṭiṇaṉ) eṉṉum aracaṉ iṭiyar kulattiṟ piṟanta tamiḻa iṭiyarpōl ēṟutaḻuvi maṇanta oru tiraviṭa maṉṉaṉē. kiruṣṇa (karuppaṉ, kariyaṉ) eṉṉum peyariṉ kruṣ eṉṉum mutaṉiliyum, karuḷ eṉṉum teṉcol tiripē. kaḷ-karu-karuḷ. karuḷutal karuttal.
matupura eṉpatu maturā eṉat tirintateṉpatu poruntātu.
pīkār : pāṭalipuram (Patra). itu pātirippuliyūr eṉṉum tamiḻppeyariṉ vaṭamoḻip peyarppu. itu pāṭaliputtiram eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. mutalil teṉṉārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattic cērnta tiruppā tirippuliyūr pāṭaliputtiram eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ piṉ atu vaṭanāṭṭu nakarappeyarāy amintatu. pātiri eṉṉum pūppeyar vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pāṭali eṉat tiriyum.
oṇṇiṟap pātirippūc cērtalāl  (nālaṭi. 139)
vaṅkāḷam kāḷikkōṭṭam (Culcutta). itu kāḷikōyilāṟ peṟṟa peyar.
kāḷi mutaliṟ pālinilat teyvamāyiruntu, piṉṉarp pōril veṟṟi tarupavaḷ (koṟṟavi) eṉṟum, ammi nōyi uṇṭākkupavaḷum nīkkupavaḷum eṉṟum, nampap paṭṭatiṉāl ēṉi nilaṅkaḷiṉ teyvamāyum, tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṉmutalāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭavaḷ. kōyilik kōṭṭam eṉpatu paḻantamiḻ marapu, vētakkāla āriyat teyvaṅkaḷuḷ kāḷi illi. vaṅkañceṉṟa piṉṉarē āriyar kāḷi vaṇakkatti mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. kāḷi eṉṉum peyar karuppi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉcol; kaḷ-kāḷ-kāḷam-kāḷi. muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuṭ koḷkiyum uruva vaṇakkamum kōyil vaḻipāṭum vēta āriyarkkilli.
tamluk. itu tamiḻakam eṉpataṉ tiripāyirukkalām. vaṅkañ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya paṇṭit tamiḻa vaṇikar ippeyari iṭṭaṉar pōlum!
ūr, puram, puri mutaliya tamiḻiṭappeyarīṟukaḷ, tirintum tiriyātum iṉṟum vaṭanāṭṭiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mutalil, puram eṉpatu kōpuramuḷḷa nakariyum puri eṉpatu matil cūḻnta nakariyum kuṟittaṉa.
2. kulappeyarkaḷ
pampāy maṇṭalattiṉ teṉpakutiyil vāḻntavar vēḷir eṉṉum tamiḻa vakuppār.
vāṇiyaṉ (vāṇikaṉ) eṉṉum kuṭippeyar paṉiyā eṉṟum, ceṭṭi eṉṉum kuṭippeyar cēṭ(ṭu) eṉṟum, vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. eṭṭu - eṭṭi - ceṭṭi. eṭṭutal = uyartal. eṭṭam = uyaram. eṭṭi - uyarntōṉ. cirēṣṭa eṉpatu cirēya eṉpataṉ uccattaram (Superlative Degree).
pāṇṭava kavuravariṉ muṉṉōr katiravaṉ marapiṉarātaliṉ cōḻaṉ vaḻiyiṉarumāvar, muccuṭariyum mutalākak koṇṭa mūvaraca marapukaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē iruntuvantamiyum muṉpu pāṇṭiyarum piṉpu cēra cōḻarum vaṭanāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum teṉṉāṭṭuk katirava marapiṟkum potu muṉṉōṉākak kūṟappaṭutalum, ataṉāl cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟamiyum, nōkkuka.
3. centamiḻum koṭuntamiḻum
tamiḻar vaṭakkē cellaccella. taṭpaveppa nilimāṟṟam, cōmpal, pulavariṉmi, tamiḻppuravalariṉmi, putiya cuṟṟuccārpu, tāynāṭṭoṭu toṭarpiṉmi mutaliya karaṇiyaṅkaḷāl, tamiḻ tirintu koṭuntamiḻ eṉappaṭṭatu. tiriyāta teṉṉilattamiḻ centamiḻ eṉappeṟṟatu. cilar koṭuntamiḻ tiruntic centamiḻāyīṟṟeṉpar. avar aṟiyār. tamiḻ vaḷarcciyil muṟpaṭṭa tiruntāta nilikaḷellām kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉpākattilēyē tīrntuviṭṭaṉa.
koṭun tamiḻccoṟkaḷ potuvāy, poruḷ tirinta col, vaṭivutirinta col, olitirinta col, centamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟa col, putuccol eṉa aivakippaṭum.
e.kā.

aḷ (kātu) ... aṭuku - vērmaṟiyāc col
ammu (to sell) - vērmaṟinta col
iṉi, iṟṟittamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟut teluṅkiṟ putuc coṟpōl tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām vērccol kumari nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṉa, eṉṟu koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
talikkaḻaka aḻivum iṭiyīṭum
tōrā. ki.mu. 4500 pōl, pāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ kālattil, arapik kaṭaluḷḷa viṭattu nilappakutiyum, nāvalantīviṉ teṉpakutiyāṉa kumarik kaṇṭamum, kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa. vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭaliṉum arapikkaṭal, muntiyatākum. ataṉālēyē, kaṭal teyvamākiya vāraṇaṉi mēṟṟicit talivaṉ eṉṟum, vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭalit toṭukaṭal eṉṟum, kūṟiṉar.
kuṇāatu kariporu toṭukaṭaṟ kuṇakkum  (puṟam. 6).
talikkaḻakattip puranta pāṇṭiyar eṇpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, akkaḻakattiṉ iṟutikkālap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷuri kūṟum.
peru (mutu) nāri, peruṅ (mutu) kuruku, kaḷariyāviri mutaliya nūlkaḷ talikkaḻakattāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟaṉavākac collap peṟum.
4. iṭikkaḻakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 4000-1500)
talikkaḻakam aḻintu ēṟattāḻa 500 āṇṭukaṭkuppiṉ, kumariyāṟu kaṭaloṭu kalanta iṭattil, alivāy eṉṟō kayavāy eṉṟō, katavam eṉṟō putavam eṉṟō peyar peṟṟirunta tuṟi nakaril, veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ iṭikkaḻakatti niṟuviṉāṉ. aṉṟum āriyarumilli; āriyakkalappuḷḷa nūlumilli.
iṭikkaḻaka irukkiyik kapāṭapuram eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yilakkiyam kūṟum. maturiyi maturāpuri eṉṟu virittalpōl, kapāṭattiyum kapāṭapuram eṉṟu virittirukkalām. katavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu ilakkaṇappōlit tiripām; alivāy allatu kayavāy eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, kapāṭa eṉpatu arippeyar moḻi peyarppām; putavam eṉpatu peyarāyiṉ, atu muḻuppeyar moḻipeyarppām. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam pukār (āṟu kaṭaliṟ pukumiṭam) eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntamiyum, keṭilam kaṭaloṭu kūṭumiṭam kūṭalūr eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuḷḷamiyum nōkkuka.
5. koṭuntamiḻum tiraviṭamum
orukālattil koṭuntamiḻ eṉṟirunta ticimoḻikaḷ (Regional Dialects), piṟkālattil tiraviṭam eṉṉum kiḷimoḻikaḷāyt tirintuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻar aṟiya, mutalāvatu tirinta kiḷimoḻi teluṅkē. atu tirinta kālam ēṟattāḻak ki.mu. 1500. teluṅku nāṭṭiṟkut teṟkē nīṇṭakālamāyt tamiḻoṉṟē vaḻaṅki vantatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari
āyiṭit
tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālap paṉampāraṉār kūṟiyatu pōṉṟē,
neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamut toṭiyōḷ puvamun
tamiḻvaram paṟutta taṇpuṉal naṉṉāṭṭu  (cilap. 8 : 1, 2)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālamvari vēṅkaṭattiṟkut teṟkil tamiḻtavira vēṟemmoḻiyum vaḻaṅkātiruntamiyik kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉi, tiraviṭamum, (1) vaṭatiraviṭam, (2) naṭuttiraviṭam, (3) teṉ tiraviṭam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṉtiraviṭam, iṭit tiraviṭam, piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum aḻikkalām.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilkaḷē tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṉpu avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ pintit tōṉṟiya neṭil kaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kuṟilkaḷum pintiyē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. ekara okarak kuṟilvarikaṭku mikikkuṟi yiruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. ivviru kuṟilkaḷum tōṉṟu muṉṉarē. orucārt tamiḻar vintiyamaliyaṭuttum ataṟkappālum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. avar moḻiyē piṉpu cūracēṉi, mākati, makārāṭṭiram mutaliru pirākirutaṅkaḷākap pirintu pōyirukkiṉṟatu. ap pirākirutaṅkaḷiṉ tiripē inti, vaṅkam, marātti, kucarātti mutaliya iṟṟimoḻikaḷ. ivaṟṟil ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ illi. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ tamiḻāyiruppatuṭaṉ, toṭaramippilum ivi tamiḻiyē muṟṟilum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa eṇkaḷum orumi paṉmi yeṉṉum iraṇṭē. intiyil āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālē uḷa. immoḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum maraput toṭarkaḷiyum paḻamoḻikaḷiyum nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟip pēcum makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr tiraviṭarāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum muṭipiṟkē varamuṭiyum.
marāttiyum kucarāttiyum orukālattil tiraviṭamoḻikaḷāyk koḷḷappeṟṟu, pañcatirāviṭattiṉ iru kūṟukaḷāyk kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. marāttiyi aṭuttut teṟkē vaḻaṅkuvatu teluṅku.
vintiyamalikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa inti, vaṅkam mutaliya moḻi kaḷi vaṭatiraviṭam allatu muṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum, ammali yiyaṭutta marātti, kucarātti mutaliya moḻikaḷi naṭuttiraviṭam allatu iṭittiraviṭam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya moḻikaḷit teṉtiraviṭam allatu piṉtiraviṭam eṉṟum, koḷḷiṉum poruntum.
iṟṟi niliyil inti vaṅka mutaliyavaṟṟi vaṭa nāvalam eṉṟum, marātti kucarāttiyi naṭunāvalam eṉṟum, koḷvatum poruttamām.
tamiḻ vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirintateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟāka, mutaṟkaṇ, teṉtiraviṭattil teṉkōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa teluṅkiṉiṉṟum vaṭakōṭi moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa pirākuvī yiṉiṉṟum, cilacoṟkaḷ īṇṭu eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟum.

e.ṭu.
aḻuttu - attu, iluppi - ippa, urukku - ukku, koḻuppu - kovvu, ceruppu - ceppu, tiruttu - tittu, curuṭṭu - cuṭṭa, neruppu - nippu, paruppu - pappu, perutta - petta, maruntu - mantu, viruntu - vintu.
uruṇṭi, oruttaṉ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ uṇṭi, ottaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻilum tirintiruppiṉum, avaṟṟiṉ tiruntiya vaṭivukaḷum vaḻakkil uḷa, teluṅkil aṅṅaṉamaṉṟu.

kumppu, aṇṭṭāru eṉṟu eḻutuvatu tamiḻ marapaṉṟu.
vaṭakkē cellaccella, moḻiyolikaḷ iṅṅaṉam valuttuk koṇṭē pōkum. itaṟkut toṭakkam teluṅkum muṭivu camaṟkirutamum ākum. teluṅku vaṭa moḻipōl vallōci peṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ, vaṭaticiyāṟ peyar peṟṟiruppatum kavaṉikkattakkatu. tamiḻolikaḷ mutaṟkaṇ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pōl kaṭuvolikaḷum (Surds) polivolikaḷumāy (Scnants) pōṭṭiyiruntu piṉṉark kaṭumiyum polivum nīṅkiṉaveṉpatu, vaṉkāy mīṇṭum meṉpiñcāyiṟṟeṉṟu kūṟuvatupōṉṟatē.
ṅkka, ñcca, ṇṭṭa, ntta, mppa, eṉṉum vaṉkūṭṭolikaḷ teluṅkiṟ peruvaḻakkāy varum; tamiḻil maruntiṟkuṅ kāṇak kiṭiyā. mutaṉ mutal vaṭaticiyāl vaṭaku eṉṟu peyar peṟṟa tum, uṇmiyil vaṭamoḻikku aṭippaṭiyum, teluṅkē.
7. pirākuvīt tirivu
tamiḻ vēṅkaṭattiṟku vaṭakkē ceṉṟu tiraviṭamāyt tirinta tuṭaṉ, tiraviṭamoḻikaḷum vaṭakkē cellaccella mēṉ mēlum tirintum, ciṟuttum citintum, citaṟiyum, ilakkiya maṟṟum, āriyamāka māṟiyum, pōyiṉa.
vaṭamēṟkōṭit tiraviṭa moḻiyāṉa pirākuvīc coṟkaḷ varumāṟu :

nī kāca = nī pōkiṟāy.
nāpiṉ tēr ē? = uṉ peyar eṉṉa?
kaṉā pāva hamē cuṉ urāṭṭī tūlik = eṉ appaṉār antac ciṉṉa uṟiyil (vīṭṭil) kuṭiyirukkiṟār.
kh, gh mutaliya cila mūccoli yeḻuttukkaḷum, cār (char) tantam mutaliya inti vaṭamoḻiccoṟkaḷum, pirākuvīyiṟ kalantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
perumpāl mēli moḻinūlaṟiñar, anākarika nili nākarika nilikku muntiya teṉṉum neṟimuṟiyik kuruṭṭut taṉamāyp piṉpaṟṟi, tiraviṭam tiruntit tamiḻāṉa teṉṟum, pirākuvī muntunili moḻiyeṉṟum, tamiḻar vaṭamēṟkiṉiṉṟu peluccittāṉa vaḻiyāyt teṟku vantāreṉṟum, vantavaḻiyil peluccittāṉam iruppatāl aṅkattu moḻi anākarika niliyil uḷḷateṉṟum, teṟkuvantapiṉ tammoḻiyi vaḷarttuk koṇṭāreṉṟum, uṇmikku muṟṟum māṟākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
oru moḻiyiṉ coṟkaḷ, muntunili, citinili ākiya iru niliyilum kuṟuvaṭivu koṇṭu niṟkum. pirākuvīccoṟkaḷ kuṟuvaṭivu koṇṭiruppatu, citiniliyēyaṉṟi muntuniliyaṉṟu. vāykaḷ eṉṉum oru colliṉ citivi nōkkiṉum iv vuṇmi viḷaṅkiviṭum.
vaḻi - (vayi) - vāy = uṇavu pukum vaḻi. kaḷḷutal kalattal allatu kūṭutal. paṉmi oru poruḷiṉ kūṭṭamātalāl, kaḷ eṉṉum col paṉmiyīṟāyiṟṟu.
vāy - pā (pi.) kaḷ-k (pi.). vāykaḷ - pāk (pi.) iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
8. ticimoḻit terippu (Regional Dialectic Selection)
tamiḻil mikunta colvaḷa muṇṭu. oru celvattāy vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu, oruvakippaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru putalviyum eṭuttuccelvatu pōl, tamiḻiluḷḷa oru poruṭ pala coṟkaḷum ovvoṉṟi ovvoru ticimoḻiyum kiyāṇṭuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. ïš(bj.), maṉi(ka), vīṭu(ma.).
9. kuṭiyēṟṟap pātukāppu (Colonial Preservation)
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷik koṇṭa kumarik kaṇṭam muḻukippōṉamiyālum, pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷiyum koṇṭa mutaliru kaḻakavilakkiyam muṟṟum aḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭamiyālum, iṉṟu oru cila paḻañcoṟkaḷiṉ muntu vaṭivic cila ticimoḻikaḷē tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
e.ṭu.
cey - ki. chey (te) = ki.
al - aṉṟu alli (ka.) = aṅku
aritu - ātu (etirmaṟiyiṭi, niliyum īṟum). arutu (ma.)
nīṅkaḷ - niṅkaḷ - niṅṅaḷ (ma).
nīṉ-niṉ, nīm-nim, nīṅkaḷ-nīṅkaḷ (vēṟṟumiyaṭi).

nūṉ (nī), nūm (nīr) eṉpaṉa nīṉ, nīm eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ mūlamā yiruntu vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉa tamiḻ muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ. nuṉ, num eṉpaṉa avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumiyaṭikaḷ (Oblique Bases). iviyē marātti, inti mutaliya moḻikaḷil tu, tum eṉat tirintu, eḻuvāy vēṟṟumippeyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyar iruvaki vaḻakkiṟkum māṟāka, neṭumutal kuṟukiya num eṉṉum tiri vēṟṟumiyaṭiyi eḻuvāy vēṟṟumip peyarākak kūṟiyiruttalāl (tol. 325, 326, 623). avar kālattil (ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) marātti tiraviṭa moḻiyākak karutappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉṟum. atil num eṉṉum vaṭivam muṉṉilip paṉmi mutalvēṟṟumip peyarāka vaḻaṅkiyirukkalāmeṉṟum, karuta iṭamuṇṭu. nakaram takaramākat tirivatu iyalpē.

ulakam eṉṉum col makkaḷ eṉṉum - poruḷiṟ palar pālīṟāy varumpōtu, intiyil log eṉṟu eṭuppoli koḷḷum.
e.ṭu. hamlōk = nāṅkaḷ, tumlōk = nīṅkaḷ.
ilakkaṇa amitikaḷ
iṟantakāla viṉimuṟṟum eccamum
tamiḻiṟ ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu āvīṟṟu viṉic col, iṟantakāla viṉiyeccamākum. intiyil ivvāypāṭṭuc col iṟantakāla muṟṟum eccamumākum.
e.ṭu.
mutaṉili	 	i.kā. muṟṟum eccamum
ā = vā 			āyā = vantāṉ, vantu.
pōl = col 	pōlā = coṉṉāṉ, colli.
ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭuccol, tamiḻil oru kālattil muṟṟākavum iruntatupōṉṟē, ceyyā eṉṉum vāypāṭum iruntirukkalām.
jāttā hi, pōlttā hi mutaliya nikaḻkāla intiviṉi muṟṟukkaḷ. pōttāṭu, cepputtāṭu mutaliya teluṅku nikaḻkāla viṉi muṟṟukkaḷi oru maruṅku ottamintiruttal kāṇka.
muṉṉili viṉi
iya eṉṉum tamiḻ viyaṅkōḷīṟu, intiyil iyē eṉṉum matippuṟa vēvalīṟākat tirintuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. pōliyē = colluṅkaḷ, colluka.
kūṭātu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ollātu eṉṉum tamiḻ etirmaṟi viṉiccol, teluṅkil ottu - vantu eṉat tiriyum. atu piṉ intiyil mat eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
e.ṭu. pōkavattu = pōkavēṇṭām (teluṅku) 
jāmat = pōkavēṇṭām (inti)
toḻiṟpeyarum nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccamum
tamiḻil al īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar viyaṅkōḷ eṉṉum ēval vakiyākavum payaṉpaṭuvatupōl, intiyil ṉā īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar ēvalākavum āḷap peṟukiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. karṉā = 1. ceyal (ceyki) - toḻiṟpeyar.
2. cey, ceyyuṅkaḷ - ēval.
iṉi, karṉā allatu ataṉ tiripāṉa karṉē vāypāṭu etirkāla viṉiyeccamākavum intiyil vaḻaṅkuvatu, ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṉum poruḷil ceyyal vēṇṭum allatu ceyal vēṇṭum eṉpatu, tamiḻil vaḻaṅkuvati orupuṭi ottatē.
ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi mutaṉilikaḷ
intiyil, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi mutaṉ nilikaḷ, āv (vā) jāv (pō) eṉṉum ēvalorumiyuṭaṉ kūṭi, iṟantakāla viṉiyeccap poruḷpaṭum. itil kar eṉṉum iṟantakāla niṟivu viṉiyecca vīṟu tokkatākak koḷḷap peṟum.
e.ṭu. sūṉ = kēḷ. sūṉjāv = kēṭṭuviṭṭup pō.
tēk = pār. tēk āvō = pārttuviṭṭu vārum.
tamiḻil ellā viṉi mutaṉilikaḷum vā. pō eṉṉum ēvalorumiyuṭaṉ kūṭi nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccap poruḷ paṭum.
e.ṭu. ceyvā = ceyyavā, pārpō = pārkkappō - iruvā = irukkavā, viḻupō = viḻappō.
māṟu eṉṉum iṭiccol
māṟu eṉṉum iṭiccol, ēkāra īṟēṟṟuk kaḻakac ceyyuṭkaḷil ētupporuḷil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu (puṟam. 4, 20, 22, 92, 271, 380, naṟ. 261).
aṉiyi yākaṉ māṟē  (puṟam. 4)
itaṟkup paḻiya vuriyāciriyar attaṉmiyi yātalāl eṉṟu poruḷurittu, māṟeṉpatu ētup poruḷpaṭuvatōr iṭiccol eṉṟu ilakkaṇak kuṟippum varintuḷḷār. cēṉāvariyar mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip poruṭkaṇ varum māṟu eṉṟum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar māṟeṉṉum iṭiccol viṉiyi aṭuttuk kāraṇapporuḷ uṇarttiniṟṟaliṉ eṉṟum, ilakkaṇa viḷakka vuriyāciriyar mūṉṟāvataṉ poruḷavāy varum māṟu eṉṟum, urittuḷḷaṉar.
intiyil kē eṉṉum urupoṭu cērntu varum mārē eṉṉuñcol, ētupporuḷiṭic collākavē vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. u ātmīkē mārē = anta māntaṉālē.
i patpūkēmārē = it tī nāṟṟattiṉāl.

12. vaṭatiraviṭa moḻikaḷ āriyamāy māṟiyami
1906 - ām āṇṭu intiya moḻiyāyvukkaṇakku (Linguistic Survey of India) eṭutta kiriyarcaṉ turimakaṉār, arittiraviṭa naṭimoḻikaḷ (Semi-Dravidian Dialects) eṉṉum talippiṉ kīḻppiṉvarumāṟu varintiruppatu. tiraviṭarukkum tamiḻarukkum ōr eccarikkiyāyuḷḷatu.
vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa pala tiraviṭa marapiṉar tattam iṉa moḻiyi viṭṭuviṭṭu ētēṉumōr āriya naṭi moḻiyip pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar. halapī eṉappaṭum moḻi itaṟkoru nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭu. atu ivvāyvuk kaṇakkil marāttiyoṭu cērttuk kūṟappaṭum. atu marāttiyiṉālum cattīcakaṭiyiṉālum mikutiyum taḻuvappaṭṭa kalavimoḻi.
iṅku, atupōṉṟa naṭimoḻikaḷuḷ iraṇṭit tiraviṭak kuṭumpattiṟkup piṟcērkkiyākak kuṟippōm. itaṉāl attakiya moḻikaḷil āriyattiṉ tākkuṟavu ettuṇi niṟivākavuḷḷateṉpati, māṇavaṉ aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. avviru naṭimoḻikaḷum amaravōttiyic cērnta latāṭi allatu rattāṭi eṉappaṭuvatum, naraciṅpurattilum cintu vārattilum pēcappaṭum pariyā eṉappaṭuvatum, ākum. iv vāyvukkaṇakkiṟkākat tokukkappeṟṟa kuṟippiṉpaṭi, avaṟṟip pēcuvōr toki varumāṟu :
latāṭi (Ladhadi) 		2,122
pariyā (Bharia)		  330
	mottam		2,452	
ivviru naṭimoḻikaḷum muṉpu kōṇṭiyōṭu (Gondi) cērkkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. eṉiṉum, iṉṟu avi muṟṟum āriya vaṇṇamāyviṭṭaṉa.

mēliyāriyam
vaṭa tiraviṭattip pēciya vaṭanāvala makkaḷ. vaṭamēṟkākac ceṉṟu mēliyāciyāviṟ cumēriya nākarikattip parappiyum naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta āriya nākarikattiṟku aṭikōliyum, palvēṟu nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāyp pirintu pōyiṉar eṉpatu, mokeñcōtarō - arappā nākarikattiṟkum cumēriya nākarikattiṟkumuḷḷa oṟṟumiyālum, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkari nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaṭkum tamiḻar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅ kaṭkumuḷḷa oppumiyālum, mēliyāciya moḻikaḷilum vaṭa āppirikka moḻi kaḷilum airōppiya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
vaṭamēli yāppirikkāvic cērnta kāṉāmoḻiyilum, vā, pō, tūkku mutaliya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷāy amintiruppatu, mikak kavaṉikkat takkatām.
mokeñcōtarō muttiriyeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻeḻuttuk kaḷiṉ mūla vaṭivik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atu avi tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭiyāta niliyil vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar kiyāṇṭa eḻuttumuṟi eṉpati yallatu, tamiḻar vaṭakkiṉiṉṟu teṟkē vantār eṉṟu uṇarttātu. mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ  ceyyappeṟum putuppuṉivukaḷiṉ (Inventions) paḻiya amippukkaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkivaruvatum, atu intiyarē avaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭittār eṉṟu kāṭṭāmiyum, kāṇka.
āriyak kuṭumpattic cērnta airōppiya moḻikaḷuḷ, vaṭamēṟkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkivarum tiyūttāṉiya (Teutonic) moḻikaḷ, ciṟappāka āṅkila cākacaṉiyam (Anglo-Saxon), tamiḻukku mika neruṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. annerukkattik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ varumāṟu :
(1)	viḻuttam perumpālum mutalaciyil viḻutal.
(2)	taṉikkuṟiliyaṭuttamey uyiroṭu puṇariṉ iraṭṭal.
(3)	irumiyeṇ iṉmi.
(4)	viṉikaḷ tuvvīṟotta īṟṟāl iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭal, e.ṭu. walked, told, burnt.
(5)	tiyyotta muṉṉili yorumiyīṟu paṇṭi yāṅkila vaḻakki liruntami.
	e.ṭu. nī yirutti - Thou art.
(6)	cila aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷ āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum potuvāyiruttal.
(7)	āriyattiluḷḷa tamiḻccoṟkaḷip potuvāka āṅkilamē tamiḻukku neruṅkiya vaṭiviṟkāṭṭal.
tiyūttāṉiyat tamiḻccoṟkaḷ

15. coṉmāṟṟat tolivukkaṇippu (Glotto-telemetry)
āriyattiṟkum tamiḻukkum (tiraviṭattiṟkum) cila coṟkaḷ tām potuvāyirukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, avviru kuṭumpamoḻi kaṭkum vēṟu yātoru toṭarpumilli yeṉṟum, palar karutukiṉṟaṉar. pala tiraviṭac coṟkaḷ āriyattāṟ kaṭaṉmaṭṭum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi, tiraviṭakkūṟukaḷ āriya moḻiyamippiṟkuṭ pukavilliyeṉṟu, pērā. parō tam camaṟkiruta moḻi (The Sanskrit Language) eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār. itu teṉmoḻi vaṭamoḻit toṭarpit talikīḻāka vittu āyntataṉ viḷivākum.
oru peruntāymoḻi iyaṟkiyākap paravumpōtu, āyiraṅkal toliviṟkorumuṟi ataṉ paḻañcoṟṟokutiyil ēṟattāḻa mūṉṟi loru paṅki iḻakkiṉṟatu. atōṭu putuccoṟkaḷum putiya collamiti yilakkaṇa vamitikaḷum pukukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuṇmiyi aṭippaṭiyāka vittu oru moḻi taṉ mūlamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu evvaḷavu tolivu vilakiyuḷḷa teṉṟō, ettuṇit tirintuḷḷa teṉṟō, kaṇikkaviyalum, ituvē coṉmāṟṟat tolivuk kaṇippām.
airōppiya āriyamoḻikaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pēraḷavu tirin tuḷḷataṟku, avi vilakic ceṉṟuḷḷa cēymi aḷavē karaṇi yamām. āyiṉum, iṉṟum avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉoṭṭukkaḷum piṉṉoṭṭukkaḷum perumpālum tamiḻc coṟkaḷākavē yuḷḷaṉa.
e.ṭu. kum - kummal L. cumulus. kum - L. cum.
ik kum eṉṉum collē, com, con, col, co eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, sym, syn, syl eṉṟu kirēkkattilum, sam eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyilum, tirintu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷit tōṟṟuvittuḷḷatu. tamiḻkkūṟu āriyaccollamippiṟ kaluntuḷḷateṉpataṟku, iḵtoṉṟē pōtiya cāṉṟām.
tamiḻit talikīḻāy vittāynta kālṭuvelārum, āriyattiṟkum tiraviṭattiṟkumuḷḷa toṭarpi allatu oppumiyip piṉvarumāṟu kuṟittuḷḷār.
(1)	kirēkka moḻiyilum tiraviṭattilum ṉakaram uṭampaṭu meyyāka varukiṉṟatu.
(2)	īriṉa moḻikaḷilum paṭarkkip pakāp peyarkaḷum viṉikaḷum pālīṟēṟkiṉṟaṉa.
(3)	paṭarkki yorumic cuṭṭuppeyar allatu oṉṟaṉ pāṟpeyar, īriṉattilum takaravīṟu (d or t) koṇṭuḷḷatu.
(4)	ilattīṉiṟ pōṉṟu tamiḻiṟ palaviṉpā līṟu akaramākum.
(5)	īriṉattilum akaram cēymiyiyum ikaram aṇmiyiyum cuṭṭum.
(6)	pāracīkattilum tiraviṭattilum takara iṭinili iṟanta kālaṅ kāṭṭum.
(7)	mutaṉilimey iraṭṭittu iṟantakālaṅ kāṭṭuvatu īriṉattilu muṇṭu.
(8)	īriṉattilum palaviṉikaḷ mutaṉili nīṇṭu toḻiṟ peyarākiṉṟaṉa.
iṟṟit tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ

maliyāḷam teṉṉāṭṭu moḻiyum paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻiṉ tiripumāyiṉum, tuñcattu eḻuttaccaṉiṉ aṭimit taṉattāl, piṟa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷiṉum mikutiyākavum aḷaviṟantum āriya vaṇṇa mākkappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam
tamiḻiṉ piṟantakam muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam eṉpatu, mukkaḻaka (muccaṅka) varalāṟṟālum, cilappatikāra aṭiyārkku nallāruriyālum, (P.T.) cīnivāciyaṅkār, cēciyaṅkār, irāmaccantira tīṭcitar mutaliyōr eḻutiya varalāṟṟu, nūlkaḷālum, eṉ mutaṟṟāy moḻi muṉṉuriyālum, teḷḷat teḷiyat terintatu.
tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam kumarik kaṇṭamē eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa :
(1) tamiḻum ataṉoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷum nāvalantēyattiṟkuḷḷēyē, vaḻaṅkutalum; teṉmoḻi vaṭakkē cellac cella āriyappāṅkil valuttum urut teriyātu tirintum oṭuṅkiyum ilakkiyamaṟṟum iṭiyīṭu paṭṭum, teṟkē vara vara mellōci koṇṭum tiruntiyum virintum ilakkiyamuṟṟum ceṟintum; iruttal.
(2) nāvalat tēyattiṟku veḷiyē tiraviṭa moḻiyiṉmiyum, mēli moḻikaḷiluḷḷa teṉ coṟkaṭkellām tamiḻilēyē vēriruttal.
(3) teṉmoḻik kuṭumpattic cērtta tamiḻ maliyāḷam kaṉṉaṭam teluṅku ākiya nāḷperumoḻiyuḷ muḻut tūymi yuḷḷa tamiḻ teṉṉāṭṭil teṉkōṭiyil vaḻaṅkutal.
(4) tamiḻnāṭṭuḷḷum teṟkē cellac cellat tamiḻ tiruntiyum ciṟantum iruttalum nelli vaṭṭārattil colvaḷam mikutalum coṟkaḷ tūya eḷiya muṟiyilum vaḻaṅkutal. 
	(tiruttak kalliṟkut teṟkiṭṭup piṟantavaṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkum iti uṇarttum).
	tamiḻnāṭu, tamiḻnāṭar eṉṟu pāṇṭiyanāṭum pāṇṭi yarumē peyar peṟṟaṉar.
(5)	tamiḻ, vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum teluṅku mutaliya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilumuḷḷa, vallolikaḷiṉṟi, muppāṉ perum pālum eḷiya polivolikaḷik koṇṭiruttalum eṭṭum pattu, paṉṉiraṇṭumāka meyyolikaḷ koṇṭa moḻikaḷ āttirēliyāvilum ataṉiyaṭutta tīvukaḷilum vaḻaṅkutal.
(6)	tamiḻ muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭintu muttamiḻāṉapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa talikkaḻakam 49 nāṭukaḷik koṇṭu īrāyiraṅkal tolivu nīṇṭirunta teṉkōṭip paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅkari maturiyil iruntamiyum, kumarik kaṇṭat tōṟṟattiṉ eṇṇiṟku meṭṭāt toṉmiyum, atu varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē muḻukip pōṉami.
(7)	teṉṉimaram āttirēliyat tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟē piṟateṉ kiḻakkut tīvukaṭkuk koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatākac collap paṭuvatum, kumarikkaṇṭattil ēḻteṅku nāṭiruntamiyum, teṉ eṉṉuñ col teṉṉi marattiyum teṟkut ticiyiyuṅ kuṟittal.
(8)	paṇṭit tamiḻc ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nīr nāyum, uriyāciri yarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kārōtimamum (kāraṉṉam) āttirēliyāviṟkut teṟkiluḷḷa tākamēṉiyat tīvil (Tasmania) iṉṟumiruttal.
(9)	vaṇikattāl vanta iraṇṭōr ayalnāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷum nilittiṇi (tāvara) vakikaḷut tavira, maṟṟellāk karup poruḷkaḷum, kāla vakikaḷum nilavakiḷumākiya mutaṟporuḷum, teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuc ciṟappāka uriyaviyē paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruttal. kuḷir nāṭṭiṟkuriya kōtumi vāṟkōtumi mutaliyaṉavum, paṉikkaṭṭi uṟipaṉi mutaliyaṉavum teṉṉāṭṭil viḷiyāmi; paṇṭiyilakkiyattuc collappaṭāmi.
(10)	makkaḷiṉ nākarikat toṭakkattiyuṇarttum aintiṇi makkaṭ pākupāṭum, kuṟiñci makaḷir taḻiyuṭiyum, nāṭṭāṭcikku muṟpaṭṭa ūrāṭciyum, akapporuṭ ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum, aintiṉi nilappākupāṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅkum aṭuttaṭuttu amittirāmi.
(11)	tamiḻ makkaḷ paḻaṅkaṟkālattiliruntu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē toṭarntu vāḻntu vantiruttalum, avarkku vantēṟik karuttiṉmi.
(12)	tamiḻar piṟa nāṭṭiliruntu vantāreṉpataṟkup paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattil oru cāṉṟu miṉmi.
(13)	teṉṉāṭu, teṉṉar (teṉṉāṭṭār), teṉmoḻi, teṉṟamiḻ, teṉṉavaṉ (pāṇṭiyaṉ) teṉkali eṉṉum peyarkaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantuḷḷami.
(14)	paṇṭit tamiḻar tam muṉṉōrit teṉpulattār eṉṟaḻit tamiyum; iṟanta muṉṉōr iṭam teṉpulam teṉṉulaku eṉṟum, kūṟṟuvaṉ teṉṟicik kiḻavaṉ, teṉṟici mutalvaṉ, teṉpulakkōṉ eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruttal.
(15)	ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam colvaḷam coṟṟūymi mutaliya ellā vakiyilum tamiḻ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉuñ ciṟantirut talum, teṉmoḻi vaḷarcciyiṉ muntu nilikaḷiyellām tamiḻē kāṭṭi niṟṟalum. tāymoḻi varalāṟṟiyum iṉa varalāṟṟiyum varitaṟkēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷit tamiḻē tāṅki niṟṟalum.
(16) teṟku vaṭakkut teriyātavaṉ, teṟkum vaṭakkumāyt tirikiṉṟavaṉ. teṉvaṭal, teṉpalli, vaṭapalli (aṇikaḷ) mutaliya vaḻakkukkaḷil, teṉṟici muṟkuṟikkap peṟutal.
(17) eṇṭicit talivaruḷ vaṭapāl mūvar tavira ēṉiya talivar kumari nāṭṭiṟkē poruttamāyiruttal.
(18)perumpālum coṟkaḷ kottuk kottākavum kulaṅ kulamākavum toṭar toṭarākavum amintu tamiḻilēyē vaḻaṅki varutal.
(19)āppirikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ īṉ maruttuva vaḻakkam pōl, tanti paṭukki, kuṟavariṭi vaḻaṅkiyatākac collap peṟutal. kuṟatti piḷḷipeṟak kuṟavaṉ maruntu kuṭittāṉ.

tamiḻnāṭṭaraciṉ tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭṭuc cīrtiruttam
kumarināṭṭuc civaṉ (cēyōṉ) kōvilkaḷiṟ kurukkaḷ allatu paṇṭāram eṉṉum vakuppārum, tirumāl kōvilkaḷil nampimār eṉṉum vakuppārum, kāḷi kōvilkaḷil uvaccar eṉṉum vakuppārum, pūciyum potu vaḻipāṭum taṉit tamiḻil naṭatti vantaṉar.
tirukkōviṟ paṇikaḷik kavaṉittaṟku, ūrtoṟum ūravip pirivāṉa kōyil vāriyam eṉṉum kuḻu viruntatu. tali nakarkaḷilum kōnakarkaḷilum uḷḷa peruṅ kōvilkaḷil, aracariṉ nēraṭi yāṭcikku kuṭpaṭṭa mutu kēḷvi (Supervising officer), iḷaṅkēḷvi (Subordinate supervising officer) eṉṉum īratikārikaḷ iruntu vantaṉar.
nīṇṭa kālamākap puṉciṟu teyvaṅkaḷiyē pōṟṟi vantavarum kaṭavuḷiyalpi yaṟiyātavarumāṉa āriyar, ki.pi. 5 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṉṉarē, mūvēntariyum palvēṟu vakiyil vayappaṭutti, ākamam, caṅkiti, tantiram eṉṉum mūvakit toḻu maṟikaḷi iyaṟṟi, pirāmaṇarē tirukkōvilkaḷil vaḻipāṭu ceytu varumāṟu tiṭṭañ ceytu viṭṭaṉar. tamiḻar kōvilkaḷil āriyarāṭci pukuntatu, kaṟiyāṉ puṟṟiṟ pāmpu kuṭi koṇṭatu pōṉṟatē.
ātalāl, etaṟkum añcātum evvetirppiyum poruṭ paṭuttātum, piṉ varumāṟu cīrtiruttaṅkaḷi uṭaṉaṭiyākac ceyvatu tamiḻ nāṭṭaraciṉ talimēl viḻunta taliyāya kaṭamiyām.
(1)	kōvil vaḻipāṭu tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟal.
(2)	av vam mata nampikkiyum teyvap pattiyum kalvit takutiyum paṇip payiṟciyum tūyavoḻukkam uḷḷa, pala vakuppārum pūcakarāka amarttap peṟal.
(3)	pūcakar pataviyilum kiyaṭiñar (Trustees) pataviyilum toṭarmarapu (Hereditariness) nīkkappaṭal.
(4)	aṟiñarum ārāycciyāḷarum aṟikkiyāḷarum uyar pataviyārum potumakkaḷum aracarum, tiruvuṇṇāḻiki yallāta kōvilakattuṭ pukavuc cīṭṭup peṟṟup pukaviṭal.
(5)	peruṅ kōvilkaḷiṉ varumāṉattiloru pakutiyip potu nalap paṇikkuc celaviṭal.
(6)	aṟaniliyap pātukāpput tuṟi yamiccar kaṭavuḷ nampikki yuṭiyavarā yiruttal.
(7)	uruvilā vaḻipāṭṭip paṭip paṭiyākap potumakkaḷiṭip pukuttal.
kaṭavuḷ ellārkkum potuvāṉa aṉpārnta tantiyāta 
lālum, kaṇakkaṟṟa ulakaṅkaḷi yuṭimiyālum, avarukku oru tēvi yumiṉmi yālum, arumi makkaḷ pōṉṟa aṉittuyirum iṉpamāka vāḻvatē avar viruppātalālum, vāḻkkiyi ṉiṉṟu matatti vēṟākap pirippatum, tāḻttappaṭṭa vakuppiṉar kaṭavuḷ irukkiyāṉa kōvilukkuṭ pukuvatit taṭuppatum, kōviṟ cottu kōviṟ paṇikkē celaviṭappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉpatum, kaṭavu ḷiyalpi aṟiyātavarum avarukku māṟāṉavarumāṉa taṉṉala vakuppāriṉ ceyalātaliṉ atip poruṭ paṭuttātu potu nalap paṇiyil īṭupaṭal vēṇṭum.
2. matac camanōkku
civaṉiyam, māliyam, ulakiyam (lōkāyatam allatu cārvākam) eṉṉum mūṉṟē toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻa mataṅka ḷāyiṉum, iṉṟu veḷināṭṭu mataṅkaḷum tamiḻariṭip pukun tiruppatālum, am matattārit tamiḻa rallar eṉa vilakkal kūṭāmiyālum, iṟṟi muṟippaṭi, camaṇam, puttam, kiṟittavam, icalām eṉṉum mataṅkaḷiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭu mataṅka ḷākavē kūṟal vēṇṭum.
ulakiyam oṉṟu maṭṭum ulakam muḻumikkum toṉṟu toṭṭup potuvākum.
mataṅkaḷellām, tēviyam (Theism) allatu uḷḷiyam (ātikam), tēviliyam (Atheism) allatu illiyam (nātikam) eṉa iru vakippaṭṭiruppatāl, mataviyal vāyilāka makkaḷi oṉṟu paṭuttal iyalātu. ataṉālēyē, intiya oṉṟiyamum (Indian Union) mataviyalit taḻuvātu ulakiyaliyē (Secularism) taḻuviyuḷḷatu.
ākavē, tāymoḻiyoṉṟē ōriṉap pala matattāriyum oṉṟāyiṇikka vallatām. ātalāl, tamiḻar em matattārā yiṉum tamiḻit tāypōṟ pēṇik kāttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ eṉpatu taṉit tamiḻē. ayal nāṭṭu matam eṉṉuṅ karaṇiyat tāl, ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷi āḷutal kūṭātu. tamiḻmoḻi paṟṟiyē tamiḻar eṉṉum iṉam amintiruppatāl, tamiḻc colli vaḻaṅkātār tamiḻarākār. ayal nāṭṭu matam paṟṟiya ayaṉ moḻic coṟkaḷi yellām tamiḻilmoḻi peyart tāḷa vēṇṭum. nērt teṉcol ēṟkeṉavē yillāviṭiṉ, putuccol puṉintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
matam eṉpatu, kāṇappaṭāta maṟumi nōkkiya tātalāl, ellā mataṅkaḷiyum camamāka nōkkutal vēṇṭum. ovvoru matattārkkum tam matattip piṟarkkeṭut turikkavurimi yiruppiṉum, atu paṟṟip piṟa matattāri veṟuppatum pakippatum, mata nōkkattiṟkum māntaṉ taṉmikkum muṟṟum māṟāṉa tākum.
kaṭavu ḷiruppatu uṇmiyāyiṉ, avari nampuvāriyum nampātāriyum avarē maṟumiyiṟ kavaṉittuk koḷvār. immiyip poṟutta maṭṭil, ellārum uṭaṉ piṟappāka oṉṟi vāḻal vēṇṭum. atuvē uyartiṇi yeṉṉum makkaṭ ṭaṉmi.
mānta riyaṟki vēṟupāṭṭu viḷakkam
mānta rellārum orē tiṇi yāyiṉum, nākarika vāḻkkiyil uṇavu maṭṭumaṉṟi, uṭi, uṟiyuḷ, karuvi, taṭṭu muṭṭu, ūrti, maruntu, iṉpuṟutti, aṟivunūl mutaliya palvēṟu poruḷkaḷ tēviyāyiruppatāl, avaṟṟi yellām uruvākkuvataṟku, avar akak karaṇaṅkaḷum puṟak karaṇaṅkaḷum iyaṟkiyilēyē vevvēṟu vakippaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ovvoru poruḷum uḻippiṉālēyē uruvākkappaṭum. uṭaluḻip pum matiyuḻippum eṉa uḻippu iruvaki. iruvaki yuḻippum iṉṟi yamiyātaṉavē. uṭaluḻippāḷikaḷ uṭal valimiyarākavum matiyuḻip pāḷikaḷ mativalimiyarākavum piṟakkiṉṟaṉar. itu iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭu. itiyē ūḻ (viti) eṉpar.
ellārum mati valiyarāyiṉ uṭaluḻippu naṭipeṟātu. iti, ellārum pallak kēṟiṉāl evar tūkkuvatu? eṉṉum paḻamoḻi uṇarttum. āyiṉ, uṭaluḻippāḷiyarit tāḻvākak karutuvatum, avarkku vēṇṭiya vāḻkkit tēvi kaḷit tara maṟuppatum, koṭumiyum aḵṟiṇit taṉmiyum ākum. uṭaluḻippāḷiyariyum uṭaṉpiṟappup pōṟ karuti matikka vēṇṭu meṉpatiyē,
